Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/test/java/com/lumen/order/service/OrderServiceImplTest.java`（本 Task 先写建单 / 编辑 / 查询这批用例 —— 该测试类**最终 53 条**：Task 3 的建单/编辑/查询 + Task 4 的状态转换 + Task 7 与各轮复审的补充都追加在同一个类里）：

```java
package com.lumen.order.service;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.lumen.order.OrderConstants;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CargoRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemRequest;
import com.lumen.order.entity.Order;
import com.lumen.order.entity.OrderCargo;
import com.lumen.order.entity.OrderServiceItem;
import com.lumen.order.error.OrderErrorCode;
import com.lumen.order.exception.OrderException;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderCargoMapper;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderMapper;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderServiceItemMapper;
import com.lumen.order.service.impl.OrderServiceImpl;
import com.lumen.numbering.service.NumberGenerator;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoSettings;
import org.mockito.quality.Strictness;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThatThrownBy;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.lenient;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.times;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

/**
 * OrderServiceImpl 单元测试 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>纯 Mockito, 不带 {@code @SpringBootTest} —— 与 {@code lumen-dispatch} / {@code lumen-customer}
 * 的既有做法一致 (它们一共 26 个测试文件全部无 {@code @SpringBootTest})。
 */
@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
@MockitoSettings(strictness = Strictness.LENIENT)
@DisplayName("OrderServiceImpl 单元测试")
class OrderServiceImplTest {

    @Mock private OrderMapper orderMapper;
    @Mock private OrderCargoMapper cargoMapper;
    @Mock private OrderServiceItemMapper serviceItemMapper;
    @Mock private NumberGenerator numberGenerator;

    private OrderService service;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        service = new OrderServiceImpl(orderMapper, cargoMapper, serviceItemMapper, numberGenerator);
        lenient().when(numberGenerator.generate(OrderConstants.NUMBER_RULE_CODE))
                .thenReturn("ORD-20261110-000001");
    }

    private CreateOrderRequest sampleRequest() {
        return requestWith("CNY", false);
    }

    /**
     * 造一个建单请求 —— {@code settlementCurrency} / {@code isOversized} 可传 null
     * (模拟「客户端没带这两个字段」的部分更新请求)。
     */
    private CreateOrderRequest requestWith(String settlementCurrency, Boolean isOversized) {
        return new CreateOrderRequest(
                42L, "OCEAN_FCL", "EXPORT", "FOB", "D2D",
                "上海洋山港", "洛杉矶港",
                null, null, null,
                1, "40HQ",
                "MSKU1234567", "五金配件",
                isOversized, null,
                settlementCurrency, "测试委托单",
                List.of(new CargoRequest("钢材", "STEEL", "7208", "CARTON", 10d, "CTN",
                        1000d, 900d, 12.5d, 100d, 80d, 50d, true,
                        5000d, "CNY", "ACME", "A1", false, null, null, null)),
                List.of(new ServiceItemRequest("OCEAN_FREIGHT", "海运费", "FREIGHT", "PER_CONTAINER",
                        1d, 3000d, 3000d, "CNY", null, 2500d, false, null)));
    }

    /** 库里已存在的委托单 —— DRAFT 态 (可编辑), 币种/超限标记由参数决定。 */
    private Order editableOrder(String settlementCurrency, int isOversized) {
        Order o = new Order();
        o.setId(100L);
        o.setOrderNo("ORD-20261110-000001");
        o.setOrderStatus(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        o.setCustomerId(42L);
        o.setSettlementCurrency(settlementCurrency);
        o.setIsOversized(isOversized);
        return o;
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("create: 生成委托单号 + 落草稿态 + 明细聚合回写主表")
    void create_generatesNoAndAggregates() {
        Order saved = new Order();
        saved.setId(100L);
        saved.setOrderStatus(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.insert(any(Order.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> {
            Order o = inv.getArgument(0);
            o.setId(100L);
            return 1;
        });
        when(cargoMapper.insert(any(OrderCargo.class))).thenReturn(1);
        when(serviceItemMapper.insert(any(OrderServiceItem.class))).thenReturn(1);

        var view = service.create(sampleRequest());

        assertThat(view.orderNo()).isEqualTo("ORD-20261110-000001");
        assertThat(view.orderStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        assertThat(view.customerId()).isEqualTo(42L);

        // 汇总回写: pkg_qty=10, weight_kg=1000, volume_cbm=12.5
        ArgumentCaptor<Order> captor = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(Order.class);
        verify(orderMapper).insert(captor.capture());
        Order inserted = captor.getValue();
        assertThat(inserted.getTotalQty()).isEqualByComparingTo("10");
        assertThat(inserted.getTotalWeightKg()).isEqualByComparingTo("1000");
        assertThat(inserted.getTotalVolumeCbm()).isEqualByComparingTo("12.5");
        assertThat(inserted.getIsDangerous()).isZero();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("create: 货物明细为空 → OrderException(CARGO_EMPTY)")
    void create_emptyCargo_throws() {
        CreateOrderRequest req = new CreateOrderRequest(
                42L, "OCEAN_FCL", "EXPORT", "FOB", "D2D", null, null, null, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, false, null, "CNY", null,
                List.of(), List.of(new ServiceItemRequest("OCEAN_FREIGHT", "海运费", "FREIGHT",
                        "PER_CONTAINER", 1d, 3000d, 3000d, "CNY", null, 2500d, false, null)));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.create(req))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.CARGO_EMPTY);
        verify(orderMapper, never()).insert(any(Order.class));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("create: 服务项为空 → OrderException(SERVICE_ITEM_EMPTY)")
    void create_emptyServiceItems_throws() {
        CreateOrderRequest req = new CreateOrderRequest(
                42L, "OCEAN_FCL", "EXPORT", "FOB", "D2D", null, null, null, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, false, null, "CNY", null,
                List.of(new CargoRequest("钢材", null, null, null, 1d, null, 1d, null, 1d,
                        null, null, null, true, null, null, null, null, false, null, null, null)),
                List.of());

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.create(req))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.SERVICE_ITEM_EMPTY);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("get: 委托单不存在 → OrderException(ORDER_NOT_FOUND)")
    void get_missing_throws() {
        when(orderMapper.selectById(999L)).thenReturn(null);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.get(999L))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("get: 返回详情 + 明细 + 服务项 (allowedNextStates 本轮恒为空列表)")
    void get_returnsDetailWithChildren() {
        Order o = new Order();
        o.setId(100L);
        o.setOrderNo("ORD-20261110-000001");
        o.setOrderStatus(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        o.setCustomerId(42L);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);

        OrderCargo c = new OrderCargo();
        c.setId(1L); c.setOrderId(100L); c.setCargoNameCn("钢材"); c.setPkgQty(BigDecimal.TEN);
        when(cargoMapper.selectList(any(LambdaQueryWrapper.class))).thenReturn(List.of(c));

        OrderServiceItem s = new OrderServiceItem();
        s.setId(2L); s.setOrderId(100L); s.setFeeName("海运费"); s.setAmount(BigDecimal.valueOf(3000));
        when(serviceItemMapper.selectList(any(LambdaQueryWrapper.class))).thenReturn(List.of(s));

        OrderDetailView view = service.get(100L);

        assertThat(view.orderNo()).isEqualTo("ORD-20261110-000001");
        assertThat(view.cargo()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(view.serviceItems()).hasSize(1);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 编辑路径

    @Test
    @DisplayName("update: 请求不带 settlementCurrency / isOversized → 保留库里的原值")
    void update_omittingCurrencyAndOversizedKeepsDbValues() {
        Order o = editableOrder("USD", 1);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        when(cargoMapper.insert(any(OrderCargo.class))).thenReturn(1);
        when(serviceItemMapper.insert(any(OrderServiceItem.class))).thenReturn(1);

        service.update(100L, requestWith(null, null));

        // 两次 updateById: 头部更新 + 明细聚合回写
        ArgumentCaptor<Order> captor = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(Order.class);
        verify(orderMapper, times(2)).updateById(captor.capture());
        Order headerUpdate = captor.getAllValues().get(0);
        assertThat(headerUpdate.getSettlementCurrency()).isEqualTo("USD");
        assertThat(headerUpdate.getIsOversized()).isEqualTo(1);

        // 聚合回写那条 UPDATE 只带 4 个聚合列 —— 两列是 null, 不进 SET, 不会把原值冲掉
        Order aggUpdate = captor.getAllValues().get(1);
        assertThat(aggUpdate.getSettlementCurrency()).isNull();
        assertThat(aggUpdate.getIsOversized()).isNull();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("update: 请求带 isOversized=false → 落 0 (取消超限标记)")
    void update_withOversizedFalseClearsFlag() {
        Order o = editableOrder("USD", 1);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        when(cargoMapper.insert(any(OrderCargo.class))).thenReturn(1);
        when(serviceItemMapper.insert(any(OrderServiceItem.class))).thenReturn(1);

        service.update(100L, requestWith(null, false));

        ArgumentCaptor<Order> captor = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(Order.class);
        verify(orderMapper, times(2)).updateById(captor.capture());
        Order headerUpdate = captor.getAllValues().get(0);
        assertThat(headerUpdate.getIsOversized()).isZero();
        // 没带币种 → 币种不动
        assertThat(headerUpdate.getSettlementCurrency()).isEqualTo("USD");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("update: 请求带 settlementCurrency=USD → 落 USD")
    void update_withCurrencyOverwritesDbValue() {
        Order o = editableOrder("CNY", 0);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        when(cargoMapper.insert(any(OrderCargo.class))).thenReturn(1);
        when(serviceItemMapper.insert(any(OrderServiceItem.class))).thenReturn(1);

        service.update(100L, requestWith("USD", null));

        ArgumentCaptor<Order> captor = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(Order.class);
        verify(orderMapper, times(2)).updateById(captor.capture());
        Order headerUpdate = captor.getAllValues().get(0);
        assertThat(headerUpdate.getSettlementCurrency()).isEqualTo("USD");
        // 没带超限标记 → 标记不动
        assertThat(headerUpdate.getIsOversized()).isZero();
    }
}
```

> **注意**：上面这个测试文件里 `NumberGenerator` 的 import 已在代码块内 (`com.lumen.numbering.service.NumberGenerator`)。
> `allowedNextStates` 的断言**不在本 Task** —— Task 3 的 service 里还没注入 `StateMachineEngine`，
> `toDetail` 暂时返回空列表。该断言在 **Task 4** 用独立的
> `get_allowedNextStatesFromEngine` 用例锁。

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/dto/CargoRequest.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.dto;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.PositiveOrZero;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

/**
 * 货物明细请求行 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>管理端 {@code CreateOrderRequest} 与门户端 {@code PortalFreightOrderRequest}
 * (Task 7 新建, {@code com.lumen.customer.dto}) 共用这一个 record。
 *
 * <p>字段级约束 (长度/正数) 由 jakarta bean validation 兜住 → HTTP 400 + PARAM_INVALID (4001);
 * 本 record 不做集合级校验 (「明细不能为空」是 service 的事, 抛 400_101)。
 *
 * <p><b>约束宽度逐列对齐 {@code t_order_cargo} 的真实列宽</b> (V42 迁移) —— 宽度比列宽小的约束
 * 会把合法数据挡在门外, 宽度比列宽大的约束则形同虚设 (MySQL 直接截断或报 1406)。
 * 对应关系: {@code cargo_name_cn/cargo_name_en/brand_name/model → VARCHAR(128)},
 * {@code hs_code/un_no/danger_class → VARCHAR(16)}, {@code pkg_type → VARCHAR(32)},
 * {@code unit → VARCHAR(16)}, {@code currency_code → CHAR(3)}, {@code remark → VARCHAR(500)};
 * {@code pkg_qty} ≥ 0 (传 0 或不传 = 件数未知, 散货拼箱 LCL 常见), 与 DDL 的
 * {@code NOT NULL DEFAULT 0} 一致; 其余数量/尺寸/金额列 {@code DECIMAL} 不允许负数。
 *
 * <p><b>数量列的宽度由 service 兜</b> —— DTO 只管「非负」, 不管「装不装得下」:
 * {@code @PositiveOrZero} 放行 {@code 1e999} (Jackson 解析成 {@code Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY})
 * 与 {@code 1e15} (超出 {@code DECIMAL(18,3)}), 这两种都得在 service 里转成
 * {@code NUMERIC_OUT_OF_RANGE} (400_105), 否则会变成 500。
 */
public record CargoRequest(
        @Size(max = 128) String cargoNameCn,
        @Size(max = 128) String cargoNameEn,
        @Size(max = 16) String hsCode,
        @Size(max = 32) String pkgType,
        @PositiveOrZero Double pkgQty,
        @Size(max = 16) String unit,
        @PositiveOrZero Double weightKg,
        @PositiveOrZero Double netWeightKg,
        @PositiveOrZero Double volumeCbm,
        @PositiveOrZero Double lengthCm,
        @PositiveOrZero Double widthCm,
        @PositiveOrZero Double heightCm,
        Boolean isStackable,
        @PositiveOrZero Double cargoValue,
        @Size(max = 3) String currencyCode,
        @Size(max = 128) String brandName,
        @Size(max = 128) String model,
        Boolean isDangerous,
        @Size(max = 16) String unNo,
        @Size(max = 16) String dangerClass,
        @Size(max = 500) String remark
) {}
```

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/dto/ServiceItemRequest.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.dto;

import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.PositiveOrZero;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

/**
 * 服务项 (费用预算) 请求行 (Iteration 4.6). 管理端与门户端共用。
 *
 * <p><b>约束宽度逐列对齐 {@code t_order_service_item} 的真实列宽</b> (V42 迁移):
 * {@code fee_type → VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL}、{@code fee_name → VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL}、
 * {@code fee_category → VARCHAR(32)}、{@code calc_basis → VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL}、
 * {@code currency_code → CHAR(3)}、{@code remark → VARCHAR(500)};
 * 数量/单价/金额三列是 {@code DECIMAL}, 不允许负数。
 *
 * <p>三列 {@code NOT NULL} 的字段 ({@code feeType} / {@code feeName} / {@code calcBasis}) 标
 * {@code @NotBlank} —— 少传会被 jakarta bean validation 挡在 controller 口 → HTTP 400 + PARAM_INVALID (4001),
 * 而不是落一条 {@code NULL} 明细进库。
 *
 * <p>{@code costAmount} 是**可空列** ({@code DECIMAL(18,2) DEFAULT NULL}), 不标 {@code @NotNull};
 * 但仍标 {@code @PositiveOrZero} —— 成本额不允许负数。
 *
 * <p><b>受控词表不在 DTO 上校验</b> —— {@code feeType} / {@code calcBasis} / {@code feeCategory}
 * 的合法取值由 {@code OrderConstants} 的各组 List 定义, 由 service 层统一校验
 * (抛 {@code ENUM_VALUE_INVALID} 400_106); DTO 上只用 {@code @Size} 卡长度。
 */
public record ServiceItemRequest(
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 32) String feeType,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 128) String feeName,
        @Size(max = 32) String feeCategory,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 16) String calcBasis,
        @PositiveOrZero Double qty,
        @PositiveOrZero Double unitPrice,
        @PositiveOrZero Double amount,
        @Size(max = 3) String currencyCode,
        Long supplierId,
        @PositiveOrZero Double costAmount,
        Boolean isMandatory,
        @Size(max = 500) String remark
) {}
```

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/dto/CreateOrderRequest.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.dto;

import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotNull;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 管理端建单请求 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>管理端与门户端都用这个 record 作传输对象</b> —— 管理端直接用它当
 * {@code @RequestBody}; 门户端先用 {@code com.lumen.customer.dto.PortalFreightOrderRequest}
 * 收参, 再由 {@code PortalFreightOrderController.toCreateRequest} 转成本 record
 * (门户那份**没有 customerId**: 客户由服务端从登录态注入, body 里压根不该有这个字段)。
 * 共用一个 DTO 会逼出「@NotNull 但门户不传」或「不标但管理端能漏传」的二选一, 两个都是坑。
 *
 * <p>{@code cargo} / {@code serviceItems} <b>不标 {@code @NotEmpty}</b> —— 空集合由 service 抛
 * {@code OrderErrorCode.CARGO_EMPTY} / {@code SERVICE_ITEM_EMPTY}, 避免同一个失败有两条码。
 * 但两个集合组件<b>标了 {@code @Valid}</b> —— 不标的话嵌套 record 上的 jakarta 约束
 * ({@code CargoRequest} / {@code ServiceItemRequest} 的长度与正数) 不会被级联校验,
 * 超长字符串会一路走到 MySQL 报 1406 → 500 而不是 400。
 *
 * <p><b>长度约束逐列对齐 {@code t_order} 的真实列宽</b> (V42 迁移):
 * {@code business_type → VARCHAR(32)}、{@code trade_direction / incoterms / service_type → VARCHAR(16)}、
 * {@code origin_address / dest_address → VARCHAR(255)}、{@code container_type_code → VARCHAR(16)}、
 * {@code marks_and_nos / cargo_description → VARCHAR(500)}、{@code temperature_range → VARCHAR(32)}、
 * {@code remark → VARCHAR(1000)}、{@code settlement_currency → CHAR(3)}。
 * 共 12 个定长/限长字段, 全部有 {@code @Size} —— 少一个就等于超长时撞 MySQL 1406 → 500 而不是 400。
 */
public record CreateOrderRequest(
        @NotNull Long customerId,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 32) String businessType,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 16) String tradeDirection,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 16) String incoterms,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 16) String serviceType,
        @Size(max = 255) String originAddress,
        @Size(max = 255) String destAddress,
        LocalDate cargoReadyDate,
        LocalDate etd,
        LocalDate eta,
        Integer containerQty,
        @Size(max = 16) String containerTypeCode,
        @Size(max = 500) String marksAndNos,
        @Size(max = 500) String cargoDescription,
        Boolean isOversized,
        @Size(max = 32) String temperatureRange,
        @Size(max = 3) String settlementCurrency,
        @Size(max = 1000) String remark,
        @Valid List<CargoRequest> cargo,
        @Valid List<ServiceItemRequest> serviceItems
) {}
```

```java
     * 门户端给客户看的服务项**不应该**带我方成本 —— 见 PortalFreightOrderController#detail。

                qty, unitPrice, amount, currencyCode, null, null, isMandatory, source, remark);
```

Create `OrderDetailView.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.dto;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 委托单详情 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>{@code allowedNextStates} = {@code StateMachineEngine.allowedNext("ORDER", 当前态)},
 * 前端据此渲染可执行动作按钮 —— 这是本轮唯一一处「表驱动状态机反向驱动 UI」的地方。
 * <br><b>门户端例外</b>: 门户视图会把这一列收窄成「客户自己点得到的」那一组
 * (见 {@code PortalFreightOrderController.portalNextStates}), 管理端才是整张状态机的出边。
 *
 * <p><b>由 {@code OrderServiceImpl.toDetail} 计算</b> —— 该实现已注入
 * {@code StateMachineEngine}, 走 {@code stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(
 * OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE, orderStatus)}; 引擎返回 null 时兜底成
 * {@code List.of()} (见 {@code OrderServiceImpl.allowedNextStates})。
 */
public record OrderDetailView(
        Long id,
        String orderNo,
        Long customerId,
        String businessType,
        String tradeDirection,
        String incoterms,
        String serviceType,
        String originAddress,
        String destAddress,
        BigDecimal totalQty,
        BigDecimal totalWeightKg,
        BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm,
        String orderStatus,
        String settlementCurrency,
        LocalDate etd,
        LocalDate eta,
        LocalDateTime createdAt,
        LocalDate cargoReadyDate,
        Integer containerQty,
        String containerTypeCode,
        String marksAndNos,
        String cargoDescription,
        Integer isDangerous,
        Integer isOversized,
        String temperatureRange,
        String remark,
        String cancelReason,
        String closeReason,
        String voidReason,
        LocalDateTime submittedAt,
        LocalDateTime confirmedAt,
        LocalDateTime deliveredAt,
        LocalDateTime settledAt,
        LocalDateTime closedAt,
        Long createdBy,
        Long updatedBy,
        List<CargoView> cargo,
        List<ServiceItemView> serviceItems,
        List<String> allowedNextStates
) {

    /**
     * 返回一份换掉服务项的副本 —— 其余 38 个组件逐个原样保留。
     * 门户端用它把内部成本从服务项里摘掉 (见 ServiceItemView#withoutInternalCost)。
     */
    public OrderDetailView withServiceItems(List<ServiceItemView> newServiceItems) {
        return new OrderDetailView(id, orderNo, customerId, businessType, tradeDirection,
                incoterms, serviceType, originAddress, destAddress, totalQty, totalWeightKg,
                totalVolumeCbm, orderStatus, settlementCurrency, etd, eta, createdAt,
                cargoReadyDate, containerQty, containerTypeCode, marksAndNos, cargoDescription,
                isDangerous, isOversized, temperatureRange, remark, cancelReason, closeReason,
                voidReason, submittedAt, confirmedAt, deliveredAt, settledAt, closedAt,
                createdBy, updatedBy, cargo, newServiceItems, allowedNextStates);
    }

    /**
     * 返回一份换掉 allowedNextStates 的副本 —— 其余 38 个组件逐个原样保留。
     *
     * <p><b>门户端用它裁掉客户看不到的内部状态</b> —— 管理端的 allowedNextStates
     * 是整张状态机的出边 (含 VOID / IN_OPERATION / SETTLED / CLOSED 这些内部动作),
     * 直接返给客户等于把我方内部流转暴露出去, 而且已确认的单会因为
     * 「CONFIRMED → CANCELLED 是主管动作」而多出一个「取消」按钮, 客户一点必撞 409。
     */
    public OrderDetailView withAllowedNextStates(List<String> newAllowedNextStates) {
        return new OrderDetailView(id, orderNo, customerId, businessType, tradeDirection,
                incoterms, serviceType, originAddress, destAddress, totalQty, totalWeightKg,
                totalVolumeCbm, orderStatus, settlementCurrency, etd, eta, createdAt,
                cargoReadyDate, containerQty, containerTypeCode, marksAndNos, cargoDescription,
                isDangerous, isOversized, temperatureRange, remark, cancelReason, closeReason,
                voidReason, submittedAt, confirmedAt, deliveredAt, settledAt, closedAt,
                createdBy, updatedBy, cargo, serviceItems, newAllowedNextStates);
    }
}
```

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/service/OrderService.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.service;

import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 货运委托单业务接口 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>门户端也用这个接口</b> —— {@code com.lumen.customer.controller.PortalFreightOrderController}
 * (Task 7 新建) 调下面带 {@code customerId} 的那组方法拿客户归属边界, 门户控制器不做任何状态机判定。
 */
public interface OrderService {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 管理端

    /** 建草稿。委托单号由 {@code NumberGenerator.generate("ORDER")} 生成。 */
    OrderView create(CreateOrderRequest request);

    /** 编辑 (仅 DRAFT / PENDING_CONFIRM 可编辑, 否则抛 ORDER_NOT_EDITABLE)。 */
    OrderDetailView update(Long id, CreateOrderRequest request);

    /** 分页列表。 */
    PageResult<OrderView> page(long current, long size, String status, Long customerId,
                                String orderNo, String businessType);

    /** 详情。 */
    OrderDetailView get(Long id);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 门户端 (客户归属边界)

    /** 列当前客户的委托单。 */
    PageResult<OrderView> pageByCustomer(Long customerId, long current, long size, String status);

    /** 取详情。**归属不符 → ORDER_NOT_FOUND (404)**, 不是 403 —— 不能泄露「存在但不属于你」。 */
    OrderDetailView getOwned(Long id, Long customerId);

    /** 客户建单 (强制 DRAFT 态)。 */
    OrderView createForCustomer(Long customerId, CreateOrderRequest request);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 状态转换
    //
    // 11 个状态转换方法 (submit / confirm / returnToDraft / cancel / voidOrder /
    // startOperation / deliver / settle / close + 门户端 confirmOwned / cancelOwned)
    // **在 Task 4 才声明并实现** —— 本 Task 的 OrderServiceImpl 只实现上面这 7 个方法,
    // 若此时就声明转换方法, 接口有抽象方法而实现类没有 → 编译不过。
}
```

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/service/impl/OrderServiceImpl.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.service.impl;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.numbering.service.NumberGenerator;
import com.lumen.order.OrderConstants;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CargoRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CargoView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemView;
import com.lumen.order.entity.Order;
import com.lumen.order.entity.OrderCargo;
import com.lumen.order.entity.OrderServiceItem;
import com.lumen.order.error.OrderErrorCode;
import com.lumen.order.exception.OrderException;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderCargoMapper;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderMapper;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderServiceItemMapper;
import com.lumen.order.service.OrderService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 货运委托单业务实现 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>租户</b>: {@code tenant_id} 由 {@code MyMetaObjectHandler} 在插入时按
 * {@code TenantContext} 自动填; 查询侧由 {@code MybatisPlusConfig} 的
 * {@code TenantLineInnerInterceptor} 自动加 {@code AND tenant_id = ?}。本类**不手动**设 tenantId。
 *
 * <p><b>软删</b>: 三个实体的 {@code deleted} 都带 {@code @TableLogic}, MP 自动补 {@code deleted = 0}。
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class OrderServiceImpl implements OrderService {

    private final OrderMapper orderMapper;
    private final OrderCargoMapper cargoMapper;
    private final OrderServiceItemMapper serviceItemMapper;
    private final NumberGenerator numberGenerator;

    /** 分页 size 上限 —— 与 CsExceptionController 一致。 */
    private static final long MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 200L;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 管理端

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderView create(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return createInternal(request, null, OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView update(Long id, CreateOrderRequest request) {
        Order order = requireOrder(id);
        if (!OrderConstants.EDITABLE_STATES.contains(order.getOrderStatus())) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_EDITABLE,
                    "当前状态 " + order.getOrderStatus() + " 不可编辑");
        }
        List<CargoRequest> cargo = requireCargo(request);
        List<ServiceItemRequest> items = requireServiceItems(request);

        applyHeader(order, request, null);
        applyUpdateOverrides(order, request);
        orderMapper.updateById(order);

        replaceChildren(order, cargo, items);
        return get(id);
    }

    @Override
    public PageResult<OrderView> page(long current, long size, String status, Long customerId,
                                       String orderNo, String businessType) {
        long effectiveSize = Math.min(size <= 0 ? 20 : size, MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        long pageNum = current <= 0 ? 1 : current;

        LambdaQueryWrapper<Order> q = new LambdaQueryWrapper<Order>()
                .orderByDesc(Order::getCreatedAt);
        if (status != null && !status.isBlank()) {
            q.eq(Order::getOrderStatus, status);
        }
        if (customerId != null) {
            q.eq(Order::getCustomerId, customerId);
        }
        if (businessType != null && !businessType.isBlank()) {
            q.eq(Order::getBusinessType, businessType);
        }
        if (orderNo != null && !orderNo.isBlank()) {
            q.like(Order::getOrderNo, orderNo);
        }

        Page<Order> result = orderMapper.selectPage(new Page<>(pageNum, effectiveSize), q);
        List<OrderView> views = result.getRecords().stream().map(this::toView).toList();
        return PageResult.of(views, result.getTotal(), pageNum, effectiveSize);
    }

    @Override
    public OrderDetailView get(Long id) {
        Order order = requireOrder(id);
        return toDetail(order);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 门户端

    @Override
    public PageResult<OrderView> pageByCustomer(Long customerId, long current, long size, String status) {
        return page(current, size, status, customerId, null, null);
    }

    @Override
    public OrderDetailView getOwned(Long id, Long customerId) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectById(id);
        // 归属不符一律按「不存在」处理 —— 不能泄露「存在但不属于你」
        if (order == null || !customerId.equals(order.getCustomerId())) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return toDetail(order);
    }

    /**
     * 客户自助建单。
     *
     * <p><b>customerId 一律以本方法的入参为准</b> —— 门户端的 DTO 里根本没有 customerId 字段,
     * 客户端无法指定归属; 这里再强制覆盖一次, 保证「客户只能给自己建单」。
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderView createForCustomer(Long customerId, CreateOrderRequest request) {
        if (customerId == null) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND, "缺少客户身份");
        }
        return createInternal(request, customerId, OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 内部

    /**
     * @param customerIdOverride 非空时**覆盖** {@code request.customerId()} (门户端路径);
     *                           为空时用 {@code request.customerId()} (管理端路径)。
     */
    private OrderView createInternal(CreateOrderRequest request, Long customerIdOverride, String status) {
        List<CargoRequest> cargo = requireCargo(request);
        List<ServiceItemRequest> items = requireServiceItems(request);

        Order order = new Order();
        applyHeader(order, request, customerIdOverride);
        applyCreateDefaults(order, request);
        order.setOrderNo(numberGenerator.generate(OrderConstants.NUMBER_RULE_CODE));
        order.setOrderStatus(status);
        orderMapper.insert(order);

        replaceChildren(order, cargo, items);
        return toView(order);
    }

    private List<CargoRequest> requireCargo(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        if (request.cargo() == null || request.cargo().isEmpty()) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.CARGO_EMPTY);
        }
        return request.cargo();
    }

    private List<ServiceItemRequest> requireServiceItems(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        if (request.serviceItems() == null || request.serviceItems().isEmpty()) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.SERVICE_ITEM_EMPTY);
        }
        return request.serviceItems();
    }

    /**
     * 头部字段的部分覆盖 —— 建单与编辑走同一套 setter 口径。
     *
     * <p><b>编辑是部分覆盖, 不是全量替换</b> —— MP 默认 {@code updateStrategy = NOT_NULL},
     * 实体上为 null 的字段不进 SET 子句, 客户端漏传的字段**保留库里的旧值** (而不是被清空)。
     *
     * <p><b>{@code settlementCurrency} / {@code isOversized} 不在这里</b> —— 这两列的「缺省」语义
     * 两条路径不同 (建单落 DDL 默认值, 编辑保留库中原值), 由 {@link #applyCreateDefaults} /
     * {@link #applyUpdateOverrides} 各自决定。
     */
    private void applyHeader(Order order, CreateOrderRequest request, Long customerIdOverride) {
        order.setCustomerId(customerIdOverride != null ? customerIdOverride : request.customerId());
        order.setBusinessType(request.businessType());
        order.setTradeDirection(request.tradeDirection());
        order.setIncoterms(request.incoterms());
        order.setServiceType(request.serviceType());
        order.setOriginAddress(request.originAddress());
        order.setDestAddress(request.destAddress());
        order.setCargoReadyDate(request.cargoReadyDate());
        order.setEtd(request.etd());
        order.setEta(request.eta());
        order.setContainerQty(request.containerQty());
        order.setContainerTypeCode(request.containerTypeCode());
        order.setMarksAndNos(request.marksAndNos());
        order.setCargoDescription(request.cargoDescription());
        order.setTemperatureRange(request.temperatureRange());
        order.setRemark(request.remark());
    }

    /**
     * 建单路径的缺省值 —— 对齐 DDL 的 {@code settlement_currency CHAR(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CNY'}
     * 与 {@code is_oversized TINYINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0}。
     *
     * <p>请求里的 {@code settlementCurrency} 为 null/blank → 落 {@code CNY};
     * {@code isOversized} 为 null → 落 {@code 0}。
     */
    private static void applyCreateDefaults(Order order, CreateOrderRequest request) {
        order.setSettlementCurrency(
                request.settlementCurrency() == null || request.settlementCurrency().isBlank()
                        ? "CNY" : request.settlementCurrency());
        order.setIsOversized(Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.isOversized()) ? 1 : 0);
    }

    /**
     * 编辑路径的覆盖 —— **null 一律保留库里原值**, 与 MP 的 NOT_NULL 更新策略同口径。
     *
     * <p>{@code settlementCurrency} 为 null **或 blank** → 不设 (库里是 USD 就还是 USD);
     * {@code isOversized} 为 null → 不设; 非 null 则正常写入 —— 所以「取消超限标记」传
     * {@code false} → 落 {@code 0}, 清除动作仍然可用。
     *
     * <p><b>为什么不能一刀切成默认值</b> —— 一个不带这两字段的部分更新请求会把库里的
     * USD / {@code is_oversized=1} 冲掉, 用户没碰过的字段被静默改写。
     */
    private static void applyUpdateOverrides(Order order, CreateOrderRequest request) {
        if (request.settlementCurrency() != null && !request.settlementCurrency().isBlank()) {
            order.setSettlementCurrency(request.settlementCurrency());
        }
        if (request.isOversized() != null) {
            order.setIsOversized(request.isOversized() ? 1 : 0);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 用明细整体替换子表, 然后把聚合值回写主表。
     *
     * <p>先软删旧的再插新的 —— 简单且不会留下孤儿行; 委托单明细量小 (几十行),
     * 整表替换的写放大可以接受。
     *
     * <p>聚合值同时落在两处: (1) 一条**只含 4 个聚合列 + {@code version} + MP 自动维护的
     * {@code updated_at}** 的 UPDATE 语句 (SET 子句实际 6 列), 不碰主表其它业务列 ——
     * {@code version} 进 SET 是因为它非 null 且默认 {@code updateStrategy = NOT_NULL} 放行;
     * {@code updated_at} 必然进 SET:
     * {@code BaseEntity.updatedAt} 标的是 {@code fill = INSERT_UPDATE}, 而 MP 的
     * {@code TableFieldInfo.getSqlSet} 对 fill 字段**不做 null 判断**;
     * (2) 传进来的 {@code order} 实例 —— {@code create()} 直接拿它拼 {@code OrderView} 返回,
     * 聚合值必须在这份实例上可见, 否则建单接口返回的汇总恒为空。
     *
     * <p>WHERE 子句由 MP 拼: {@code id = ?} + 逻辑删除条件 {@code deleted = 0} +
     * 租户条件 {@code tenant_id = ?} (多租户拦截器只加 WHERE, 不加 SET)。
     */
    private void replaceChildren(Order order, List<CargoRequest> cargo, List<ServiceItemRequest> items) {
        Long orderId = order.getId();
        cargoMapper.delete(new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderCargo>().eq(OrderCargo::getOrderId, orderId));
        serviceItemMapper.delete(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>().eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, orderId));

        BigDecimal totalQty = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal totalWeight = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal totalVolume = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        boolean anyDangerous = false;

        for (CargoRequest r : cargo) {
            OrderCargo row = new OrderCargo();
            row.setOrderId(orderId);
            row.setCargoNameCn(r.cargoNameCn());
            row.setCargoNameEn(r.cargoNameEn());
            row.setHsCode(r.hsCode());
            row.setPkgType(r.pkgType());
            row.setPkgQty(nz(r.pkgQty(), 3));
            row.setUnit(r.unit());
            row.setWeightKg(nz(r.weightKg(), 3));
            row.setNetWeightKg(opt(r.netWeightKg(), 3));
            row.setVolumeCbm(nz(r.volumeCbm(), 3));
            row.setLengthCm(opt(r.lengthCm(), 3));
            row.setWidthCm(opt(r.widthCm(), 3));
            row.setHeightCm(opt(r.heightCm(), 3));
            row.setIsStackable(r.isStackable() == null ? 1 : (r.isStackable() ? 1 : 0));
            row.setCargoValue(opt(r.cargoValue(), 2));
            row.setCurrencyCode(r.currencyCode());
            row.setBrandName(r.brandName());
            row.setModel(r.model());
            row.setIsDangerous(Boolean.TRUE.equals(r.isDangerous()) ? 1 : 0);
            row.setUnNo(r.unNo());
            row.setDangerClass(r.dangerClass());
            row.setRemark(r.remark());
            cargoMapper.insert(row);

            totalQty = totalQty.add(nz(r.pkgQty(), 3));
            totalWeight = totalWeight.add(nz(r.weightKg(), 3));
            totalVolume = totalVolume.add(nz(r.volumeCbm(), 3));
            anyDangerous = anyDangerous || Boolean.TRUE.equals(r.isDangerous());
        }

        for (ServiceItemRequest r : items) {
            OrderServiceItem row = new OrderServiceItem();
            row.setOrderId(orderId);
            row.setFeeType(r.feeType());
            row.setFeeName(r.feeName());
            row.setFeeCategory(r.feeCategory());
            row.setCalcBasis(r.calcBasis());
            row.setQty(nz(r.qty(), 3));
            row.setUnitPrice(nz(r.unitPrice(), 4));
            row.setAmount(nz(r.amount(), 2));
            row.setCurrencyCode(r.currencyCode() == null || r.currencyCode().isBlank()
                    ? "CNY" : r.currencyCode());
            row.setSupplierId(r.supplierId());
            row.setCostAmount(opt(r.costAmount(), 2));
            row.setIsMandatory(Boolean.TRUE.equals(r.isMandatory()) ? 1 : 0);
            row.setSource(OrderConstants.SRC_MANUAL);
            row.setRemark(r.remark());
            serviceItemMapper.insert(row);
        }

        Order agg = new Order();
        agg.setId(orderId);
        agg.setTotalQty(totalQty);
        agg.setTotalWeightKg(totalWeight);
        agg.setTotalVolumeCbm(totalVolume);
        agg.setIsDangerous(anyDangerous ? 1 : 0);
        orderMapper.updateById(agg);

        order.setTotalQty(totalQty);
        order.setTotalWeightKg(totalWeight);
        order.setTotalVolumeCbm(totalVolume);
        order.setIsDangerous(anyDangerous ? 1 : 0);
    }

    private Order requireOrder(Long id) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectById(id);
        if (order == null) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return order;
    }

    private OrderView toView(Order o) {
        return new OrderView(
                o.getId(), o.getOrderNo(), o.getCustomerId(), o.getBusinessType(),
                o.getTradeDirection(), o.getIncoterms(), o.getServiceType(),
                o.getOriginAddress(), o.getDestAddress(),
                o.getTotalQty(), o.getTotalWeightKg(), o.getTotalVolumeCbm(),
                o.getOrderStatus(), o.getSettlementCurrency(),
                o.getEtd(), o.getEta(), o.getCreatedAt());
    }

    private OrderDetailView toDetail(Order o) {
        List<OrderCargo> cargoRows = cargoMapper.selectList(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderCargo>().eq(OrderCargo::getOrderId, o.getId()));
        List<OrderServiceItem> itemRows = serviceItemMapper.selectList(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>().eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, o.getId()));

        List<CargoView> cargoViews = new ArrayList<>();
        for (OrderCargo c : cargoRows) {
            cargoViews.add(new CargoView(c.getId(), c.getCargoNameCn(), c.getCargoNameEn(),
                    c.getHsCode(), c.getPkgType(), c.getPkgQty(), c.getUnit(), c.getWeightKg(),
                    c.getNetWeightKg(), c.getVolumeCbm(), c.getLengthCm(), c.getWidthCm(),
                    c.getHeightCm(), c.getIsStackable(), c.getCargoValue(), c.getCurrencyCode(),
                    c.getBrandName(), c.getModel(), c.getIsDangerous(), c.getUnNo(),
                    c.getDangerClass(), c.getRemark()));
        }
        List<ServiceItemView> itemViews = new ArrayList<>();
        for (OrderServiceItem s : itemRows) {
            itemViews.add(new ServiceItemView(s.getId(), s.getFeeType(), s.getFeeName(),
                    s.getFeeCategory(), s.getCalcBasis(), s.getQty(), s.getUnitPrice(),
                    s.getAmount(), s.getCurrencyCode(), s.getSupplierId(), s.getCostAmount(),
                    s.getIsMandatory(), s.getSource(), s.getRemark()));
        }

        return new OrderDetailView(
                o.getId(), o.getOrderNo(), o.getCustomerId(), o.getBusinessType(),
                o.getTradeDirection(), o.getIncoterms(), o.getServiceType(),
                o.getOriginAddress(), o.getDestAddress(),
                o.getTotalQty(), o.getTotalWeightKg(), o.getTotalVolumeCbm(),
                o.getOrderStatus(), o.getSettlementCurrency(),
                o.getEtd(), o.getEta(), o.getCreatedAt(),
                o.getCargoReadyDate(), o.getContainerQty(), o.getContainerTypeCode(),
                o.getMarksAndNos(), o.getCargoDescription(), o.getIsDangerous(),
                o.getIsOversized(), o.getTemperatureRange(), o.getRemark(),
                o.getCancelReason(), o.getCloseReason(), o.getVoidReason(),
                o.getSubmittedAt(), o.getConfirmedAt(), o.getDeliveredAt(),
                o.getSettledAt(), o.getClosedAt(), o.getCreatedBy(), o.getUpdatedBy(),
                cargoViews, itemViews, List.of());
    }

    /**
     * NOT NULL 的数量/金额列 —— null 落 0, 非 null 截到列的 scale。
     *
     * <p><b>必须截位</b> —— MySQL 对超出 DECIMAL scale 的小数是**静默舍入** (不报错),
     * 不显式 setScale 的话 12.12345 会悄悄变成 12.123。
     */
    private static BigDecimal nz(Double v, int scale) {
        return v == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO.setScale(scale)
                : BigDecimal.valueOf(v).setScale(scale, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }

    /**
     * 可空的金额/尺寸列 —— null 保持 null (不把「没填」变成 0), 非 null 截到列的 scale。
     */
    private static BigDecimal opt(Double v, int scale) {
        return v == null ? null : BigDecimal.valueOf(v).setScale(scale, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
    }
}
```

> **`createdBy` / `updatedBy` 本轮留空**（列可空）。
> 本仓 `TenantContext` 只有一个 `ThreadLocal<Long>`, 值是 **tenantId** 而不是 userId ——
> 拿它填「谁建的」是**错的**。等真要记操作人时, 再从
> `SecurityContextHolder` 里取 `RbacUserDetails.getUserId()`。所以上面代码里**没有** `currentUserId()` 方法,
> 也没有任何 `setCreatedBy` / `setUpdatedBy` 调用 —— 实现时不要"顺手补上"。

## Task 4: 状态机 9 转换 + 守卫 + 事件发布

**Files:**
- Create: `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/event/OrderStateChangedEvent.java`
- Create: `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/event/OrderEventTypeRegistrar.java`
- Modify: `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/service/impl/OrderServiceImpl.java`
- Modify: `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/service/OrderService.java`
- Modify: `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/test/java/com/lumen/order/service/OrderServiceImplTest.java`
- Create: `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/test/java/com/lumen/order/event/OrderEventTypeRegistrarTest.java`

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试 —— 合法转换 / 非法转换 409 / 守卫**

在 `OrderServiceImplTest.java` 的 import 区补：

```java
import com.lumen.common.tenant.TenantContext;
import com.lumen.extension.outbox.EventBus;
import com.lumen.extension.state.StateMachineEngine;
import com.lumen.order.event.OrderStateChangedEvent;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
```

在字段区补 mock：

```java
    @Mock private StateMachineEngine stateMachineEngine;
    @Mock private EventBus eventBus;
```

在 `setUp()` 里改成（**三处都要补，缺一个用例就红**）：

```java
    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        service = new OrderServiceImpl(orderMapper, cargoMapper, serviceItemMapper,
                numberGenerator, stateMachineEngine, eventBus);
        lenient().when(numberGenerator.generate(OrderConstants.NUMBER_RULE_CODE))
                .thenReturn("ORD-20261110-000001");
        // Mockito 对 int 返回值的默认是 0 —— 而 0 行在业务上意味着「乐观锁冲突」
        // (applyTransition 里 rows == 0 → ORDER_CONFLICT), 所以默认必须打 1,
        // 否则每个走到 updateById 的用例都会撞 ORDER_CONFLICT。
        lenient().when(orderMapper.updateById(any(Order.class))).thenReturn(1);
        // applyTransition 里要 TenantContext.require() —— 单测没有 Spring 的租户过滤器,
        // 不 set 就直接 IllegalStateException("tenant context not set")。
        TenantContext.set(1L);
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        TenantContext.clear();
    }
```

> **租户上下文必须显式 set / clear** —— `TenantContext.require()`
> (`lumen-common/.../tenant/TenantContext.java`) 在没有值时抛
> `IllegalStateException("tenant context not set")`, 单测里没人给你 set。
> `@AfterEach` 里 `clear()` 也不能省 —— 同一个 JVM 里跑多个测试类,
> 漏清会把租户带到别的用例里。

> **注意**：事件发布走的是 **`EventBus`**（`com.lumen.extension.outbox.EventBus`），
> **不是** `ApplicationEventPublisher`。`DefaultEventBus.publish` 会在同一事务里写
> `t_event_outbox` 行 + 再转发给 `ApplicationEventPublisher` —— 直接注入
> `ApplicationEventPublisher` 会**绕过 outbox**，事件不会落库，finance 侧也收不到。
> 单元测试 mock 的必须是 `EventBus`。


在文件末尾追加用例：

```java
    private Order existingOrder(String status) {
        Order o = new Order();
        o.setId(100L);
        o.setOrderNo("ORD-20261110-000001");
        o.setOrderStatus(status);
        o.setCustomerId(42L);
        o.setSettlementCurrency("CNY");
        o.setTotalQty(BigDecimal.TEN);
        o.setTotalWeightKg(BigDecimal.valueOf(1000));
        o.setTotalVolumeCbm(BigDecimal.valueOf(12.5));
        // requireBasicsComplete 要查这 4 个必填项 —— 缺一个就抛「必填项缺失, 不能提交」,
        // 所以 existingOrder 里必须补齐 (取值全在 OrderConstants 的受控词表里)。
        o.setBusinessType("OCEAN_FCL");
        o.setTradeDirection("EXPORT");
        o.setIncoterms("FOB");
        o.setServiceType("D2D");
        return o;
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("submit: DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM, 发一条 OrderStateChangedEvent")
    void submit_draftToPendingConfirm_publishesEvent() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT)).thenReturn(List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM));
        // requireBasicsComplete 会数明细条数 —— MP 3.5.7 的 selectCount 返回 Long,
        // Mockito 不打桩就返回 null → 拆箱直接 NPE (不是 CARGO_EMPTY)。
        when(cargoMapper.selectCount(any())).thenReturn(1L);
        when(serviceItemMapper.selectCount(any())).thenReturn(1L);

        service.submit(100L);

        ArgumentCaptor<OrderStateChangedEvent> captor =
                ArgumentCaptor.forClass(OrderStateChangedEvent.class);
        verify(eventBus).publish(captor.capture());
        OrderStateChangedEvent ev = captor.getValue();
        assertThat(ev.getFromState()).isEqualTo(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        assertThat(ev.getToState()).isEqualTo(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM);
        assertThat(ev.getAction()).isEqualTo("submit");
        assertThat(ev.getOrderId()).isEqualTo(100L);
        assertThat(ev.getCustomerId()).isEqualTo(42L);
        assertThat(ev.eventType()).isEqualTo("order.state.changed");
        assertThat(ev.aggregateType()).isEqualTo("ORDER");
        assertThat(ev.aggregateId()).isEqualTo("100");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("submit: 非法转换 → OrderException(STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID) 409, 不是 StateMachineException")
    void submit_illegalTransition_throws409() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_SETTLED);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_SETTLED)).thenReturn(List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_CLOSED));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit(100L))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID);

        // 关键: 不能让 StateMachineException (14001/400) 逃出去
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit(100L))
                .isNotInstanceOf(com.lumen.extension.state.StateMachineException.class);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("cancel: 原因空白 → OrderException(REASON_REQUIRED)")
    void cancel_blankReason_throws() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.cancel(100L, "   "))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.REASON_REQUIRED);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("cancel: 原因超过 255 字 → OrderException(REASON_TOO_LONG), 不撞 MySQL 1406")
    void cancel_reasonTooLong_throws() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);

        // V42 的 cancel_reason 是 VARCHAR(255) —— 超长必须在 service 层挡,
        // 否则落库 MySQL 1406 → GlobalExceptionHandler 无分支 → HTTP 500。
        String tooLong = "x".repeat(256);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.cancel(100L, tooLong))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.REASON_TOO_LONG);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("cancel: 转换原因进事件流 (getReason), 同时落订单行 cancel_reason 列")
    void cancel_reasonGoesIntoEventAndOrderRow() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT)).thenReturn(List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED));

        service.cancel(100L, "客户临时取消本委托单");

        // 订单行上留痕
        assertThat(o.getCancelReason()).isEqualTo("客户临时取消本委托单");
        // 事件流里也带着原因 (退回这种没有列可落的原因就只存在于事件流里)
        ArgumentCaptor<OrderStateChangedEvent> captor =
                ArgumentCaptor.forClass(OrderStateChangedEvent.class);
        verify(eventBus).publish(captor.capture());
        assertThat(captor.getValue().getReason()).isEqualTo("客户临时取消本委托单");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("submit: 乐观锁冲突 (updateById 命中 0 行) → ORDER_CONFLICT, 且事件没发出去")
    void submit_optimisticLockConflict_throwsAndDoesNotPublish() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT)).thenReturn(List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM));
        when(cargoMapper.selectCount(any())).thenReturn(1L);
        when(serviceItemMapper.selectCount(any())).thenReturn(1L);
        // 状态 UPDATE 命中 0 行 = 版本不匹配 = 有人并发改过
        when(orderMapper.updateById(any(Order.class))).thenReturn(0);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit(100L))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_CONFLICT);

        // 关键: 冲突时不能发出「其实没发生」的转换事件
        verify(eventBus, never()).publish(any());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("confirm: 服务项金额合计为 0 → OrderException(AMOUNT_INVALID)")
    void confirm_zeroAmount_throws() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        OrderServiceItem s = new OrderServiceItem();
        s.setId(2L); s.setOrderId(100L); s.setAmount(BigDecimal.ZERO);
        when(serviceItemMapper.selectList(any(LambdaQueryWrapper.class))).thenReturn(List.of(s));
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM)).thenReturn(List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.confirm(100L))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.AMOUNT_INVALID);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("get: allowedNextStates 来自 StateMachineEngine.allowedNext")
    void get_allowedNextStatesFromEngine() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        lenient().when(cargoMapper.selectList(any(LambdaQueryWrapper.class))).thenReturn(List.of());
        lenient().when(serviceItemMapper.selectList(any(LambdaQueryWrapper.class))).thenReturn(List.of());
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT)).thenReturn(
                List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED,
                        OrderConstants.STATUS_VOID));

        OrderDetailView view = service.get(100L);
        assertThat(view.allowedNextStates()).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(
                OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_VOID);
    }
```

```bash
cd lumen-parent && mvn -pl lumen-order test -Dtest=OrderServiceImplTest
```

预期：编译失败 —— `找不到符号: 类 OrderStateChangedEvent` / 构造器参数不匹配。

- [ ] **Step 3: 建 `OrderStateChangedEvent`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/event/OrderStateChangedEvent.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.event;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonCreator;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.annotation.JsonProperty;
import com.lumen.extension.outbox.DomainEvent;
import lombok.Getter;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * 委托单状态变更事件 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>eventType 单一 + action 字段区分语义</b> —— 设计文档 (05v4) 用的是
 * {@code order.submitted} / {@code order.confirmed} 等多个 eventType, 但
 * {@code DomainEvent.eventType()} 是单值契约, 做多个子类会显著放大代码面。
 * 本类用一个 {@code eventType = "order.state.changed"} + {@code action} 承载全部 9 个动作
 * (submit / confirm / return / cancel / void / start_operation / deliver / settle / close)。
 *
 * <p><b>被 {@code lumen-finance} 反射消费</b> —— finance <b>不依赖</b> lumen-order,
 * 它按 {@code event.getClass().getSimpleName()} 判定类型、用反射取 getter
 * (照 {@code BusinessEventListener.mapDeliveryOrderEvent} 的既有套路)。
 * 所以<b>字段名不要随便改</b> —— finance 侧按 {@code getToState} / {@code getOrderId} /
 * {@code getCustomerId} / {@code getTotalWeightKg} / {@code getTotalVolumeCbm} /
 * {@code getTotalQty} / {@code getEstimatedAmount} 取值, 改了名字 finance 侧会静默拿到 null。
 *
 * <p><b>getter 命名必须是 {@code getXxx}</b> —— finance 侧反射是
 * {@code cls.getMethod("getToState")}, {@code getMethod} 只认 {@code get} 前缀的公有方法。
 * 照 {@code DeliveryOrderStateChangedEvent} 的写法: 类上打 {@code @Getter},
 * 字段即可自动生成 {@code getXxx()}; 基类 {@code DomainEvent} 自己的
 * {@code eventType()} / {@code aggregateType()} / {@code aggregateId()} / {@code tenantId()}
 * 是<b>无 get 前缀</b> 的抽象方法, 由子类覆写。
 *
 * <p><b>构造器必须打 {@code @JsonCreator} + {@code @JsonProperty}</b> ——
 * 这是本类能不能被 outbox 重投反序列化回来的<b>硬前提</b>:
 * {@code OutboxDispatcher} 的 mapper 虽然把字段可见性放宽成
 * {@code Visibility.FIELD, ANY} (让 private final 字段能被反射填),
 * 但 Jackson <b>仍然需要一个能实例化对象的构造器</b> ——
 * 本类没有无参构造器, 构造器又不带 {@code @JsonCreator} 时, Jackson 直接抛
 * {@code InvalidDefinitionException("no Creators, like default constructor, exist")},
 * dispatcher 捕获后重试 → 重试耗尽 → 这行 outbox 变 {@code DEAD_LETTER} (毒丸)。
 * 既有 {@code CountryStateChangedEvent} / {@code DeliveryOrderStateChangedEvent} 全是这个写法,
 * 本类照抄。payload 里缺某个 key (例如老数据没有 {@code reason}) 时, 对应参数传 null ✓
 * (dispatcher 的 mapper 没开 {@code FAIL_ON_MISSING_CREATOR_PROPERTIES})。
 *
 * <p><b>{@code reason} 字段承载「为什么转」</b> —— 取消 / 作废 / 关账的原因同时落在
 * 订单行的 {@code cancel_reason} / {@code void_reason} / {@code close_reason} 三列;
 * <b>退回 (return) 的原因只存在于本事件里</b> —— V42 的 {@code t_order} 没有
 * {@code return_reason} 列, 本轮不为此加列 (加列要动迁移脚本, 超出本轮范围)。
 *
 * <p><b>{@code actor} 本轮恒为 null</b> —— 还没接入操作人上下文 (登录态 / 员工 ID),
 * 字段先留着, 将来接上时不用改事件签名。
 */
@Getter
public class OrderStateChangedEvent extends DomainEvent {

    private final Long tenantId;
    private final Long orderId;
    private final String orderNo;
    private final String fromState;
    private final String toState;
    private final String action;
    private final String reason;
    private final Long customerId;
    private final BigDecimal totalWeightKg;
    private final BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm;
    private final BigDecimal totalQty;
    private final BigDecimal estimatedAmount;
    private final String currencyCode;
    private final String actor;

    @JsonCreator
    public OrderStateChangedEvent(
            @JsonProperty("tenantId") Long tenantId,
            @JsonProperty("orderId") Long orderId,
            @JsonProperty("orderNo") String orderNo,
            @JsonProperty("fromState") String fromState,
            @JsonProperty("toState") String toState,
            @JsonProperty("action") String action,
            @JsonProperty("reason") String reason,
            @JsonProperty("customerId") Long customerId,
            @JsonProperty("totalWeightKg") BigDecimal totalWeightKg,
            @JsonProperty("totalVolumeCbm") BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm,
            @JsonProperty("totalQty") BigDecimal totalQty,
            @JsonProperty("estimatedAmount") BigDecimal estimatedAmount,
            @JsonProperty("currencyCode") String currencyCode,
            @JsonProperty("actor") String actor) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId, "tenantId");
        Objects.requireNonNull(orderId, "orderId");
        Objects.requireNonNull(toState, "toState");
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
        this.orderId = orderId;
        this.orderNo = orderNo;
        this.fromState = fromState;
        this.toState = toState;
        this.action = action;
        this.reason = reason;
        this.customerId = customerId;
        this.totalWeightKg = totalWeightKg;
        this.totalVolumeCbm = totalVolumeCbm;
        this.totalQty = totalQty;
        this.estimatedAmount = estimatedAmount;
        this.currencyCode = currencyCode;
        this.actor = actor;
    }

    @Override
    public String eventType() {
        return "order.state.changed";
    }

    @Override
    public String aggregateType() {
        return "ORDER";
    }

    /** {@code DomainEvent.aggregateId()} 的签名是 {@code String} —— 必须转字符串。 */
    @Override
    public String aggregateId() {
        return String.valueOf(orderId);
    }

    @Override
    public Long tenantId() {
        return tenantId;
    }
}
```

> **已核实（不要照着旧写法写）**：
> - `DomainEvent` 基类**没有** `super(tenantId)` 构造器 —— 它只有隐式无参构造，
>   `tenantId` 必须由子类**自己存字段**并覆写 `tenantId()` 返回。
> - `DomainEvent.aggregateId()` 的返回类型是 **`String`**，不是 `Long`。
> - 基类的 `eventType()` / `aggregateType()` / `aggregateId()` / `tenantId()` 是
>   **无 get 前缀**的抽象方法；业务字段才用 `@Getter` 生成 `getXxx()`。
>
> 上面的类**必须**提供 `getToState()` / `getOrderId()` / `getCustomerId()` /
> `getTotalWeightKg()` / `getTotalVolumeCbm()` / `getTotalQty()` / `getEstimatedAmount()`
> 这 7 个 `get` 前缀的 getter（finance 侧按这些名字反射）—— `@Getter` 已经全部生成。
>
> **构造器上的 `@JsonCreator` / `@JsonProperty` 不能省** —— `OutboxDispatcher` 的 mapper
> 只是把**字段**可见性放宽成 `Visibility.FIELD, ANY`, 它**不能替 Jackson 造对象**:
> 本类没有无参构造器, 构造器不带 `@JsonCreator` 时 Jackson 直接抛
> `InvalidDefinitionException("no Creators, like default constructor, exist")`
> → dispatcher 捕获 → 重试 → 耗尽 → outbox 行变 `DEAD_LETTER`。
> `FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES=false` 只保证「payload 多出来的 key 不炸」,
> **不解决「没有构造器」的问题**。既有 `CountryStateChangedEvent` /
> `DeliveryOrderStateChangedEvent` 全是 `@JsonCreator` + `@JsonProperty` 写法, 照抄。

- [ ] **Step 4: 建 `OrderEventTypeRegistrar`（不建就等于往 outbox 里投毒）**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/event/OrderEventTypeRegistrar.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.event;

import com.lumen.extension.outbox.DomainEvent;
import com.lumen.extension.outbox.DomainEventTypeRegistrar;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * lumen-order 模块的 {@link DomainEventTypeRegistrar} 实现 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>把 lumen-order 写入 {@code t_event_outbox} 的业务事件注册到
 * {@link com.lumen.extension.outbox.OutboxDispatcher}, 让 dispatcher 能反序列化
 * payload 并重新 publish 到 Spring {@code ApplicationEventPublisher}.
 *
 * <p><b>不加会怎样</b>: {@code OutboxDispatcher.parseEvent} 对未注册的 event_type 抛
 * {@code IllegalArgumentException("Unknown event type: ...")} → 该 outbox 行被当毒丸,
 * 重试到 maxRetries 后转 {@code DEAD_LETTER}, 事件永久丢失。
 * 既有 {@code DispatchEventTypeRegistrar} / {@code CsEventTypeRegistrar} 都是为同一个坑加的。
 *
 * <p>事件类型字符串与 {@link OrderStateChangedEvent#eventType()} 保持一致
 * (本类硬编码字符串而非引用常量, 与既有两个 registrar 同款写法 —— 它们不能反向依赖
 * 事件类所在模块的常量)。
 */
@Component
public class OrderEventTypeRegistrar implements DomainEventTypeRegistrar {

    /** 与 {@link OrderStateChangedEvent#eventType()} 一致. */
    private static final String EVENT_TYPE_ORDER_STATE_CHANGED = "order.state.changed";

    @Override
    public Map<String, Class<? extends DomainEvent>> registeredTypes() {
        return Map.of(
                EVENT_TYPE_ORDER_STATE_CHANGED, OrderStateChangedEvent.class
        );
    }
}
```

补一条单测锁住「注册表的 key 与事件自己的 `eventType()` 逐字相等」——
**用实例取那个字符串, 不要在测试里再抄一遍字面量** (抄一遍等于没锁):

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/test/java/com/lumen/order/event/OrderEventTypeRegistrarTest.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.event;

import com.lumen.extension.outbox.DomainEvent;
import com.lumen.order.OrderConstants;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

@DisplayName("OrderEventTypeRegistrar 单元测试")
class OrderEventTypeRegistrarTest {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("registeredTypes: order.state.changed → OrderStateChangedEvent, key 与事件实例的 eventType() 逐字相等")
    void registeredTypes_keyMatchesEventType() {
        OrderEventTypeRegistrar registrar = new OrderEventTypeRegistrar();

        Map<String, Class<? extends DomainEvent>> types = registrar.registeredTypes();

        OrderStateChangedEvent event = new OrderStateChangedEvent(
                1L, 100L, "ORD-20261110-000001",
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, "submit",
                null, 42L, BigDecimal.valueOf(1000), BigDecimal.valueOf(12.5), BigDecimal.TEN,
                BigDecimal.ZERO, "CNY", null);

        // key 用实例的 eventType() 去查 —— registrar 里写错字符串就立刻红
        assertThat(types).containsEntry(event.eventType(), OrderStateChangedEvent.class);
        assertThat(types).hasSize(1);
    }
}
```

> **不重复计费** —— 补 registrar 不会让 finance 重复入账:
> `lumen-finance` 的 `BillingEngineServiceImpl.accrue(...)` 先插 `t_billing_event_log`,
> 靠 `UNIQUE (tenant_id, source_event_id, deleted)` 兜底幂等, 重复事件直接跳过。

- [ ] **Step 5: 在 `OrderServiceImpl` 里实现状态转换**

**先补接口声明** —— `OrderService.java` 的 11 个转换方法在 Task 3 里**没有**声明
（Task 3 的 `OrderServiceImpl` 只实现了 7 个 CRUD 方法, 若提前声明转换方法, 接口有抽象方法
而实现类没有 → 编译不过）。所以本 Step 要先在接口里补上声明, 再写实现：

在 `OrderService.java` 的 `createForCustomer` 之后补：

```java
    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 状态转换

    /** 提交待客户确认 (DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM)。 */
    OrderDetailView submit(Long id);

    /** 客户确认 (PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED)。 */
    OrderDetailView confirm(Long id);

    /** 退回销售修改 (PENDING_CONFIRM → DRAFT)。 */
    OrderDetailView returnToDraft(Long id, String reason);

    /** 取消。 */
    OrderDetailView cancel(Long id, String reason);

    /** 作废。 */
    OrderDetailView voidOrder(Long id, String reason);

    /** 开始操作执行 (CONFIRMED → IN_OPERATION)。 */
    OrderDetailView startOperation(Long id);

    /** 交付 (IN_OPERATION → DELIVERED)。 */
    OrderDetailView deliver(Long id);

    /** 结算 (DELIVERED → SETTLED)。 */
    OrderDetailView settle(Long id);

    /** 关账 (SETTLED → CLOSED)。 */
    OrderDetailView close(Long id, String reason);

    /** 门户端确认 (归属校验 + 状态机)。 */
    OrderDetailView confirmOwned(Long id, Long customerId);

    /** 门户端取消 (归属校验 + 状态机)。 */
    OrderDetailView cancelOwned(Long id, Long customerId, String reason);
```

在 `OrderServiceImpl` 的字段区补两个依赖：

```java
    private final StateMachineEngine stateMachineEngine;
    private final EventBus eventBus;
```

并在 import 区补：

```java
import com.lumen.extension.outbox.EventBus;
import com.lumen.extension.state.StateMachineEngine;
```

> **事件发布用 `EventBus`（`com.lumen.extension.outbox.EventBus`）不是 `ApplicationEventPublisher`** ——
> `DefaultEventBus.publish` 会在**同一事务内**写 `t_event_outbox` 行 + `ApplicationEventPublisher.publishEvent`，
> 这正是「同事务写 outbox」的既有机制。`EventBus.publish` 是 `Propagation.MANDATORY`，
> 所以调用方（转换方法）**必须是 `@Transactional`**。

在 `OrderServiceImpl` 里加转换实现（每个都走同一条 `transition` 收口）：

```java
    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 状态转换

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView submit(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, "submit");
        requireBasicsComplete(o);
        o.setSubmittedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, "submit", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView confirm(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED, "confirm");
        requirePositiveAmount(o);
        o.setConfirmedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED, "confirm", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView returnToDraft(Long id, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "return");
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "return", reason);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView cancel(Long id, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED, "cancel");
        o.setCancelReason(reason);
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED, "cancel", reason);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView voidOrder(Long id, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_VOID, "void");
        o.setVoidReason(reason);
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_VOID, "void", reason);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView startOperation(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_IN_OPERATION, "start_operation");
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_IN_OPERATION, "start_operation", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView deliver(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_DELIVERED, "deliver");
        o.setDeliveredAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_DELIVERED, "deliver", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView settle(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_SETTLED, "settle");
        o.setSettledAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_SETTLED, "settle", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView close(Long id, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        // 关账也要原因 —— 与 cancel / voidOrder / returnToDraft 同口径:
        // controller 的 @RequestBody(required = false) 允许 body 缺省,
        // 缺省时由这里抛 REASON_REQUIRED (400), 而不是让空原因静默落库。
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CLOSED, "close");
        o.setCloseReason(reason);
        o.setClosedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CLOSED, "close", reason);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView confirmOwned(Long id, Long customerId) {
        Order o = requireOwned(id, customerId);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED, "confirm");
        requirePositiveAmount(o);
        o.setConfirmedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED, "confirm", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView cancelOwned(Long id, Long customerId, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOwned(id, customerId);
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED, "cancel");
        o.setCancelReason(reason);
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED, "cancel", reason);
    }
```

再加收口与守卫方法：

```java
    /**
     * 状态合法性判定 —— **必须走 {@code StateMachineEngine}**, 不看 Java 常量表。
     *
     * <p>用 {@code allowedNext} 先判 (而不是直接调 {@code assertTransition}), 因为
     * {@code assertTransition} 抛的是 {@code StateMachineException} —— code 14001 / HTTP **400**,
     * 与本轮约定的 409 / 409_101 不符。收口在这里统一转换, 客户端拿到的就是 409。
     */
    private void requireTransition(Order order, String toState, String action) {
        List<String> allowed = stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(
                OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE, order.getOrderStatus());
        if (!allowed.contains(toState)) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID,
                    "当前状态 " + order.getOrderStatus() + " 不允许转换到 " + toState);
        }
    }

    /**
     * 落状态 + 发事件 + 返回详情 —— 所有转换的唯一出口。
     *
     * <p><b>状态 UPDATE 命中 0 行 = 乐观锁冲突 → 抛 ORDER_CONFLICT (409_103),
     * 而且必须在发事件之前抛</b> —— 冲突时不能发出一条「其实没发生」的转换事件
     * (否则 finance 会照着一条不存在的转换去计费)。
     * 判定依据: 实体带 {@code @Version} → MP 生成
     * {@code SET ..., version = <新值> WHERE id = ? AND version = <旧值>} → 行必然变化
     * → MySQL affected rows 必为 1 → <b>命中 0 行只可能是版本不匹配</b>, 不是「值没变」。
     * (MP 的 {@code OptimisticLockerInnerInterceptor.doOptimisticLocker} 算出的新版本是
     * <b>参数</b> —— {@code updatedVersionVal = old + 1}, 写回实体后由 {@code SET} 取到;
     * 它生成的不是字面量 {@code version = version + 1}。)
     * 与 {@code OrderServiceImpl.update()} / {@code replaceChildren()} 的既有写法同款。
     *
     * <p><b>转换原因 ({@code reason}) 进事件流</b> ({@code getReason()}) ——
     * 取消 / 作废 / 关账的原因同时落在订单行的
     * {@code cancel_reason} / {@code void_reason} / {@code close_reason} 三列 (V42 都是 VARCHAR(255));
     * <b>退回 (return) 的原因只存在于事件里</b> —— V42 的 {@code t_order} 没有
     * {@code return_reason} 列, 本轮不为此加列 (加列要动迁移脚本, 超出本轮范围)。
     */
    private OrderDetailView applyTransition(Order order, String toState, String action, String reason) {
        String from = order.getOrderStatus();
        order.setOrderStatus(toState);
        int rows = orderMapper.updateById(order);
        if (rows == 0) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_CONFLICT,
                    "委托单已被他人修改, 请刷新后重试");
        }

        BigDecimal estimated = serviceItemMapper.selectList(
                        new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>()
                                .eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, order.getId()))
                .stream()
                .map(OrderServiceItem::getAmount)
                .filter(a -> a != null)
                .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);

        eventBus.publish(new OrderStateChangedEvent(
                TenantContext.require(), order.getId(), order.getOrderNo(),
                from, toState, action, reason, order.getCustomerId(),
                order.getTotalWeightKg(), order.getTotalVolumeCbm(), order.getTotalQty(),
                estimated, order.getSettlementCurrency(), null));

        return toDetail(order);
    }

    /**
     * 转换原因守卫 —— 长度区间 5..255 字。
     *
     * <p><b>上限 255 是 DDL 定的</b> —— V42 的 {@code cancel_reason} / {@code close_reason} /
     * {@code void_reason} 三列都是 {@code VARCHAR(255)}。不校验上限的话, 客户端传 300 字
     * → MySQL 1406 Data too long → {@code DataIntegrityViolationException}
     * → {@code GlobalExceptionHandler} 没有对应分支 → 落 catch-all → <b>HTTP 500</b>。
     * 在 service 层挡掉才能给客户端一个正经的 400。
     */
    private void requireReason(String reason) {
        if (reason == null || reason.isBlank() || reason.trim().length() < OrderConstants.REASON_MIN_LENGTH) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.REASON_REQUIRED);
        }
        if (reason.trim().length() > OrderConstants.REASON_MAX_LENGTH) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.REASON_TOO_LONG);
        }
    }

    /** submit 守卫: 必填项齐 + 至少 1 条明细 + 至少 1 条服务项。 */
    private void requireBasicsComplete(Order o) {
        if (o.getCustomerId() == null || o.getBusinessType() == null || o.getTradeDirection() == null
                || o.getIncoterms() == null || o.getServiceType() == null) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.CARGO_EMPTY,
                    "必填项缺失, 不能提交");
        }
        long cargoCount = cargoMapper.selectCount(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderCargo>().eq(OrderCargo::getOrderId, o.getId()));
        if (cargoCount == 0) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.CARGO_EMPTY);
        }
        long itemCount = serviceItemMapper.selectCount(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>().eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, o.getId()));
        if (itemCount == 0) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.SERVICE_ITEM_EMPTY);
        }
    }

    /** confirm 守卫: 服务项金额合计 > 0。 */
    private void requirePositiveAmount(Order o) {
        BigDecimal total = serviceItemMapper.selectList(
                        new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>()
                                .eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, o.getId()))
                .stream()
                .map(OrderServiceItem::getAmount)
                .filter(a -> a != null)
                .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
        if (total.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.AMOUNT_INVALID);
        }
    }

    private Order requireOwned(Long id, Long customerId) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectById(id);
        if (order == null || !customerId.equals(order.getCustomerId())) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return order;
    }
```

```java
                OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE, o.getOrderStatus());

                cargoViews, itemViews, allowedNext);
```

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/test/java/com/lumen/order/controller/OrderControllerTest.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.controller;

import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.order.OrderConstants;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;
import com.lumen.order.service.OrderService;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyLong;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.isNull;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

/**
 * OrderController wire contract 单测 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>照 {@code CsExceptionControllerTest} 的做法: 直接 new controller + 调方法,
 * 不用 MockMvc —— 锁的是「参数名 / 返回信封 / 分页口径」这些前端真正依赖的形状。
 */
@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
@DisplayName("OrderController 单元测试 (wire contract)")
class OrderControllerTest {

    @Mock private OrderService service;

    @InjectMocks
    private OrderController controller;

    @Test
    @DisplayName("page: 分页参数名 current/size, 返回 R<PageResult<OrderView>>")
    void page_usesCurrentAndSize() {
        OrderView v = new OrderView(1L, "ORD-20261110-000001", 42L, "OCEAN_FCL", "EXPORT",
                "FOB", "D2D", "上海", "洛杉矶", null, null, null,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "CNY", null, null, null);
        when(service.page(anyLong(), anyLong(), isNull(), isNull(), isNull(), isNull()))
                .thenReturn(PageResult.of(List.of(v), 1, 1, 20));

        var res = controller.page(1L, 20L, null, null, null, null);

        assertThat(res.getCode()).isZero();
        assertThat(res.getData().getRecords()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(res.getData().getPageNum()).isEqualTo(1);
        verify(service).page(eq(1L), eq(20L), isNull(), isNull(), isNull(), isNull());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("get: 返回 R<OrderDetailView>")
    void get_returnsDetail() {
        OrderDetailView d = new OrderDetailView(1L, "ORD-20261110-000001", 42L, "OCEAN_FCL",
                "EXPORT", "FOB", "D2D", null, null, null, null, null,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "CNY", null, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
                List.of(), List.of(), List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM));
        when(service.get(1L)).thenReturn(d);

        var res = controller.get(1L);

        assertThat(res.getCode()).isZero();
        assertThat(res.getData().orderNo()).isEqualTo("ORD-20261110-000001");
        assertThat(res.getData().allowedNextStates())
                .containsExactly(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("cancel: body 缺省 (null) → reason 传 null 给 service, 由 service 抛 REASON_REQUIRED")
    void cancel_nullBody_passesNullReasonToService() {
        // @RequestBody(required = false) 是这条链路的生死线 ——
        // body 缺省时必须能进到方法体 (而不是在参数解析阶段炸成 500),
        // 缺省的原因由 service 的 requireReason 挡成 400。
        when(service.cancel(1L, null)).thenReturn(detail());

        var res = controller.cancel(1L, null);

        assertThat(res.getCode()).isZero();
        verify(service).cancel(1L, null);
    }

    private OrderDetailView detail() {
        return new OrderDetailView(1L, "ORD-20261110-000001", 42L, "OCEAN_FCL",
                "EXPORT", "FOB", "D2D", null, null, null, null, null,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "CNY", null, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
                List.of(), List.of(), List.of());
    }
}
```
