- [ ] **Step 3: 建 `OrderController`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/controller/OrderController.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.controller;

import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;
import com.lumen.order.service.OrderService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 管理端货运委托单控制器 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>路径前缀 {@code /api/v1} —— 与既有管理端控制器一致
 * ({@code /api/v1/finance/accruals} / {@code /api/v1/dispatch/orders} / {@code /api/v1/wms/customers})。
 *
 * <p>分页参数名用 {@code current} / {@code size}（不是 pageNum / pageSize）——
 * 与 {@code CsExceptionController} / {@code SysAuditLogController} 一致。
 *
 * <p>异常统一由 {@code GlobalExceptionHandler.handleBiz} 处理
 * （{@code OrderException extends BizException}），本类不写 try/catch。
 *
 * <p><b>带 reason 的 4 个端点（return / cancel / void / close）的 body 可缺省</b> ——
 * 它们的 {@code @RequestBody} 必须写成 {@code required = false}。
 * {@code @RequestBody} 默认 {@code required = true}：客户端<b>不带 body</b> 时，
 * Spring 在<b>参数解析阶段</b>就抛 {@code HttpMessageNotReadableException}
 * ("Required request body is missing")，<b>方法体根本进不去</b> ——
 * 代码里那层 {@code body == null ? null : body.get("reason")} 的兜底形同虚设。
 * 而 order 模块<b>没有</b> {@code HttpMessageNotReadableException} 的 handler
 * （{@code GlobalExceptionHandler} 只处理 {@code BizException} /
 * {@code MethodArgumentNotValidException} / {@code BindException} /
 * {@code AccessDeniedException} / {@code AuthenticationException}），
 * 其余全落 catch-all → <b>HTTP 500</b>。
 * 正确行为是进到方法体 → service 的 {@code requireReason} 抛
 * {@code OrderException(REASON_REQUIRED)} → <b>HTTP 400</b>。
 * 全仓其它地方一律这么写（{@code PortalCommonController} / {@code PortalReconController} /
 * {@code ApprovalController} / {@code BillingAccrualController} / {@code BillingAdjustmentController} /
 * {@code BillingInvoiceController}）。
 *
 * <p><b>create / update 用 {@code @Valid @RequestBody}</b> ——
 * {@code CreateOrderRequest} 的 {@code cargo} / {@code serviceItems} 组件上带 {@code @Valid}，
 * 级联到 {@code CargoRequest} / {@code ServiceItemRequest} 的 jakarta 约束
 * （超长 / 负数 / 缺必填）→ {@code MethodArgumentNotValidException}
 * → {@code GlobalExceptionHandler.handleValidation} → <b>HTTP 400 + PARAM_INVALID (4001)</b>。
 * 管理端与门户端必须同口径（门户端的 {@code PortalFreightOrderRequest} 同样要在集合组件上写 {@code @Valid}）。
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/orders")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class OrderController {

    private final OrderService service;

    @GetMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:view')")
    public R<PageResult<OrderView>> page(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") long current,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") long size,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long customerId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String orderNo,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String businessType) {
        return R.ok(service.page(current, size, status, customerId, orderNo, businessType));
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:view')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> get(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.get(id));
    }

    @PostMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:create')")
    public R<OrderView> create(@Valid @RequestBody CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return R.ok(service.create(request));
    }

    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:edit')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> update(@PathVariable Long id,
                                      @Valid @RequestBody CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return R.ok(service.update(id, request));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/submit")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:submit')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> submit(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.submit(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/confirm")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:confirm')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> confirm(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.confirm(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/return")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:submit')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> returnToDraft(@PathVariable Long id,
                                             @RequestBody(required = false) Map<String, String> body) {
        return R.ok(service.returnToDraft(id, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/cancel")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:cancel')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> cancel(@PathVariable Long id,
                                     @RequestBody(required = false) Map<String, String> body) {
        return R.ok(service.cancel(id, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/void")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:void')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> voidOrder(@PathVariable Long id,
                                         @RequestBody(required = false) Map<String, String> body) {
        return R.ok(service.voidOrder(id, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/start-operation")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:operate')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> startOperation(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.startOperation(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/deliver")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:operate')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> deliver(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.deliver(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/settle")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:settle')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> settle(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.settle(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/close")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:close')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> close(@PathVariable Long id,
                                    @RequestBody(required = false) Map<String, String> body) {
        return R.ok(service.close(id, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }
}
```

> **13 个端点与权限码的对应**（`@PreAuthorize` 的 authority 必须与 V42 seed 的 code 逐字一致）：
>
> | 端点 | authority |
> |---|---|
> | `GET /api/v1/orders`、`GET /api/v1/orders/{id}` | `order:view` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders` | `order:create` |
> | `PUT /api/v1/orders/{id}` | `order:edit` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/submit`、`/return` | `order:submit` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/confirm` | `order:confirm` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/cancel` | `order:cancel` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/void` | `order:void` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/start-operation`、`/deliver` | `order:operate` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/settle` | `order:settle` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/close` | `order:close` |
>
> **「带 reason 的 4 个端点」**（return / cancel / void / close）用
> `@RequestBody(required = false) Map<String, String>` 而不是专门的 DTO —— 理由：这 4 个端点的 body
> 只有 `{"reason": "..."}` 一个键，为它建 4 个 record 是纯噪音。
> **`required = false` 不能省** —— 默认 `required = true` 时客户端不带 body 会在
> 参数解析阶段炸（`HttpMessageNotReadableException`），order 模块没有它的 handler
> → HTTP 500，方法体里那层 `body == null` 兜底永远走不到。
> reason 的长度校验在 service 层做（`OrderConstants.REASON_MIN_LENGTH` = 5 下限，
> `OrderConstants.REASON_MAX_LENGTH` = 255 上限 —— 对齐 V42 的
> `cancel_reason` / `close_reason` / `void_reason` 三列 `VARCHAR(255)`），
> 越界分别返回 `400_103 REASON_REQUIRED` / `400_107 REASON_TOO_LONG`。

## Task 6: finance 侧接 `OrderStateChangedEvent` → 交付即计费

**Files:**

**为什么放在 Task 6 而不是 Task 4**：Task 4 只负责「事件发出去」，Task 6 负责「finance 收到并计费」。
两者之间的契约是**反射**（finance 不依赖 lumen-order），所以必须有一个单测锁住
「finance 按 `getXxx()` 读到了正确的值」—— 这个单测就是本 Task 的核心。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试 —— finance 能读到 Order 事件并映射成计费 DTO**

在 `BusinessEventListenerTest.java` 的 import 区补：

```java
import com.lumen.finance.FinanceConstants;
```

> `FinanceConstants` 已经在 import 区了（既有测试用了 `FinanceConstants.BIZ_LAST_MILE`），
> **不要重复加**。

在文件末尾的 `test stubs` 段之前插入 2 个用例：

```java
    // ---------- 4. OrderStateChangedEvent DELIVERED → engine.accrue 被调用 ----------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("OrderStateChangedEvent DELIVERED → engine.accrue(DTO) 被调用, 字段映射正确")
    void onEvent_orderDelivered_callsAccrue() {
        OrderStateChangedEvent event = new OrderStateChangedEvent(
            1001L,                                    // tenantId
            "DELIVERED",                              // toState
            555L,                                     // orderId
            42L,                                      // customerId
            new BigDecimal("1200"),                   // totalWeightKg
            new BigDecimal("12.5"),                   // totalVolumeCbm
            new BigDecimal("88"),                     // totalQty
            new BigDecimal("8600.00")                 // estimatedAmount
        );

        listener.onEvent(event);

        ArgumentCaptor<BillingEventDto> cap = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(BillingEventDto.class);
        verify(engine).accrue(cap.capture());
        BillingEventDto dto = cap.getValue();
        assertThat(dto.tenantId()).isEqualTo(1001L);
        assertThat(dto.businessType()).isEqualTo(FinanceConstants.BIZ_ORDER);
        assertThat(dto.businessId()).isEqualTo(555L);
        assertThat(dto.direction()).isEqualTo(FinanceConstants.DIR_RECEIVABLE);
        assertThat(dto.counterpartyId()).isEqualTo(42L);
        assertThat(dto.counterpartyType()).isEqualTo(FinanceConstants.CP_CUSTOMER);
        assertThat(dto.env()).containsEntry("weight", new BigDecimal("1200"));
        assertThat(dto.env()).containsEntry("quantity", new BigDecimal("88"));
        assertThat(dto.env()).containsEntry("amount", new BigDecimal("8600.00"));
        assertThat(dto.sourceEventId()).isNotBlank();
    }

    // ---------- 5. OrderStateChangedEvent 非 DELIVERED → 静默 ----------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("OrderStateChangedEvent CONFIRMED → 静默跳过, 不调 accrue")
    void onEvent_orderNotDelivered_skipped() {
        OrderStateChangedEvent event = new OrderStateChangedEvent(
            1001L, "CONFIRMED", 555L, 42L,
            new BigDecimal("1200"), new BigDecimal("12.5"), new BigDecimal("88"),
            new BigDecimal("8600.00"));

        listener.onEvent(event);

        verify(engine, never()).accrue(any(BillingEventDto.class));
    }
```

在文件末尾的 `test stubs` 段里补一个 stub 类（**类名必须叫
`OrderStateChangedEvent`** —— listener 按 `getClass().getSimpleName()` 分派，
名字对不上就静默跳过，测试会「假绿」）：

```java
    /**
     * 委托单状态事件 stub (Iteration 4.6).
     *
     * <p>类名必须与 lumen-order 的 {@code OrderStateChangedEvent} <b>逐字相同</b> ——
     * listener 靠 {@code getClass().getSimpleName()} 分派, 名字对不上会静默跳过。
     *
     * <p>getter 全部用 {@code getXxx} 命名 —— finance 侧反射是
     * {@code cls.getMethod("getToState")}, 只认 get 前缀。
     */
    public static class OrderStateChangedEvent extends DomainEvent {
        private final Long tenantId;
        private final String toState;
        private final Long orderId;
        private final Long customerId;
        private final BigDecimal totalWeightKg;
        private final BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm;
        private final BigDecimal totalQty;
        private final BigDecimal estimatedAmount;

        public OrderStateChangedEvent(Long tenantId, String toState, Long orderId, Long customerId,
                                      BigDecimal totalWeightKg, BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm,
                                      BigDecimal totalQty, BigDecimal estimatedAmount) {
            this.tenantId = tenantId;
            this.toState = toState;
            this.orderId = orderId;
            this.customerId = customerId;
            this.totalWeightKg = totalWeightKg;
            this.totalVolumeCbm = totalVolumeCbm;
            this.totalQty = totalQty;
            this.estimatedAmount = estimatedAmount;
        }

        public Long getTenantId() { return tenantId; }
        public String getToState() { return toState; }
        public Long getOrderId() { return orderId; }
        public Long getCustomerId() { return customerId; }
        public BigDecimal getTotalWeightKg() { return totalWeightKg; }
        public BigDecimal getTotalVolumeCbm() { return totalVolumeCbm; }
        public BigDecimal getTotalQty() { return totalQty; }
        public BigDecimal getEstimatedAmount() { return estimatedAmount; }

        @Override public String eventType() { return "order.state.changed"; }
        @Override public String aggregateType() { return "ORDER"; }
        @Override public String aggregateId() { return String.valueOf(orderId); }
        @Override public Long tenantId() { return tenantId; }
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 在 `BusinessEventListener` 里加 case + 映射方法**

在 `mapToBillingEvent` 的 switch 里加一行：

```java
        return switch (simpleName) {
            case "DeliveryOrderStateChangedEvent" -> mapDeliveryOrderEvent(event);
            case "OrderStateChangedEvent" -> mapOrderEvent(event);
            // TODO(WMS): add cases for "WmsInboundCompletedEvent" and
            //            "WmsOutboundCompletedEvent" once lumen-wms publishes them.

```

在 `mapDeliveryOrderEvent` 方法下面加：

```java
    /**
     * 货运委托单状态事件 → BillingEventDto (仅 DELIVERED 触发计费).
     *
     * <p>env 提取规则 (全部通过反射取 getter):
     * <ul>
     *   <li>{@code weight}   ← {@code getTotalWeightKg()} (BigDecimal)</li>
     *   <li>{@code quantity} ← {@code getTotalQty()} (件数)</li>
     *   <li>{@code amount}   ← {@code getEstimatedAmount()} (委托单服务项金额合计)</li>
     *   <li>{@code distance} ← 不提供 (委托单没有距离维度; 留空让 Aviator 走 null 分支)</li>
     * </ul>
     *
     * <p><b>env 的 key 必须落在 BillingScriptEngine 的沙箱白名单内</b>
     * (amount / weight / distance / quantity / customer_level / zone / service_type /
     * is_weekend / is_holiday) —— 超出白名单求值时抛
     * {@code FinanceException_UnknownVar}, accrual 落 OPEN_FAILED。
     *
     * <p>businessType = {@code ORDER} —— 对应 {@code t_billing_rule.business_type}。
     * 没有 ACTIVE 的 ORDER 规则时 {@code accrue} 会走 markNoRule 分支 (不写 accrual),
     * 这是预期行为, 不是 bug。
     */
    private BillingEventDto mapOrderEvent(DomainEvent event) {
        Object toState = invokeGetter(event, "getToState", "toState");
        if (!FinanceConstants.FIN_TRIGGER_STATE_ORDER.equals(toState)) {
            return null;
        }

        Map<String, Object> env = new HashMap<>();
        putIfPresent(env, "weight",   invokeGetter(event, "getTotalWeightKg", "weight"));
        putIfPresent(env, "quantity", invokeGetter(event, "getTotalQty", "quantity"));
        putIfPresent(env, "amount",   invokeGetter(event, "getEstimatedAmount", "amount"));

        Long businessId = asLong(invokeGetter(event, "getOrderId", "businessId"));
        Long counterpartyId = asLong(invokeGetter(event, "getCustomerId", "counterpartyId"));
        return new BillingEventDto(
            event.tenantId(),
            event.getEventId(),
            FinanceConstants.BIZ_ORDER,
            businessId,
            FinanceConstants.DIR_RECEIVABLE,
            counterpartyId,
            FinanceConstants.CP_CUSTOMER,
            env
        );
    }
```

## Task 7: 门户端 —— 客户自助建委托单 / 看自己的单 / 确认 / 取消

**Files:**
- Modify: `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/src/main/java/com/lumen/customer/security/PortalAuthService.java` —— 门户 JWT 的 perms 来自这里的**硬编码清单 `CUSTOMER_PERMS`, 不查 DB**（V42 seed 的 `sys_permission` 只对管理端走 `sys_role_permission` 的那条通路有效）
- Modify: `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/src/test/java/com/lumen/customer/security/PortalAuthServiceTest.java`
- Create: `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/src/main/java/com/lumen/customer/dto/PortalFreightOrderRequest.java`
- Create: `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/src/main/java/com/lumen/customer/controller/PortalFreightOrderController.java`

> **Task 1 已经在 `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/pom.xml` 里加了 `lumen-order` 依赖** ——
> 本 Task 直接 import `com.lumen.order.dto.*` / `com.lumen.order.service.OrderService`，不要再改 pom。

- [ ] **Step 1: 先写失败测试 —— JWT 里必须带 4 个新 authority**

改 `PortalAuthServiceTest.java` 里 `login_issuedTokenCarriesMerchantBillingAuthorities`
（就是那个 `containsExactlyInAnyOrder`）—— **把断言从 11 个改成 15 个**：

```java
        assertThat(perms).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(
            "customer:track:view",
            "customer:order:create",
            "customer:order:view",
            "customer:order:cancel",
            "customer:invoice:view",
            "customer:recon:view",
            "customer:payment:view",
            "customer:adjustment:view",
            "customer:adjustment:create",
            "customer:profile:view",
            "customer:profile:edit",
            // 4.6 — 货运委托单 (门户端 4 个)
            "customer:freight_order:view",
            "customer:freight_order:create",
            "customer:freight_order:confirm",
            "customer:freight_order:cancel");
```

> **为什么必须改这条测试**：`CUSTOMER_PERMS` 是**硬编码常量**，不查库。
> 只做 V42 的 `sys_permission` / `sys_role_permission` seed 而漏改这个常量，
> 门户的 **5 个端点会全 403**（`view` 这个 authority 覆盖列表 + 详情两个端点，
> 4 个 authority 对应 5 个端点）而且日志看不出原因 —— 这条断言是那道漏斗的唯一防线。
> `containsExactlyInAnyOrder` 是**精确匹配**：多一个少一个都红，别改成 `contains`。
>
> **断言打在「签发出去的 token 的 perms claim」上，而不是反射读 `CUSTOMER_PERMS` 私有常量**
> —— 走登录链路才能证明「perms 真的进了 token」；反射读常量只能证明常量里有那 4 个字符串，
> 中间隔着「token 怎么组装」这一层就断了。所以这里**不需要**给 `CUSTOMER_PERMS` 加 getter。
>
> 另外：Task 1 的 V42 seed 已经把 4 个 `customer:freight_order:*` 绑给了 role 14 (customer)，
> 但门户登录**根本不走** `sys_role_permission` —— 门户 JWT 的 perms 全部来自这个常量。
> **两条通路都要有**，少任何一条都 403。

- [ ] **Step 5: 建 `PortalFreightOrderRequest`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/src/main/java/com/lumen/customer/dto/PortalFreightOrderRequest.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.customer.dto;

import com.lumen.order.dto.CargoRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemRequest;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 门户端建委托单请求 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>字段与 {@code com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest} 完全一致,
 * <b>只是没有 {@code customerId}</b> —— 客户由 {@code PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId()}
 * 强制注入, body 里不该有这个字段。共用一个 DTO 会逼出「@NotNull 但门户不传」
 * 或「不标但管理端能漏传」的二选一, 两个都是坑。
 *
 * <p>{@code cargo} / {@code serviceItems} <b>不标 {@code @NotEmpty}</b> —— 空集合由
 * {@code OrderService} 抛 400_101 / 400_102, 避免同一个失败有两条码。
 *
 * <p><b>但集合组件上必须标 {@code @Valid}</b> —— {@code CargoRequest} /
 * {@code ServiceItemRequest} 自带 jakarta 约束 ({@code @Size} / {@code @PositiveOrZero} /
 * {@code @NotBlank}), 而嵌套对象的约束要生效, 父 record 的集合组件上必须有 {@code @Valid}
 * (Spring 的级联校验规则)。漏了 {@code @Valid} → 门户端这些字段的约束<b>一条都不会触发</b>
 * → 门户提交超长 hsCode / 负数量直接落库 (MySQL 1406 或负数入库), 而不是 400。
 * 管理端 {@code CreateOrderRequest} 的同名组件就是带 {@code @Valid} 的 —— <b>两边同口径</b>,
 * 不能一个级联一个不级联。
 *
 * <p>{@code settlementCurrency} 标 {@code @Size(max = 3)} —— V42 的
 * {@code t_order.settlement_currency} 是 {@code CHAR(3)}, 传 4 个字符会 MySQL 1406 → 500。
 *
 * <p><b>长度约束逐列对齐 {@code t_order} 的真实列宽</b> (V42 迁移) —— 与管理端
 * {@code CreateOrderRequest} <b>完全同口径</b>:
 * {@code business_type → VARCHAR(32)}、{@code trade_direction / incoterms / service_type → VARCHAR(16)}、
 * {@code origin_address / dest_address → VARCHAR(255)}、{@code container_type_code → VARCHAR(16)}、
 * {@code marks_and_nos / cargo_description → VARCHAR(500)}、{@code temperature_range → VARCHAR(32)}、
 * {@code remark → VARCHAR(1000)}、{@code settlement_currency → CHAR(3)}。
 * 少标一个 {@code @Size} 就等于超长时撞 MySQL 1406 → 500 而不是 400。
 *
 * <p><b>{@code serviceItems} 复用 order 模块的 {@code ServiceItemRequest}</b> ——
 * 因此它带着两个**内部字段** {@code supplierId} (我方供应商) 与 {@code costAmount} (我方成本额)。
 * 这两个字段在门户写入侧会被**强制丢弃** (见
 * {@code PortalFreightOrderController.stripInternalCost}), 客户填了也不生效 ——
 * <b>但 {@code costAmount} 上的 {@code @PositiveOrZero} 仍会生效</b>: 客户若传负值,
 * 校验发生在丢弃之前 —— jakarta 的级联校验在 controller 边界就炸
 * ({@code MethodArgumentNotValidException}), 落到
 * {@code GlobalExceptionHandler.handleValidation} → HTTP 400 +
 * {@code CommonErrorCode.PARAM_INVALID} (4001, 「参数校验失败」),
 * <b>不是</b> order 域的码 (order 域根本走不到)。这是本轮已知的契约毛刺,
 * 记录在 4.6 设计文档的「本轮不修的既有缺陷」一节; 前端 TS 类型没有这两个字段,
 * 正常客户端不会触发。
 */
public record PortalFreightOrderRequest(
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 32) String businessType,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 16) String tradeDirection,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 16) String incoterms,
        @NotBlank @Size(max = 16) String serviceType,
        @Size(max = 255) String originAddress,
        @Size(max = 255) String destAddress,
        LocalDate cargoReadyDate,
        LocalDate etd,
        LocalDate eta,
        Integer containerQty,
        @Size(max = 16) String containerTypeCode,
        @Size(max = 500) String marksAndNos,
        @Size(max = 500) String cargoDescription,
        Boolean isOversized,
        @Size(max = 32) String temperatureRange,
        @Size(max = 3) String settlementCurrency,
        @Size(max = 1000) String remark,
        @Valid List<CargoRequest> cargo,
        @Valid List<ServiceItemRequest> serviceItems
) {}
```

> `CargoRequest` / `ServiceItemRequest` **直接复用** `lumen-order` 的两个 record
> （spec §六·2b 明确允许）—— `lumen-customer` 依赖 `lumen-order`，直接 import。
> 两个 record 自己的 jakarta 约束（长度 / 正数 / 必填）都在，**前提是这里的集合组件上写了 {@code @Valid}**。

- [ ] **Step 6: 建 `PortalFreightOrderController`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/src/main/java/com/lumen/customer/controller/PortalFreightOrderController.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.customer.controller;

import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import com.lumen.customer.dto.PortalFreightOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.customer.web.PortalIdentity;
import com.lumen.order.OrderConstants;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemView;
import com.lumen.order.service.OrderService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 客户门户货运委托单 controller (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>路由前缀 {@code /api/customer/freight-order} —— 与既有城配下单
 * ({@code /api/customer/order}) <b>完全分开</b>, 两者互不影响。
 * {@code CustomerContextFilter} 按 URL 前缀 {@code /api/customer/} 判定门户路由,
 * 所以这个前缀天然受门户鉴权保护。
 *
 * <p>5 个端点:
 * <ol>
 *   <li>GET  {@code /}                — {@code customer:freight_order:view}   — 我的委托单列表</li>
 *   <li>GET  {@code /{id}}            — {@code customer:freight_order:view}   — 详情 (跨客户 404)</li>
 *   <li>POST {@code /}                — {@code customer:freight_order:create} — 建单 (落 DRAFT)</li>
 *   <li>POST {@code /{id}/confirm}    — {@code customer:freight_order:confirm} — PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED</li>
 *   <li>POST {@code /{id}/cancel}     — {@code customer:freight_order:cancel}  — DRAFT / PENDING_CONFIRM → CANCELLED</li>
 * </ol>
 *
 * <p><b>客户能自助撤单的状态只有 {@code DRAFT} / {@code PENDING_CONFIRM}</b> ——
 * 依据是 order 域常量 {@link OrderConstants#PORTAL_CANCELABLE_STATES}；
 * {@code CONFIRMED} 及其后走管理端 {@code POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/cancel} ({@code order:cancel})。
 * 状态闸实现在 {@code OrderServiceImpl.requirePortalCancelable}。
 *
 * <p><b>归属边界</b>: customerId 一律来自 {@link PortalIdentity#requireCounterpartyId()},
 * <b>绝不从 body / query 读</b>。跨客户访问一律 404 (不泄露存在性)。
 *
 * <p><b>异常</b>: {@code OrderException extends BizException} → 由
 * {@code GlobalExceptionHandler.handleBiz} 翻译成 404 / 409 / 400。
 * <b>本类不新建 advice</b> (spec §六·3.1)。
 *
 * <p><b>{@code /cancel} 的 body 可缺省</b> —— {@code @RequestBody(required = false)}:
 * 客户端不带 body 时必须能进到方法体 (把 reason 传 null 给 service),
 * 由 service 的 {@code requireReason} 抛 {@code REASON_REQUIRED} (400_103) /
 * {@code REASON_TOO_SHORT} (400_108)。
 * 门户模块比管理端轻 —— {@code CustomerExceptionHandler} 有
 * {@code HttpMessageNotReadableException} 分支 (返回 400 而不是 500),
 * 但错误信息会变成「参数解析失败」而不是「原因不能为空」, 语义不对。
 *
 * <p><b>内部成本双向都堵</b> —— 写入侧建单时把服务项的 {@code supplierId} /
 * {@code costAmount} 强制置 null; 读出侧 {@code detail} / {@code confirm} / {@code cancel}
 * 三个端点返回详情前再裁一次 (见 private {@code toPortalView(OrderDetailView)})。
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/customer/freight-order")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PortalFreightOrderController {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    // ================================================================
    //  1. GET / — 我的委托单列表
    // ================================================================

    @GetMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:view')")
    public R<PageResult<OrderView>> list(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") long current,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") long size,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String status) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(orderService.pageByCustomer(customerId, current, size, status));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  2. GET /{id} — 详情
    // ================================================================

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:view')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> detail(@PathVariable Long id) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(toPortalView(orderService.getOwned(id, customerId)));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  3. POST / — 建单 (DRAFT)
    // ================================================================

    @PostMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:create')")
    public R<OrderView> create(@Valid @RequestBody PortalFreightOrderRequest req) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(orderService.createForCustomer(customerId, toCreateRequest(customerId, req)));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  4. POST /{id}/confirm — 客户确认
    // ================================================================

    @PostMapping("/{id}/confirm")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:confirm')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> confirm(@PathVariable Long id) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(toPortalView(orderService.confirmOwned(id, customerId)));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  5. POST /{id}/cancel — 客户取消
    // ================================================================

    @PostMapping("/{id}/cancel")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:cancel')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> cancel(@PathVariable Long id,
                                     @RequestBody(required = false) Map<String, String> body) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(toPortalView(
                orderService.cancelOwned(id, customerId, body == null ? null : body.get("reason"))));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  private helpers
    // ================================================================

    /**
     * 门户 DTO → 管理端 DTO。
     *
     * <p><b>customerId 用服务端取到的那个</b> —— body 里没有这个字段, 客户端也没法指定归属。
     * {@code OrderService.createForCustomer} 里还会再强制覆盖一次 (双保险)。
     *
     * <p><b>内部成本字段被抹掉</b>: {@code costAmount} (我方成本) 与 {@code supplierId}
     * (供应商) 强制置 null —— 门户端客户**不应该**能写入我方内部成本。
     */
    private CreateOrderRequest toCreateRequest(Long customerId, PortalFreightOrderRequest req) {
        List<ServiceItemRequest> items = req.serviceItems() == null
                ? List.of()
                : req.serviceItems().stream().map(this::stripInternalCost).toList();
        return new CreateOrderRequest(
                customerId,
                req.businessType(),
                req.tradeDirection(),
                req.incoterms(),
                req.serviceType(),
                req.originAddress(),
                req.destAddress(),
                req.cargoReadyDate(),
                req.etd(),
                req.eta(),
                req.containerQty(),
                req.containerTypeCode(),
                req.marksAndNos(),
                req.cargoDescription(),
                req.isOversized(),
                req.temperatureRange(),
                req.settlementCurrency(),
                req.remark(),
                req.cargo(),
                items);
    }

    /** 抹掉服务项里的内部成本字段 —— **只处理写入侧**; 读出侧的裁剪见下面的 {@code toPortalView}。 */
    private ServiceItemRequest stripInternalCost(ServiceItemRequest r) {
        return new ServiceItemRequest(
                r.feeType(), r.feeName(), r.feeCategory(), r.calcBasis(),
                r.qty(), r.unitPrice(), r.amount(), r.currencyCode(),
                null, null, r.isMandatory(), r.remark());
    }

    /**
     * <b>门户视图的收口</b> —— 做两件事: 抹掉服务项里的内部成本, 收窄 allowedNextStates。
     *
     * <p><b>第一件: 读出侧的内部成本裁剪</b> —— 抹掉服务项里的 {@code supplierId} (我方供应商)
     * 与 {@code costAmount} (我方成本额), 其余 38 个组件原样保留。
     *
     * <p><b>为什么门户详情端点必须走这一步</b>: {@code OrderDetailView} 与管理端
     * {@code com.lumen.order.controller.OrderController#get} 返回的是<b>同一个</b>视图,
     * 它的 {@code serviceItems} 带 {@code supplierId} / {@code costAmount} —— 直接返给
     * 客户就是我方采购成本与供应商名单泄露。写入侧早就堵了 (建单强制置 null),
     * 读出侧却是开的 —— <b>半堵比不堵更糟</b>: 门户自建的单看不到成本,
     * 内部建的单客户一点就看到。所以本轮把读出侧一并堵上。
     *
     * <p><b>三个端点共用这一条路径</b> —— {@code detail} / {@code confirm} / {@code cancel}
     * 返回的都是 {@code OrderDetailView}, 漏掉哪一个就漏一个口子。
     *
     * <p><b>第二件: 收窄 allowedNextStates</b> —— 见 {@link #portalNextStates(String)}。
     *
     * <p>列表端点不需要这一步 —— 它返回的是 {@code OrderView}, 压根没有成本字段。
     */
    private OrderDetailView toPortalView(OrderDetailView detail) {
        List<ServiceItemView> items = detail.serviceItems() == null
                ? null
                : detail.serviceItems().stream().map(ServiceItemView::withoutInternalCost).toList();
        return detail.withServiceItems(items)
                .withAllowedNextStates(portalNextStates(detail.orderStatus()));
    }

    /**
     * 门户能触发的下一态 —— 只认「客户自己点得到的」那两个动作。
     *
     * <p><b>状态集合不在这里写死</b> —— 取 order 域的
     * {@link OrderConstants#PORTAL_CANCELABLE_STATES} / {@link OrderConstants#PORTAL_CONFIRMABLE_STATES}:
     * 门户端按钮可见性 (本方法) 与门户端放行闸
     * ({@code OrderServiceImpl.requirePortalCancelable}) 必须同口径。
     */
    private List<String> portalNextStates(String orderStatus) {
        List<String> next = new ArrayList<>();
        if (OrderConstants.PORTAL_CONFIRMABLE_STATES.contains(orderStatus)) {
            next.add(OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED);
        }
        if (OrderConstants.PORTAL_CANCELABLE_STATES.contains(orderStatus)) {
            next.add(OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED);
        }
        return next;
    }
}
```

> **读出侧的内部成本本轮已堵**（实施期订正，2026-11-12 回填）：门户**详情 / 确认 / 取消**
> 三个端点返回详情前统一走 `toPortalView(OrderDetailView)` —— 抹掉服务项里的
> `costAmount` / `supplierId`（`ServiceItemView.withoutInternalCost()`），
> 并把 `allowedNextStates` 收窄成「客户自己点得到的那一组」。
> **没有新增门户专用 DTO** —— 共用 `OrderDetailView` + 就地裁剪
> （`OrderDetailView.withServiceItems(List)` / `withAllowedNextStates(List)`）。
> 写入侧仍由 `stripInternalCost(ServiceItemRequest)` 负责 —— **读写两侧都堵**。

> **验收命令的形式**：bootstrap 的 IT 走 **failsafe**，必须用
> `mvn -pl lumen-bootstrap verify -Dit.test='XxxIT'`。
> `mvn test -Dit.test=X` 会跑 **0 个用例** = **假绿**。
> 单条 IT 类用 `-DargLine="-Xmx3g"`；一次跑多条用 `-DargLine="-Xmx3000m"`。

- [ ] **Step 1: 建共享 fixture `OrderAdminFixtureBase`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderAdminFixtureBase.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.HttpEntity;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — 货运委托单管理端 IT 的共享 fixture.
 *
 * <p>3 个管理端 IT ({@code OrderLifecycleIT} / {@code OrderStateMachineSeedIT} /
 * {@code OrderNumberingIT} / {@code OrderBillingAccrualIT}) 共用:
 * 一个绑了 role 1 (SUPER_ADMIN) 的 admin 用户 + 一个客户 + 建单 body 构造器.
 *
 * <p><b>为什么用 role 1</b>: V42 把 10 个 {@code order:*} 权限显式绑给了 role 1
 * (id 334..343)。{@code sys_role_permission} 不是「SUPER_ADMIN 自动拥有全部权限」,
 * 靠 seed 显式补行 —— 用别的角色就得多绑一批权限, 与本 IT 要验的东西无关。
 *
 * <p><b>id 段</b>: fixture 主键从 970_001 递增 (避开 PortalOrderFixtureBase 的 960_001+
 * 与 BillingPermissionIT 的 901_001+)。{@code t_order} 是 AUTO_INCREMENT,
 * 但这里仍然手工指定 id —— 便于按 id 精确清理。
 */
abstract class OrderAdminFixtureBase extends IntegrationBase {

    protected static final long TEST_TENANT = 1L;
    /** SUPER_ADMIN (V2 seed: id 1, code='SUPER_ADMIN', tenant 1). */
    protected static final long SUPER_ADMIN_ROLE_ID = 1L;

    protected static final String ADMIN_USER = "order_admin_46";
    protected static final String ADMIN_PWD = "order-admin-pwd-2026";
    protected static final String CUSTOMER_CODE = "C046-ORD";
    protected static final String CUSTOMER_NAME = "4.6 委托单 IT 客户";

    private static final AtomicLong FIXTURE_ID = new AtomicLong(970_001L);

    @Autowired protected JdbcTemplate jdbc;
    @Autowired protected BCryptPasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;

    protected long adminUserId;
    protected long customerId;

    private final Set<Long> orderIds = new HashSet<>();
    private final Set<Long> userIds = new HashSet<>();
    private final List<Long> ruleIds = new ArrayList<>();

    @BeforeEach
    void seedOrderFixture() {
        adminUserId = nextId();
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO sys_user (id, tenant_id, username, password_hash, real_name, status, "
                + "version, deleted, created_at, updated_at) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, '4.6 IT admin', 1, 0, 0, NOW(), NOW())",
            adminUserId, ADMIN_USER, passwordEncoder.encode(ADMIN_PWD));
        userIds.add(adminUserId);
        bindRole(adminUserId, SUPER_ADMIN_ROLE_ID);

        customerId = nextId();
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO wms_customer (id, tenant_id, code, name, deleted, version, created_at, updated_at) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, 0, 1, NOW(), NOW())",
            customerId, CUSTOMER_CODE, CUSTOMER_NAME);
    }

    @AfterEach
    void cleanOrderFixture() {
        try {
            for (Long oid : orderIds) {
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_order_cargo WHERE order_id = ?", oid);
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_order_service_item WHERE order_id = ?", oid);
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_event_outbox WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER' AND aggregate_id = ?",
                        String.valueOf(oid));
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_billing_accrual WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?", oid);
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_billing_event_log WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?", oid);
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", oid);
            }
            for (Long rid : ruleIds) {
                try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_billing_rule_item WHERE rule_id = ?", rid); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_billing_rule WHERE id = ?", rid); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
            for (Long uid : userIds) {
                try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM sys_user_role WHERE user_id = ?", uid); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM sys_user WHERE id = ?", uid); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
            try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM wms_customer WHERE id = ?", customerId); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        } finally {
            orderIds.clear();
            userIds.clear();
            ruleIds.clear();
        }
    }

    // ==================================================================
    //  fixture helpers
    // ==================================================================

    protected long nextId() {
        return FIXTURE_ID.incrementAndGet();
    }

    protected void bindRole(long userId, long roleId) {
        long pk = Math.abs(System.nanoTime()) % 2_000_000_000L;
        try {
            jdbc.update(
                "INSERT INTO sys_user_role (id, user_id, role_id, tenant_id, created_at) "
                    + "VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1, NOW())", pk, userId, roleId);
        } catch (Exception dup) {
            // already bound — 幂等
        }
    }

    /** 记下一个本 IT 创建的订单 id, 供 teardown 精确清理。 */
    protected void trackOrder(long id) {
        orderIds.add(id);
    }

    /**
     * Seed 一条 ACTIVE 的 {@code t_billing_rule} (business_type='ORDER') + 单条 FORMULA item.
     *
     * <p>{@code BillingEngineServiceImpl.accrue} 只走 rule 路径, 没有 ACTIVE rule 时会
     * markNoRule 并返回 null —— 不写 accrual。priority=1 让本 IT 的 rule 在
     * {@code ORDER BY priority ASC, id ASC} 里排最前, 保证 accrue 一定能命中。
     */
    protected long seedActiveOrderRule(String expression) {
        long ruleId = nextId();
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_billing_rule "
                + "(id, tenant_id, business_type, direction, counterparty_type, currency, priority, "
                + " status, effective_from, effective_to, remark, created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, 'ORDER', 'RECEIVABLE', 'CUSTOMER', 'CNY', 1, 'ACTIVE', "
                + " DATE_SUB(CURRENT_DATE, INTERVAL 1 DAY), NULL, '4.6 IT fixture', NOW(3), NOW(3), 0, 0)",
            ruleId);
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_billing_rule_item "
                + "(rule_id, sequence, item_type, expression, expression_hash, result_field, "
                + " created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, 'FORMULA', ?, SHA2(?, 256), 'gross_amount', NOW(3), NOW(3), 0, 0)",
            ruleId, expression, expression);
        ruleIds.add(ruleId);
        return ruleId;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    //  HTTP helpers
    // ==================================================================

    protected String loginAdmin() {
        HttpHeaders h = new HttpHeaders();
        h.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        String body = "{\"tenantId\":" + TEST_TENANT
                + ",\"username\":\"" + ADMIN_USER + "\",\"password\":\"" + ADMIN_PWD + "\"}";
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = rest.exchange(
                "/api/v1/auth/login", HttpMethod.POST, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).as("admin login 应 200").isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(resp.getBody().getCode()).isEqualTo(0);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> data = (Map<String, Object>) resp.getBody().getData();
        String token = (String) data.get("accessToken");
        assertThat(token).as("admin login 应返回 accessToken").isNotBlank();
        return token;
    }

    protected HttpHeaders adminHeaders(String token) {
        HttpHeaders h = new HttpHeaders();
        h.setBearerAuth(token);
        h.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        return h;
    }

    protected ResponseEntity<R> adminGet(String path, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.GET, new HttpEntity<>(h), R.class);
    }

    protected ResponseEntity<R> adminPost(String path, String body, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.POST, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
    }

    protected ResponseEntity<R> adminPut(String path, String body, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.PUT, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    protected Map<String, Object> dataOf(ResponseEntity<R> resp) {
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).as("响应体不应为空").isNotNull();
        return (Map<String, Object>) resp.getBody().getData();
    }

    // ==================================================================
    //  request body helpers
    // ==================================================================

    /**
     * 构造 {@code POST /api/v1/orders} 的 body.
     *
     * <p>重量 100kg / 件数 10 件 / 服务项金额 1000 —— 供计费 IT 验算。
     *
     * <p><b>载荷必须满足 DTO 的 jakarta 约束</b> —— 否则 IT 会 400 而不是走通流程,
     * 会被误判成「状态机没生效」。逐条对照:
     * {@code cargo[].cargoNameCn} "钢材" (@Size max=128) /
     * {@code cargo[].hsCode} "7208" (max=16) / {@code cargo[].pkgQty} 10 (@PositiveOrZero) /
     * {@code cargo[].weightKg} 100 / {@code cargo[].volumeCbm} 12.5 (@PositiveOrZero) /
     * {@code cargo[].currencyCode} "CNY" (max=3) /
     * {@code serviceItems[].feeType} "OCEAN_FREIGHT" (@NotBlank max=32) /
     * {@code serviceItems[].feeName} "海运费" (@NotBlank max=128) /
     * {@code serviceItems[].calcBasis} "PER_CONTAINER" (@NotBlank max=16) /
     * {@code serviceItems[].qty|unitPrice|amount} 1/1000/1000 (@PositiveOrZero) /
     * 头部 {@code businessType=OCEAN_FCL} {@code tradeDirection=EXPORT}
     * {@code incoterms=FOB} {@code serviceType=D2D} (四个都在 {@code OrderConstants} 的受控词表里) /
     * {@code settlementCurrency} "CNY" (CHAR(3)) —— 全部合规 ✓。
     */
    protected String createOrderBody() {
        return createOrderBody("CNY");
    }

    /**
     * 同 {@link #createOrderBody()}, 但结算币种可换 ——
     * 「超长字段 → 400」那条 IT 用它传 4 个字符的 "USDD"。
     */
    protected String createOrderBody(String settlementCurrency) {
        return "{"
                + "\"customerId\":" + customerId + ","
                + "\"businessType\":\"OCEAN_FCL\","
                + "\"tradeDirection\":\"EXPORT\","
                + "\"incoterms\":\"FOB\","
                + "\"serviceType\":\"D2D\","
                + "\"originAddress\":\"上海洋山港\","
                + "\"destAddress\":\"洛杉矶港\","
                + "\"cargoReadyDate\":\"2026-11-20\","
                + "\"etd\":\"2026-11-25\","
                + "\"eta\":\"2026-12-10\","
                + "\"containerQty\":1,"
                + "\"containerTypeCode\":\"40HQ\","
                + "\"marksAndNos\":\"MSKU1234567\","
                + "\"cargoDescription\":\"五金配件\","
                + "\"isOversized\":false,"
                + "\"settlementCurrency\":\"" + settlementCurrency + "\","
                + "\"remark\":\"4.6 IT fixture\","
                + "\"cargo\":[{"
                + "\"cargoNameCn\":\"钢材\",\"cargoNameEn\":\"STEEL\",\"hsCode\":\"7208\","
                + "\"pkgType\":\"CARTON\",\"pkgQty\":10,\"unit\":\"CTN\","
                + "\"weightKg\":100,\"netWeightKg\":90,\"volumeCbm\":12.5,"
                + "\"lengthCm\":100,\"widthCm\":80,\"heightCm\":50,\"isStackable\":true,"
                + "\"cargoValue\":5000,\"currencyCode\":\"CNY\",\"isDangerous\":false"
                + "}],"
                + "\"serviceItems\":[{"
                + "\"feeType\":\"OCEAN_FREIGHT\",\"feeName\":\"海运费\",\"feeCategory\":\"FREIGHT\","
                + "\"calcBasis\":\"PER_CONTAINER\",\"qty\":1,\"unitPrice\":1000,\"amount\":1000,"
                + "\"currencyCode\":\"CNY\",\"isMandatory\":true"
                + "}]"
                + "}";
    }

    /** 构造带 reason 的转换端点 body。 */
    protected String reasonBody(String reason) {
        return "{\"reason\":\"" + reason + "\"}";
    }

    /** 从 create 响应里取新建订单的 id。 */
    protected Long orderIdOf(ResponseEntity<R> resp) {
        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).as("建单应 200, 实际 %d", resp.getStatusCode().value())
                .isEqualTo(200);
        Map<String, Object> data = dataOf(resp);
        Object id = data.get("id");
        assertThat(id).as("建单响应应含 id").isNotNull();
        long orderId = ((Number) id).longValue();
        trackOrder(orderId);
        return orderId;
    }

    /** 库里当前的 order_status。 */
    protected String statusOf(long orderId) {
        return jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT order_status FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, orderId);
    }

    /** 该订单发出的 outbox 事件数 (按 aggregate_type='ORDER' + aggregate_id 隔离)。 */
    protected int outboxCount(long orderId) {
        Integer n = jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_event_outbox WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER' AND aggregate_id = ?",
                Integer.class, String.valueOf(orderId));
        return n == null ? 0 : n;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `OrderLifecycleIT` —— 9 态全链路**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderLifecycleIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — 货运委托单主链路 IT.
 *
 * <p>建单 → 提交 → 确认 → 开始操作 → 交付 → 结算 → 关账, 7 步连续走通,
 * 每一步都断言 {@code t_order.order_status}, 最后断言 outbox 事件数。
 *
 * <p><b>事件数是 6, 不是 7</b> —— 计划里写的「7 次转换 = 7 条 outbox 事件」算错了:
 * 建单<b>不发</b>事件 ({@code OrderServiceImpl.createInternal} 里没有
 * {@code eventBus.publish}), 真正发事件的是 {@code applyTransition}, 一次转换一条 ——
 * submit / confirm / start_operation / deliver / settle / close 共 6 次转换 = <b>6 条</b>。
 * 「7 步」里多出来的那一步是建单, 它不产生事件。本 IT 按 6 断言。
 *
 * <p><b>第 3 条用例锁的是「字段级 jakarta 约束」</b> —— DTO 上的
 * {@code @Size} / {@code @PositiveOrZero} 在 service 单测里<b>不触发</b>
 * (service 直接调用不走校验器), 只能在 IT 层锁: 结算币种传 4 个字符
 * (列宽 {@code CHAR(3)}) → 必须 HTTP 400 + body.code = 4001 (PARAM_INVALID)。
 * 这条同时锁住三件事: jakarta 约束真的生效 / {@code @Valid} 级联真的生效 /
 * {@code GlobalExceptionHandler.handleValidation} 真的翻译成 400。
 */
@DisplayName("OrderLifecycle IT")
class OrderLifecycleIT extends OrderAdminFixtureBase {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED → IN_OPERATION → DELIVERED → SETTLED → CLOSED")
    void fullLifecycle_walksThroughSevenTransitions() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);

        // 建单 → DRAFT
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("DRAFT");
        assertThat(outboxCount(id)).as("建单不发状态变更事件").isZero();

        // DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM
        ResponseEntity<R> submit = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/submit", null, h);
        assertThat(submit.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("PENDING_CONFIRM");

        // PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED
        ResponseEntity<R> confirm = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/confirm", null, h);
        assertThat(confirm.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("CONFIRMED");

        // CONFIRMED → IN_OPERATION
        ResponseEntity<R> start = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/start-operation", null, h);
        assertThat(start.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("IN_OPERATION");

        // IN_OPERATION → DELIVERED
        ResponseEntity<R> deliver = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/deliver", null, h);
        assertThat(deliver.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("DELIVERED");

        // DELIVERED → SETTLED
        ResponseEntity<R> settle = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/settle", null, h);
        assertThat(settle.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("SETTLED");

        // SETTLED → CLOSED
        ResponseEntity<R> close = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/close",
                reasonBody("4.6 IT 关账"), h);
        assertThat(close.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("CLOSED");

        // 6 次转换 = 6 条 outbox 事件（建单不发事件）
        assertThat(outboxCount(id)).isEqualTo(6);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("列表按 status 过滤 + 详情返回 allowedNextStates")
    void list_filtersByStatus_andDetailReturnsAllowedNextStates() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));
        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/submit", null, h);

        ResponseEntity<R> list = adminGet("/api/v1/orders?current=1&size=10&status=PENDING_CONFIRM", h);
        assertThat(list.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(dataOf(list).get("records")).asInstanceOf(
                org.assertj.core.api.InstanceOfAssertFactories.LIST)
                .anyMatch(m -> String.valueOf(((Map<?, ?>) m).get("id")).equals(String.valueOf(id)));

        ResponseEntity<R> detail = adminGet("/api/v1/orders/" + id, h);
        assertThat(detail.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        Map<String, Object> data = dataOf(detail);
        assertThat(data.get("orderStatus")).isEqualTo("PENDING_CONFIRM");
        assertThat((List<?>) data.get("allowedNextStates")).contains("CONFIRMED");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("结算币种超长 (4 个字符) → 400 + code 4001 (PARAM_INVALID), 不落库")
    void overlongSettlementCurrency_returns400_paramInvalid() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);

        // t_order.settlement_currency 是 CHAR(3) —— 传 "USDD" 应被 jakarta 约束挡在 controller 口
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody("USDD"), h);

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value())
                .as("字段级约束应翻译成 HTTP 400, 而不是落库后 MySQL 1406 → 500")
                .isEqualTo(400);
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(resp.getBody().getCode())
                .as("GlobalExceptionHandler.handleValidation → PARAM_INVALID (4001)")
                .isEqualTo(4001);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `OrderStateMachineSeedIT` —— seed 落地 + 非法转换 409**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderStateMachineSeedIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — ORDER 状态机 seed 落地 + 非法转换 IT.
 *
 * <p>三件事:
 * <ol>
 *   <li>{@code t_state_transition} 里 {@code state_machine_code='ORDER'} 恰好 15 行,
 *       且 {@code tenant_id} 全是 0 (全局行 —— 填 1 会让其它租户看不到转换规则)。</li>
 *   <li>DRAFT 的出边恰好 3 条 (PENDING_CONFIRM / CANCELLED / VOID)。</li>
 *   <li>非法转换 (DRAFT → CONFIRMED) 返回 <b>409 + code 409_101</b>,
 *       <b>不是</b> StateMachineException 的 14001/400。</li>
 * </ol>
 */
@DisplayName("OrderStateMachineSeed IT")
class OrderStateMachineSeedIT extends OrderAdminFixtureBase {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("t_state_transition: ORDER 恰好 15 行, tenant_id 全是 0")
    void seed_hasFifteenGlobalRows() {
        Integer cnt = jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_state_transition WHERE state_machine_code = 'ORDER'", Integer.class);
        assertThat(cnt).isEqualTo(15);

        Integer nonGlobal = jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_state_transition WHERE state_machine_code = 'ORDER' AND tenant_id <> 0",
                Integer.class);
        assertThat(nonGlobal).as("状态机 seed 必须是 tenant_id=0 的全局行").isZero();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("DRAFT 的出边恰好 3 条")
    void draft_hasThreeOutgoingTransitions() {
        List<String> toStates = jdbc.queryForList(
                "SELECT to_state FROM t_state_transition WHERE state_machine_code = 'ORDER' AND from_state = 'DRAFT'",
                String.class);
        assertThat(toStates).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("PENDING_CONFIRM", "CANCELLED", "VOID");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("DRAFT → CONFIRMED 非法: 409 + code 409_101 (不是 400)")
    void illegalTransition_returns409_101() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));

        // DRAFT 直接 confirm —— 状态机不允许
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/confirm", null, h);

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value())
                .as("非法转换应 409 (StateMachineException 是 400, 已被 service 转成 409)")
                .isEqualTo(409);
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(resp.getBody().getCode()).isEqualTo(409_101);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("DRAFT");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `OrderNumberingIT` —— 编号口径**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderNumberingIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;

import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — 委托单编号口径 IT.
 *
 * <p>编号由 {@code sys_number_rule} 的 code='ORDER' 产出 (V4 seed:
 * prefix='ORD', date_format='yyyyMMdd', seq_length=6, reset_policy='DAY')。
 * 口径 = {@code ORD-yyyyMMdd-6位流水}, 例如 {@code ORD-20261110-000001}。
 *
 * <p>本 IT 锁 3 件事: 格式、唯一、库里落的就是这个号 (不是前端拼的)。
 */
@DisplayName("OrderNumbering IT")
class OrderNumberingIT extends OrderAdminFixtureBase {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("order_no 形如 ORD-yyyyMMdd-6位流水, 且两单不同")
    void orderNo_followsFormatAndIsUnique() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);

        Long first = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));
        Long second = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));

        String no1 = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT order_no FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, first);
        String no2 = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT order_no FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, second);

        assertThat(no1).matches("ORD-\\d{8}-\\d{6}");
        assertThat(no2).matches("ORD-\\d{8}-\\d{6}");
        assertThat(no1).isNotEqualTo(no2);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("建单响应里的 orderNo 与库里一致")
    void createResponse_orderNoMatchesDatabase() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);

        ResponseEntity<R> resp = adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h);
        Long id = orderIdOf(resp);
        Map<String, Object> data = dataOf(resp);

        String inDb = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT order_no FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, id);
        assertThat(data.get("orderNo")).isEqualTo(inDb);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `OrderBillingAccrualIT` —— 交付即计费**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderBillingAccrualIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/**
 * 4.6 — 委托单交付即计费 IT.
 *
 * <p>链路: 委托单走到 DELIVERED → 发 {@code OrderStateChangedEvent} →
 * finance 的 {@code BusinessEventListener} 反射读到 {@code toState=DELIVERED} →
 * 调 {@code BillingEngineService.accrue} → 写 {@code t_billing_accrual}
 * (business_type='ORDER', business_id=委托单 id)。
 *
 * <p><b>listener 是 {@code @Async("outboxExecutor")}</b> —— 所以断言必须 await,
 * 不能同步查完就断言 (那样会偶发红)。
 */
@DisplayName("OrderBillingAccrual IT")
class OrderBillingAccrualIT extends OrderAdminFixtureBase {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("DELIVERED → t_billing_accrual 出现 business_type=ORDER 的 OPEN 提单")
    void deliver_opensAccrual() {
        // business_type='ORDER' 的 ACTIVE 规则 —— 没有它 accrue 会走 markNoRule, 不写 accrual
        seedActiveOrderRule("weight * 2");

        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));

        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/submit", null, h);
        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/confirm", null, h);
        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/start-operation", null, h);
        ResponseEntity<R> deliver = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/deliver", null, h);
        assertThat(deliver.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).pollInterval(Duration.ofMillis(200)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbc.queryForList(
                    "SELECT id, business_type, business_id, gross_amount, status, counterparty_id "
                        + "FROM t_billing_accrual WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?",
                    id);
            assertThat(rows).as("交付后应产生一条 ORDER 提单").hasSize(1);
            Map<String, Object> row = rows.get(0);
            assertThat(row.get("status")).isEqualTo("OPEN");
            // 规则表达式 "weight * 2", 货物重量 100kg → gross 200
            assertThat(((Number) row.get("gross_amount")).doubleValue()).isEqualTo(200.0);
            assertThat(((Number) row.get("counterparty_id")).longValue()).isEqualTo(customerId);
        });
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("未到 DELIVERED (CONFIRMED) 不产生提单")
    void notDelivered_doesNotOpenAccrual() {
        seedActiveOrderRule("weight * 2");

        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));

        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/submit", null, h);
        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/confirm", null, h);

        // 给异步 listener 一点时间跑完 (确保它确实跑了, 而不是还没轮到)
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).pollInterval(Duration.ofMillis(200)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            Integer n = jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_billing_accrual WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?",
                    Integer.class, id);
            assertThat(n).isZero();
        });
    }
}
```

> **`await().untilAsserted()` 的用法**：断言失败会**重试**直到超时, 所以
> 「未到 DELIVERED 不产生提单」这条用例要**先 sleep 一小段**再断言 0, 否则异步
> listener 还没跑到, 断言 0 会立刻通过 (= 假绿, 没真正验到「listener 跑过但没提单」）。
> 上面那条用例的写法是**反的** —— 它会立刻通过。**改成下面这个写法**：
>
> ```java
>         // 等 listener 真的跑过 (outbox 行被消费) 再断言没有提单
>         await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).pollInterval(Duration.ofMillis(200)).until(() -> {
>             Integer dispatched = jdbc.queryForObject(
>                     "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_event_outbox WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER' AND aggregate_id = ?",
>                     Integer.class, String.valueOf(id));
>             return dispatched != null && dispatched >= 2;
>         });
>         Integer n = jdbc.queryForObject(
>                 "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_billing_accrual WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?",
>                 Integer.class, id);
>         assertThat(n).isZero();
> ```
>
> **实现时按这个版本写，不要按上面那个版本写。**

- [ ] **Step 6: `PortalFreightOrderIT` —— 门户 5 端点 + 跨客户 404**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/PortalFreightOrderIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.HttpEntity;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — 门户端货运委托单 IT.
 *
 * <p>5 个端点全走一遍: 建单 → 我的列表 → 详情 → 确认 → 取消,
 * 外加两条越权防线: 跨客户访问 404 / 空 reason 400。
 *
 * <p><b>不直接复用 {@link PortalOrderFixtureBase}</b> —— 那个基类是为了城配配送单
 * (wms_delivery_order) 设计的, 本 IT 用的是 t_order, 表和清理逻辑都不同。
 *
 * <p><b>租户</b>: 门户 JWT 的 tid=1, 但仍带 {@code X-Tenant-Id: 1}
 * (与 {@link PortalOrderFixtureBase} 一致, 少一个变量少一个坑)。
 */
@DisplayName("PortalFreightOrder IT")
class PortalFreightOrderIT extends IntegrationBase {

    /** Portal 客户角色 id = 14 (V33 seed)。 */
    private static final long CUSTOMER_ROLE_ID = 14L;
    private static final String ALICE = "alice_order_46";
    private static final String ALICE_PWD = "alice-order46-pwd";
    private static final String CAROL = "carol_order_46";
    private static final String CAROL_PWD = "carol-order46-pwd";

    private static final AtomicLong FIXTURE_ID = new AtomicLong(971_001L);

    @Autowired JdbcTemplate jdbc;
    @Autowired BCryptPasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;

    private long aliceId;
    private long carolId;
    private long customerAliceId;
    private long customerCarolId;
    private String aliceToken;
    private String carolToken;

    @BeforeEach
    void seedPortal() {
        aliceId = nextId();
        seedUser(aliceId, ALICE, ALICE_PWD);
        bindRole(aliceId);
        customerAliceId = nextId();
        seedCustomer(customerAliceId, "C046-ALICE", "4.6 门户 Alice 客户");

        carolId = nextId();
        seedUser(carolId, CAROL, CAROL_PWD);
        bindRole(carolId);
        customerCarolId = nextId();
        seedCustomer(customerCarolId, "C046-CAROL", "4.6 门户 Carol 客户");

        aliceToken = login(ALICE, ALICE_PWD);
        carolToken = login(CAROL, CAROL_PWD);
    }

    @AfterEach
    void cleanPortal() {
        // 每条语句**独立**兜底 —— 一条失败不能连累后面 7 条。
        // (旧写法把 8 条塞进同一个 try: 第 1 条一抛异常, 后面 7 条全被 catch 吞掉, 数据残留)
        //
        // t_order_cargo **没有 customer_id 列** —— 货物明细只认 order_id,
        // 所以必须走 t_order 的子查询, 不能照抄 t_order 的过滤条件。
        // 子查询也带 tenant_id —— 外层带了内层不带, 过滤口径自相矛盾。
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_order_cargo WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND order_id IN "
                + "(SELECT id FROM t_order WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND customer_id IN (?, ?))",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_order_service_item WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND order_id IN "
                + "(SELECT id FROM t_order WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND customer_id IN (?, ?))",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
        // outbox 按 aggregate_id 精确删 —— 不能写成 "aggregate_type='ORDER'" 一把梭:
        // 那会连带删掉本租户其它用例产生的 ORDER 事件, 且 t_event_outbox.tenant_id 允许 NULL
        // (跨租户事件留 NULL), 带 tenant_id=1 会漏删。
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_event_outbox WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER' AND aggregate_id IN "
                + "(SELECT CAST(id AS CHAR) FROM t_order WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND customer_id IN (?, ?))",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_order WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND customer_id IN (?, ?)",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_customer_user_link WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND user_id IN (?, ?)",
                aliceId, carolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM sys_user_role WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND user_id IN (?, ?)", aliceId, carolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM sys_user WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND id IN (?, ?)", aliceId, carolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM wms_customer WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND id IN (?, ?)",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
    }

    /** teardown 里的单条 SQL —— 失败只记日志, 不让测试因为清理失败而红。 */
    private void runQuietly(String sql, Object... args) {
        try {
            jdbc.update(sql, args);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("[teardown] 清理失败(已忽略): " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  用例
    // ================================================================

    @Test
    @DisplayName("建单 → 我的列表 → 详情: 归属正确")
    void createListDetail_works() {
        HttpHeaders h = portalHeaders(aliceToken);

        ResponseEntity<R> create = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order", createBody(), h);
        assertThat(create.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        Map<String, Object> created = dataOf(create);
        Long id = ((Number) created.get("id")).longValue();
        assertThat(created.get("orderStatus")).isEqualTo("DRAFT");
        assertThat(created.get("customerId")).isEqualTo((int) customerAliceId);

        // 我的列表 —— 只应有自己建的单
        ResponseEntity<R> list = getJson("/api/customer/freight-order?current=1&size=20", h);
        assertThat(list.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        List<Map<String, Object>> records = (List<Map<String, Object>>) dataOf(list).get("records");
        assertThat(records).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(((Number) records.get(0).get("id")).longValue()).isEqualTo(id);

        // 详情
        ResponseEntity<R> detail = getJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id, h);
        assertThat(detail.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        Map<String, Object> d = dataOf(detail);
        assertThat(d.get("orderNo")).isEqualTo(created.get("orderNo"));
        assertThat((List<?>) d.get("cargo")).hasSize(1);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("跨客户访问详情 → 404 (不泄露存在性)")
    void crossCustomerDetail_returns404() {
        HttpHeaders h = portalHeaders(aliceToken);
        ResponseEntity<R> create = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order", createBody(), h);
        Long id = ((Number) dataOf(create).get("id")).longValue();

        // Carol 访问 Alice 的单 —— 必须 404, 不是 403
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = getJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id, portalHeaders(carolToken));
        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(404);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("客户确认: PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED")
    void confirm_movesToConfirmed() {
        Long id = seedOrder(customerAliceId, "PENDING_CONFIRM");

        ResponseEntity<R> resp = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id + "/confirm", null,
                portalHeaders(aliceToken));

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("CONFIRMED");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("客户取消: PENDING_CONFIRM → CANCELLED")
    void cancel_movesToCancelled() {
        Long id = seedOrder(customerAliceId, "PENDING_CONFIRM");

        ResponseEntity<R> resp = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id + "/cancel",
                "{\"reason\":\"客户临时取消这批货\"}", portalHeaders(aliceToken));

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("CANCELLED");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("取消原因为空 → 400 (code 400_103)")
    void cancel_blankReason_returns400() {
        Long id = seedOrder(customerAliceId, "PENDING_CONFIRM");

        ResponseEntity<R> resp = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id + "/cancel",
                "{\"reason\":\"  \"}", portalHeaders(aliceToken));

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(400);
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(resp.getBody().getCode()).isEqualTo(400_103);
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  helpers
    // ================================================================

    private long nextId() {
        return FIXTURE_ID.incrementAndGet();
    }

    private void seedUser(long id, String username, String rawPassword) {
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO sys_user (id, tenant_id, username, password_hash, real_name, status, "
                + "version, deleted, created_at, updated_at) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, '4.6 portal', 1, 0, 0, NOW(), NOW())",
            id, username, passwordEncoder.encode(rawPassword), username);
    }

    private void bindRole(long userId) {
        long pk = Math.abs(System.nanoTime()) % 2_000_000_000L;
        try {
            jdbc.update(
                "INSERT INTO sys_user_role (id, user_id, role_id, tenant_id, created_at) "
                    + "VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1, NOW())", pk, userId, CUSTOMER_ROLE_ID);
        } catch (Exception dup) {
            // already bound
        }
    }

    private void seedCustomer(long id, String code, String name) {
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO wms_customer (id, tenant_id, code, name, deleted, version, created_at, updated_at) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, 0, 1, NOW(), NOW())", id, code, name);
    }

    /** 预置一条指定状态的委托单 (含 1 条货物 + 1 条服务项)。 */
    private Long seedOrder(long customerId, String status) {
        long id = nextId();
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_order (id, tenant_id, order_no, business_type, customer_id, trade_direction, "
                + " incoterms, service_type, order_status, settlement_currency, total_qty, total_weight_kg, "
                + " total_volume_cbm, is_dangerous, is_oversized, created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, 'OCEAN_FCL', ?, 'EXPORT', 'FOB', 'D2D', ?, 'CNY', 10, 100, 12.5, "
                + " 0, 0, NOW(), NOW(), 0, 1)",
            id, "ORD-IT-" + id, customerId, status);
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_order_cargo (id, tenant_id, order_id, cargo_name_cn, pkg_type, pkg_qty, unit, "
                + " weight_kg, volume_cbm, is_stackable, is_dangerous, created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, '钢材', 'CARTON', 10, 'CTN', 100, 12.5, 1, 0, NOW(), NOW(), 0, 1)",
            nextId(), id);
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_order_service_item (id, tenant_id, order_id, fee_type, fee_name, fee_category, "
                + " calc_basis, qty, unit_price, amount, currency_code, is_mandatory, source, "
                + " created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, 'OCEAN_FREIGHT', '海运费', 'FREIGHT', 'PER_CONTAINER', 1, 1000, 1000, "
                + " 'CNY', 1, 'MANUAL', NOW(), NOW(), 0, 1)",
            nextId(), id);
        return id;
    }

    private String statusOf(long orderId) {
        return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT order_status FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, orderId);
    }

    private String login(String username, String password) {
        HttpHeaders h = new HttpHeaders();
        h.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        h.set("X-Tenant-Id", "1");
        String body = "{\"username\":\"" + username + "\",\"password\":\"" + password + "\"}";
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = rest.exchange(
                "/api/auth/portal/login", HttpMethod.POST, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).as("portal login 应 200").isEqualTo(200);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> data = (Map<String, Object>) resp.getBody().getData();
        String token = (String) data.get("token");
        assertThat(token).isNotBlank();
        return token;
    }

    private HttpHeaders portalHeaders(String token) {
        HttpHeaders h = new HttpHeaders();
        h.setBearerAuth(token);
        h.set("X-Tenant-Id", "1");
        h.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        return h;
    }

    private ResponseEntity<R> postJson(String path, String body, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.POST, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
    }

    private ResponseEntity<R> getJson(String path, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.GET, new HttpEntity<>(h), R.class);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private Map<String, Object> dataOf(ResponseEntity<R> resp) {
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        return (Map<String, Object>) resp.getBody().getData();
    }

    private String createBody() {
        return "{"
                + "\"businessType\":\"OCEAN_FCL\","
                + "\"tradeDirection\":\"EXPORT\","
                + "\"incoterms\":\"FOB\","
                + "\"serviceType\":\"D2D\","
                + "\"originAddress\":\"上海洋山港\","
                + "\"destAddress\":\"洛杉矶港\","
                + "\"settlementCurrency\":\"CNY\","
                + "\"remark\":\"4.6 门户 IT\","
                + "\"cargo\":[{\"cargoNameCn\":\"钢材\",\"pkgType\":\"CARTON\",\"pkgQty\":10,"
                + "\"unit\":\"CTN\",\"weightKg\":100,\"volumeCbm\":12.5}],"
                + "\"serviceItems\":[{\"feeType\":\"OCEAN_FREIGHT\",\"feeName\":\"海运费\","
                + "\"calcBasis\":\"PER_CONTAINER\",\"qty\":1,\"unitPrice\":1000,\"amount\":1000}]"
                + "}";
    }
}
```
