- [ ] **Step 1: 建 service（13 个端点）**

Create `lumen-admin-web/src/services/order/orderService.ts`：

```ts
/**
 * Lumen Admin — 货运委托单 service (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * 后端: `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/controller/OrderController.java`
 *       路径前缀 `/api/v1/orders`, 13 个端点。
 *
 * <p><b>路径一律用相对形式 `/orders`</b> —— `services/request.ts` 的 axios baseURL
 * 已经是 `/api/v1`。
 *
 * <p><b>为什么不是 `/api/v1/orders`</b>: 仓里既有 **30 个** service 源文件（**非测试**）
 * 写的是绝对 `/api/v1/xxx`, 共 **166 处**调用（cs 4 文件/16 处、dispatch 8/43、
 * finance 9/45、wms 9/62；口径 = 去掉注释后匹配
 * `request.(get|post|put|delete)(...)(\`|')/api/v1`, 单引号与反引号都算）,
 * axios 会拼成 `/api/v1/api/v1/xxx`（已用
 * `axios.create({baseURL:'/api/v1'}).getUri({url:'/api/v1/x'})` 实测确认）。
 * 那是**既有缺陷**, 不在本轮修复范围 —— Task 11 会把它写进文档订正清单。
 * 新代码不复制这个 bug。
 */

import { request } from '@/services/request';
import type { ApiEnvelope, PageResult } from '@/services/request';

/**
 * `asData` 住在 `services/dispatch/_util.ts` (Iteration 4.1)。
 *
 * 它是**通用**的 axios 拦截器适配器 (1 行), 不是 dispatch 专属 ——
 * 这里**复用**它, **不新增第 4 份副本**。把三份副本合并到 `services/_util.ts`
 * 要改 21 个既有 service 源文件 (cs 4 + dispatch 8 + finance 9), 加上本文件共 22 个,
 * 超出本轮范围。
 *
 * <p>仓里现存的 `asData` util 副本共 **3 份**: `services/dispatch/_util.ts` /
 * `services/cs/_util.ts` / `services/finance/_util.ts` —— 本 Task 是第 3 份的**复用方**,
 * 不是第 4 份。
 */
import { asData } from '@/services/dispatch/_util';

/** 后端 `R<T>` 信封的本地别名。 */
type R<T> = ApiEnvelope<T>;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Types — mirror backend dto/OrderView.java / OrderDetailView.java
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** 货物明细行 —— 对应 `CargoView`。 */
export interface OrderCargoItem {
  id: number;
  cargoNameCn?: string | null;
  cargoNameEn?: string | null;
  hsCode?: string | null;
  pkgType?: string | null;
  pkgQty?: number | null;
  unit?: string | null;
  weightKg?: number | null;
  netWeightKg?: number | null;
  volumeCbm?: number | null;
  lengthCm?: number | null;
  widthCm?: number | null;
  heightCm?: number | null;
  isStackable?: number | null;
  cargoValue?: number | null;
  currencyCode?: string | null;
  brandName?: string | null;
  model?: string | null;
  isDangerous?: number | null;
  unNo?: string | null;
  dangerClass?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
}

/** 服务项行 —— 对应 `ServiceItemView`。 */
export interface OrderServiceItem {
  id: number;
  feeType?: string | null;
  feeName?: string | null;
  feeCategory?: string | null;
  calcBasis?: string | null;
  qty?: number | null;
  unitPrice?: number | null;
  amount?: number | null;
  currencyCode?: string | null;
  supplierId?: number | null;
  costAmount?: number | null;
  isMandatory?: number | null;
  source?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
}

/** 列表行 —— 对应 `OrderView`。 */
export interface OrderRow {
  id: number;
  orderNo: string;
  customerId: number;
  businessType?: string | null;
  tradeDirection?: string | null;
  incoterms?: string | null;
  serviceType?: string | null;
  originAddress?: string | null;
  destAddress?: string | null;
  totalQty?: number | null;
  totalWeightKg?: number | null;
  totalVolumeCbm?: number | null;
  orderStatus: string;
  settlementCurrency?: string | null;
  etd?: string | null;
  eta?: string | null;
  createdAt?: string | null;
}

/** 详情 —— 对应 `OrderDetailView` (= OrderRow + 明细 + 允许的下一状态)。 */
export interface OrderDetail extends OrderRow {
  cargoReadyDate?: string | null;
  containerQty?: number | null;
  containerTypeCode?: string | null;
  marksAndNos?: string | null;
  cargoDescription?: string | null;
  isDangerous?: number | null;
  isOversized?: number | null;
  temperatureRange?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
  cancelReason?: string | null;
  closeReason?: string | null;
  voidReason?: string | null;
  submittedAt?: string | null;
  confirmedAt?: string | null;
  deliveredAt?: string | null;
  settledAt?: string | null;
  closedAt?: string | null;
  createdBy?: number | null;
  updatedBy?: number | null;
  cargo: OrderCargoItem[];
  serviceItems: OrderServiceItem[];
  /** 状态机允许的下一状态 —— 前端据此渲染动作按钮。 */
  allowedNextStates: string[];
}

/** 列表查询参数 —— 与后端 `OrderController.page` 的 query 参数一一对应。 */
export interface OrderPageQuery {
  current?: number;
  size?: number;
  status?: string;
  customerId?: number;
  orderNo?: string;
  businessType?: string;
}

/** 建单 / 编辑的 body —— 与后端 `CreateOrderRequest` 一一对应。 */
export interface CreateOrderPayload {
  customerId: number;
  businessType: string;
  tradeDirection: string;
  incoterms: string;
  serviceType: string;
  originAddress?: string | null;
  destAddress?: string | null;
  cargoReadyDate?: string | null;
  etd?: string | null;
  eta?: string | null;
  containerQty?: number | null;
  containerTypeCode?: string | null;
  marksAndNos?: string | null;
  cargoDescription?: string | null;
  isOversized?: boolean | null;
  temperatureRange?: string | null;
  settlementCurrency?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
  cargo: Array<Partial<OrderCargoItem>>;
  serviceItems: Array<Partial<OrderServiceItem>>;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Service
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const orderService = {
  /** 1. 分页列表。 */
  async page(query: OrderPageQuery): Promise<PageResult<OrderRow>> {
    return asData<PageResult<OrderRow>>(
      request.get<R<PageResult<OrderRow>>>('/orders', { params: query }),
    );
  },

  /** 2. 详情。 */
  async get(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.get<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}`));
  },

  /** 3. 建单 (落 DRAFT)。 */
  async create(payload: CreateOrderPayload): Promise<OrderRow> {
    return asData<OrderRow>(request.post<R<OrderRow>>('/orders', payload));
  },

  /** 4. 编辑 (仅 DRAFT / PENDING_CONFIRM)。 */
  async update(id: number, payload: CreateOrderPayload): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.put<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}`, payload));
  },

  /** 5. 提交 (DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM)。 */
  async submit(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/submit`));
  },

  /** 6. 确认 (PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED)。 */
  async confirm(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/confirm`));
  },

  /** 7. 退回 (PENDING_CONFIRM → DRAFT), 必须带原因。 */
  async returnToDraft(id: number, reason: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/return`, { reason }),
    );
  },

  /** 8. 取消, 必须带原因。 */
  async cancel(id: number, reason: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/cancel`, { reason }),
    );
  },

  /** 9. 作废, 必须带原因。 */
  async voidOrder(id: number, reason: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/void`, { reason }),
    );
  },

  /** 10. 开始操作 (CONFIRMED → IN_OPERATION)。 */
  async startOperation(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/start-operation`),
    );
  },

  /** 11. 交付 (IN_OPERATION → DELIVERED)。 */
  async deliver(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/deliver`));
  },

  /** 12. 结算 (DELIVERED → SETTLED)。 */
  async settle(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/settle`));
  },

  /** 13. 关闭 (SETTLED → CLOSED), 必须带原因。 */
  async close(id: number, reason: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/close`, { reason }),
    );
  },
};
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 建状态标签组件（不复用通用 StatusTag）**

Create `lumen-admin-web/src/components/order/OrderStatusTag.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 货运委托单状态标签 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>不复用 `components/columns/StatusTag.tsx`</b> —— 那个通用组件里
 * `CLOSED` 已经映射成「已关闭」(配送异常工单), 而委托单的 `CLOSED` 是
 * 「已归档」; `DELIVERED` 在城配是「已签收」, 在委托单是「已交付」。
 * 语义不同, 复用会显示错标签。
 */

import { Tag } from 'antd';

interface Props {
  value?: string | null;
}

const COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  DRAFT: 'default',
  PENDING_CONFIRM: 'gold',
  CONFIRMED: 'blue',
  IN_OPERATION: 'processing',
  DELIVERED: 'cyan',
  SETTLED: 'purple',
  CLOSED: 'default',
  CANCELLED: 'default',
  VOID: 'red',
};

const LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  DRAFT: '草稿',
  PENDING_CONFIRM: '待确认',
  CONFIRMED: '已确认',
  IN_OPERATION: '操作中',
  DELIVERED: '已交付',
  SETTLED: '已结算',
  CLOSED: '已归档',
  CANCELLED: '已取消',
  VOID: '已作废',
};

export function OrderStatusTag({ value }: Props) {
  const key = (value ?? '').toUpperCase();
  return <Tag color={COLORS[key] ?? 'default'}>{LABELS[key] ?? key ?? '未知'}</Tag>;
}

export default OrderStatusTag;
```

```tsx
/**
 * Lumen Admin — 货运委托单列表 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * 筛选项: 委托单号 / 状态 / 业务类型 / 客户 ID。点击行进入详情。
 */

import type { ActionType, ProColumns } from '@ant-design/pro-components';
import { ProTable } from '@ant-design/pro-components';
import { App as AntApp, Button } from 'antd';
import { useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

import { DateTimeColumn } from '@/components/columns/DateTimeColumn';
import { OrderStatusTag } from '@/components/order/OrderStatusTag';
import { useAccess } from '@/hooks/useAccess';
import { orderService } from '@/services/order/orderService';
import type { OrderRow } from '@/services/order/orderService';

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'DRAFT', label: '草稿' },
  { value: 'PENDING_CONFIRM', label: '待确认' },
  { value: 'CONFIRMED', label: '已确认' },
  { value: 'IN_OPERATION', label: '操作中' },
  { value: 'DELIVERED', label: '已交付' },
  { value: 'SETTLED', label: '已结算' },
  { value: 'CLOSED', label: '已归档' },
  { value: 'CANCELLED', label: '已取消' },
  { value: 'VOID', label: '已作废' },
];

const BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'OCEAN_FCL', label: '海运整箱 (OCEAN_FCL)' },
  { value: 'OCEAN_LCL', label: '海运拼箱 (OCEAN_LCL)' },
  { value: 'AIR', label: '空运 (AIR)' },
  { value: 'RAIL', label: '铁路 (RAIL)' },
  { value: 'TRUCK', label: '陆运 (TRUCK)' },
  { value: 'EXPRESS', label: '快递 (EXPRESS)' },
  { value: 'MULTIMODAL', label: '多式联运 (MULTIMODAL)' },
  { value: 'WAREHOUSE', label: '仓储 (WAREHOUSE)' },
];

export default function OrderList() {
  const actionRef = useRef<ActionType>();
  const nav = useNavigate();
  const { message: antMessage } = AntApp.useApp();
  const access = useAccess();

  const columns: ProColumns<OrderRow>[] = [
    { title: '委托单号', dataIndex: 'orderNo', width: 180 },
    { title: '客户 ID', dataIndex: 'customerId', width: 100, hideInSearch: true },
    {
      title: '业务类型',
      dataIndex: 'businessType',
      width: 120,
      valueType: 'select',
      fieldProps: { options: BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS },
    },
    { title: '贸易方向', dataIndex: 'tradeDirection', width: 100, hideInSearch: true },
    { title: '贸易条款', dataIndex: 'incoterms', width: 100, hideInSearch: true },
    { title: '服务类型', dataIndex: 'serviceType', width: 100, hideInSearch: true },
    { title: '起运地', dataIndex: 'originAddress', width: 160, ellipsis: true },
    { title: '目的地', dataIndex: 'destAddress', width: 160, ellipsis: true },
    {
      title: '重量 (kg)',
      dataIndex: 'totalWeightKg',
      width: 100,
      hideInSearch: true,
      render: (_, r) => r.totalWeightKg ?? '—',
    },
    {
      title: '体积 (m³)',
      dataIndex: 'totalVolumeCbm',
      width: 100,
      hideInSearch: true,
      render: (_, r) => r.totalVolumeCbm ?? '—',
    },
    {
      title: '状态',
      dataIndex: 'orderStatus',
      width: 110,
      valueType: 'select',
      fieldProps: { options: STATUS_OPTIONS },
      render: (_, r) => <OrderStatusTag value={r.orderStatus} />,
    },
    {
      title: 'ETD',
      dataIndex: 'etd',
      width: 110,
      hideInSearch: true,
    },
    {
      title: 'ETA',
      dataIndex: 'eta',
      width: 110,
      hideInSearch: true,
    },
    {
      title: '创建时间',
      dataIndex: 'createdAt',
      width: 170,
      hideInSearch: true,
      render: (_, r) => <DateTimeColumn value={r.createdAt} />,
    },
    {
      title: '操作',
      valueType: 'option',
      width: 140,
      render: (_, record) => [
        <Button
          key="detail"
          type="link"
          size="small"
          disabled={!access.canRead('order:view')}
          onClick={() => nav(`/orders/${record.id}`)}
        >
          详情
        </Button>,
      ],
    },
  ];

  return (
    <ProTable<OrderRow>
      headerTitle="货运委托单"
      actionRef={actionRef}
      rowKey="id"
      columns={columns}
      search={{ labelWidth: 'auto' }}
      request={async (params) => {
        try {
          const res = await orderService.page({
            current: params.current,
            size: params.pageSize,
            orderNo: params.orderNo as string | undefined,
            status: params.orderStatus as string | undefined,
            businessType: params.businessType as string | undefined,
            customerId: params.customerId as number | undefined,
          });
          return { data: res.records, total: res.total, success: true };
        } catch {
          antMessage.error('委托单列表加载失败');
          return { data: [], total: 0, success: false };
        }
      }}
      pagination={{ defaultPageSize: 20, showSizeChanger: true }}
      toolBarRender={() => [
        <Button
          key="add"
          type="primary"
          disabled={!access.canRead('order:create')}
          onClick={() => nav('/orders/new')}
        >
          新建委托单
        </Button>,
      ]    />
  );
}
```

> **`DateTimeColumn` 的入参**是 `string | null | undefined` —— 传 `r.createdAt` 即可。

- [ ] **Step 4: 详情页（动作按钮由 `allowedNextStates` 驱动）**

Create `lumen-admin-web/src/pages/Order/Detail.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * Lumen Admin — 货运委托单详情 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>动作按钮由后端返回的 `allowedNextStates` 驱动</b> —— 前端不自己判断
 * 「当前状态能不能做什么」, 而是让状态机 (`t_state_transition`) 单点决定。
 * 9 态状态机里每个目标状态的入边唯一, 所以「目标状态 → 动作」是一一对应的。
 */

import {
  App as AntApp,
  Button,
  Card,
  Col,
  Descriptions,
  Input,
  Modal,
  Row,
  Spin,
  Table,
  Tag,
} from 'antd';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

import { OrderStatusTag } from '@/components/order/OrderStatusTag';
import { useAccess } from '@/hooks/useAccess';
import { orderService } from '@/services/order/orderService';
import type {
  OrderCargoItem,
  OrderDetail,
  OrderServiceItem,
} from '@/services/order/orderService';

const { TextArea } = Input;

/** 目标状态 → 动作。9 态状态机里每个目标状态的入边唯一, 所以这个映射是无歧义的。 */
const ACTIONS: Array<{
  to: string;
  label: string;
  perm: string;
  needReason?: boolean;
  run: (id: number, reason?: string) => Promise<OrderDetail>;
}> = [
  { to: 'PENDING_CONFIRM', label: '提交', perm: 'order:submit', run: (id) => orderService.submit(id) },
  { to: 'CONFIRMED', label: '确认', perm: 'order:confirm', run: (id) => orderService.confirm(id) },
  { to: 'DRAFT', label: '退回', perm: 'order:submit', needReason: true, run: (id, r) => orderService.returnToDraft(id, r!) },
  { to: 'IN_OPERATION', label: '开始操作', perm: 'order:operate', run: (id) => orderService.startOperation(id) },
  { to: 'DELIVERED', label: '交付', perm: 'order:operate', run: (id) => orderService.deliver(id) },
  { to: 'SETTLED', label: '结算', perm: 'order:settle', run: (id) => orderService.settle(id) },
  { to: 'CLOSED', label: '关闭归档', perm: 'order:close', needReason: true, run: (id, r) => orderService.close(id, r!) },
  { to: 'CANCELLED', label: '取消', perm: 'order:cancel', needReason: true, run: (id, r) => orderService.cancel(id, r!) },
  { to: 'VOID', label: '作废', perm: 'order:void', needReason: true, run: (id, r) => orderService.voidOrder(id, r!) },
];

/** 原因最少 5 个字 —— 与后端 `OrderConstants.REASON_MIN_LENGTH` 一致。 */
const REASON_MIN = 5;
/** 原因最多 255 个字 —— 与后端 `OrderConstants.REASON_MAX_LENGTH` 一致
 *  (V42 的 cancel_reason / close_reason / void_reason 三列都是 VARCHAR(255);
 *   超长后端抛 REASON_TOO_LONG 400_107, 前端先挡住, 免得用户填了半天气泡)。 */
const REASON_MAX = 255;

export default function OrderDetailPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const orderId = Number(id);
  const nav = useNavigate();
  const { message: antMessage } = AntApp.useApp();
  const access = useAccess();

  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<OrderDetail | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ label: string; needReason?: boolean; run: (id: number, reason?: string) => Promise<OrderDetail> } | null>(null);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const [acting, setActing] = useState(false);

  const load = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setDetail(await orderService.get(orderId));
    } catch {
      antMessage.error('委托单详情加载失败');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [orderId]);

  const submitReason = async () => {
    if (!pending) return;
    if (pending.needReason && reason.trim().length < REASON_MIN) {
      antMessage.error(`原因至少 ${REASON_MIN} 个字`);
      return;
    }
    if (pending.needReason && reason.trim().length > REASON_MAX) {
      antMessage.error(`原因最多 ${REASON_MAX} 个字`);
      return;
    }
    setActing(true);
    try {
      await pending.run(orderId, reason.trim());
      antMessage.success(`${pending.label}成功`);
      setPending(null);
      setReason('');
      await load();
    } catch {
      // 后端错误已由 request.ts 的全局通知弹出, 这里不重复提示。
    } finally {
      setActing(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <Spin />;
  }
  if (!detail) {
    return <Card>委托单不存在</Card>;
  }

  const actions = ACTIONS.filter(
    (a) => detail.allowedNextStates.includes(a.to) && access.canRead(a.perm),
  );

  return (
    <Card
      title={
        <span>
          {detail.orderNo} <OrderStatusTag value={detail.orderStatus} />
        </span>
      }
      extra={
        <span>
          {actions.map((a) => (
            <Button
              key={a.to}
              type={a.to === 'VOID' ? 'default' : 'primary'}
              danger={a.to === 'VOID'}
              style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}
              onClick={() => {
                setPending(a);
                setReason('');
              }}
            >
              {a.label}
            </Button>
          ))}
          <Button style={{ marginLeft: 8 }} onClick={() => nav('/orders')}>
            返回列表
          </Button>
        </span>
      }
    >
      <Descriptions bordered column={3} size="small">
        <Descriptions.Item label="客户 ID">{detail.customerId}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="业务类型">{detail.businessType ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="贸易方向">{detail.tradeDirection ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="贸易条款">{detail.incoterms ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="服务类型">{detail.serviceType ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="结算币种">{detail.settlementCurrency ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="起运地">{detail.originAddress ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="目的地">{detail.destAddress ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="ETD / ETA">{`${detail.etd ?? '—'} / ${detail.eta ?? '—'}`}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="重量 (kg)">{detail.totalWeightKg ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="体积 (m³)">{detail.totalVolumeCbm ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="件数">{detail.totalQty ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="箱量">{detail.containerQty ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="箱型">{detail.containerTypeCode ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="唛头">{detail.marksAndNos ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="货物描述" span={3}>
          {detail.cargoDescription ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="超限">
          {detail.isOversized ? <Tag color="orange">是</Tag> : '否'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="温区">{detail.temperatureRange ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="备注">{detail.remark ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
      </Descriptions>

      <Card size="small" title="货物明细" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
        <Table<OrderCargoItem>
          rowKey="id"
          size="small"
          pagination={false}
          dataSource={detail.cargo}
          columns={[
            { title: '品名 (中)', dataIndex: 'cargoNameCn' },
            { title: '品名 (英)', dataIndex: 'cargoNameEn' },
            { title: 'HS 编码', dataIndex: 'hsCode' },
            { title: '件数', dataIndex: 'pkgQty' },
            { title: '重量 (kg)', dataIndex: 'weightKg' },
            { title: '体积 (m³)', dataIndex: 'volumeCbm' },
            { title: '品牌', dataIndex: 'brandName' },
          ]}
        />
      </Card>

      <Card size="small" title="服务项 / 费用" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
        <Table<OrderServiceItem>
          rowKey="id"
          size="small"
          pagination={false}
          dataSource={detail.serviceItems}
          columns={[
            { title: '费用类型', dataIndex: 'feeType' },
            { title: '费用名称', dataIndex: 'feeName' },
            { title: '计费基准', dataIndex: 'calcBasis' },
            { title: '数量', dataIndex: 'qty' },
            { title: '单价', dataIndex: 'unitPrice' },
            { title: '金额', dataIndex: 'amount' },
            { title: '币种', dataIndex: 'currencyCode' },
            { title: '我方成本', dataIndex: 'costAmount' },
          ]}
        />
      </Card>

      <Modal
        open={pending != null}
        title={`${pending?.label ?? ''} — 请填写原因`}
        onCancel={() => setPending(null)}
        onOk={submitReason}
        confirmLoading={acting}
        okText="确认"
        cancelText="取消"
      >
        <TextArea
          rows={4}
          value={reason}
          maxLength={REASON_MAX}
          onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={`至少 ${REASON_MIN} 个字`}
        />
      </Modal>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 建单 / 编辑表单**

Create `lumen-admin-web/src/pages/Order/Form.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * Lumen Admin — 货运委托单建单 / 编辑表单 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>有 `:id` 路由参数 → 编辑模式 (先 GET 详情回填, 提交走 PUT);
 * 无参数 → 新建模式 (提交走 POST, 建出来是 DRAFT)。
 */

import {
  App as AntApp,
  Button,
  Card,
  DatePicker,
  Form,
  Input,
  InputNumber,
  Select,
  Space,
  Spin,
} from 'antd';
import dayjs, { Dayjs } from 'dayjs';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

import { orderService } from '@/services/order/orderService';
import type { CreateOrderPayload } from '@/services/order/orderService';

const { TextArea } = Input;

interface FormValues {
  customerId: number;
  businessType: string;
  tradeDirection: string;
  incoterms: string;
  serviceType: string;
  originAddress?: string;
  destAddress?: string;
  cargoReadyDate?: Dayjs;
  etd?: Dayjs;
  eta?: Dayjs;
  containerQty?: number;
  containerTypeCode?: string;
  marksAndNos?: string;
  cargoDescription?: string;
  isOversized?: boolean;
  temperatureRange?: string;
  settlementCurrency?: string;
  remark?: string;
  cargo?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  serviceItems?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
}

/**
 * 四个受控下拉的取值必须与后端 `com.lumen.order.OrderConstants` 的受控词表一致 ——
 * Task 3 起 service 层校验受控词表 (`requireEnums`), 下拉里出现词表外的值
 * → HTTP 400 ENUM_VALUE_INVALID (400_106), 而用户根本不知道自己错在哪。
 *
 * - `BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS` **= `OrderConstants.BUSINESS_TYPES`** ✓ (8 个, 全集)
 * - `TRADE_DIRECTION_OPTIONS` **= `OrderConstants.TRADE_DIRECTIONS`** ✓ (4 个, 全集)
 * - `INCOTERM_OPTIONS` **= `OrderConstants.INCOTERMS`** ✓ (10 个, 全集)
 * - `SERVICE_TYPE_OPTIONS` **= `OrderConstants.SERVICE_TYPES`** ✓ (D2D / P2P / D2P / P2D)
 *
 * <p>⚠️ **实施期主动偏离（2026-11-11 回填）** —— 本计划原先给的是**子集**
 * (业务类型 5/8、贸易方向 2/4、贸易条款 6/10), 实施代理按**全集**实现并已落地:
 * 后端支持、下拉里选不到的值 = 客户只能提工单让客服代下单。
 * 标签一律写成「中文 (CODE)」—— CODE 就是提交给后端的那个值, 中英文都看得懂。
 */
const BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'OCEAN_FCL', label: '海运整箱 (OCEAN_FCL)' },
  { value: 'OCEAN_LCL', label: '海运拼箱 (OCEAN_LCL)' },
  { value: 'AIR', label: '空运 (AIR)' },
  { value: 'RAIL', label: '铁路 (RAIL)' },
  { value: 'TRUCK', label: '陆运 (TRUCK)' },
  { value: 'EXPRESS', label: '快递 (EXPRESS)' },
  { value: 'MULTIMODAL', label: '多式联运 (MULTIMODAL)' },
  { value: 'WAREHOUSE', label: '仓储 (WAREHOUSE)' },
];

const TRADE_DIRECTION_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'EXPORT', label: '出口 (EXPORT)' },
  { value: 'IMPORT', label: '进口 (IMPORT)' },
  { value: 'TRANSIT', label: '过境 (TRANSIT)' },
  { value: 'TRANSIT_OVERLAND', label: '过境陆运 (TRANSIT_OVERLAND)' },
];

const INCOTERM_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'EXW', label: '工厂交货 (EXW)' },
  { value: 'FCA', label: '货交承运人 (FCA)' },
  { value: 'FOB', label: '装运港船上交货 (FOB)' },
  { value: 'CFR', label: '成本加运费 (CFR)' },
  { value: 'CIF', label: '成本、保险费加运费 (CIF)' },
  { value: 'CPT', label: '运费付至 (CPT)' },
  { value: 'CIP', label: '运费和保险费付至 (CIP)' },
  { value: 'DAP', label: '目的地交货 (DAP)' },
  { value: 'DPU', label: '目的地卸货交货 (DPU)' },
  { value: 'DDP', label: '完税后交货 (DDP)' },
];

/**
 * 服务类型 = `OrderConstants.SERVICE_TYPES` (D2D / P2P / D2P / P2D), **一个不多一个不少**。
 *
 * <p>⚠️ 原来这里写的是 `D2D / DDP / D2P` —— `DDP` 是**贸易条款 (Incoterm)**, 不是服务类型,
 * 不在 `SERVICE_TYPES` 里 → 用户选「完税交货」提交必被后端 400_106 打回。
 * 同时 `P2P` / `P2D` 两个合法值漏了。
 */
const SERVICE_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'D2D', label: '门到门 (D2D)' },
  { value: 'P2P', label: '港到港 (P2P)' },
  { value: 'D2P', label: '门到港 (D2P)' },
  { value: 'P2D', label: '港到门 (P2D)' },
];

/** 费用类型 = `OrderConstants.FEE_TYPES` (`feeType` 是 `@NotBlank`, 必填)。 */
const FEE_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'OCEAN_FREIGHT', label: '海运费' },
  { value: 'AIR_FREIGHT', label: '空运费' },
  { value: 'BAF', label: '燃油附加费' },
  { value: 'THC', label: '港口操作费' },
  { value: 'DOC_FEE', label: '单证费' },
  { value: 'CUSTOMS', label: '清关费' },
  { value: 'DELIVERY', label: '派送费' },
  { value: 'OTHER', label: '其他' },
];

/** 计费基准 = `OrderConstants.CALC_BASES` (`calcBasis` 是 `@NotBlank`, 必填)。 */
const CALC_BASIS_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'PER_SHIPMENT', label: '按票' },
  { value: 'PER_CONTAINER', label: '按箱' },
  { value: 'PER_BOX', label: '按件' },
  { value: 'PER_KG', label: '按公斤' },
  { value: 'PER_CBM', label: '按方' },
  { value: 'PER_CARGO', label: '按货物' },
];

/** 费用大类 = `OrderConstants.FEE_CATEGORIES` (可空)。 */
const FEE_CATEGORY_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'FREIGHT', label: '运费' },
  { value: 'SURCHARGE', label: '附加费' },
  { value: 'CUSTOMS', label: '清关' },
  { value: 'DELIVERY', label: '派送' },
  { value: 'WAREHOUSE', label: '仓储' },
  { value: 'OTHER', label: '其他' },
];

export default function OrderForm() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const editing = id != null;
  const orderId = editing ? Number(id) : null;
  const nav = useNavigate();
  const { message: antMessage } = AntApp.useApp();
  const [form] = Form.useForm<FormValues>();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(editing);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editing || orderId == null) return;
    void (async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const d = await orderService.get(orderId);
        form.setFieldsValue({
          customerId: d.customerId,
          businessType: d.businessType ?? undefined,
          tradeDirection: d.tradeDirection ?? undefined,
          incoterms: d.incoterms ?? undefined,
          serviceType: d.serviceType ?? undefined,
          originAddress: d.originAddress ?? undefined,
          destAddress: d.destAddress ?? undefined,
          cargoReadyDate: d.cargoReadyDate ? dayjs(d.cargoReadyDate) : undefined,
          etd: d.etd ? dayjs(d.etd) : undefined,
          eta: d.eta ? dayjs(d.eta) : undefined,
          containerQty: d.containerQty ?? undefined,
          containerTypeCode: d.containerTypeCode ?? undefined,
          marksAndNos: d.marksAndNos ?? undefined,
          cargoDescription: d.cargoDescription ?? undefined,
          isOversized: d.isOversized === 1,
          temperatureRange: d.temperatureRange ?? undefined,
          settlementCurrency: d.settlementCurrency ?? undefined,
          remark: d.remark ?? undefined,
          cargo: d.cargo.map((c) => ({ ...c })),
          serviceItems: d.serviceItems.map((s) => ({ ...s })),
        });
      } catch {
        antMessage.error('委托单详情加载失败');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [editing, orderId, form, antMessage]);

  const onSubmit = async () => {
    let values: FormValues;
    try {
      values = await form.validateFields();
    } catch {
      return; // antd 已就地提示
    }
    const payload: CreateOrderPayload = {
      customerId: values.customerId,
      businessType: values.businessType,
      tradeDirection: values.tradeDirection,
      incoterms: values.incoterms,
      serviceType: values.serviceType,
      originAddress: values.originAddress ?? null,
      destAddress: values.destAddress ?? null,
      cargoReadyDate: values.cargoReadyDate ? values.cargoReadyDate.format('YYYY-MM-DD') : null,
      etd: values.etd ? values.etd.format('YYYY-MM-DD') : null,
      eta: values.eta ? values.eta.format('YYYY-MM-DD') : null,
      containerQty: values.containerQty ?? null,
      containerTypeCode: values.containerTypeCode ?? null,
      marksAndNos: values.marksAndNos ?? null,
      cargoDescription: values.cargoDescription ?? null,
      isOversized: values.isOversized ?? null,
      temperatureRange: values.temperatureRange ?? null,
      settlementCurrency: values.settlementCurrency ?? null,
      remark: values.remark ?? null,
      cargo: (values.cargo ?? []).map((c) => {
        const { id: _id, ...rest } = c as Record<string, unknown> & { id?: number };
        void _id;
        return rest;
      }),
      serviceItems: (values.serviceItems ?? []).map((s) => {
        const { id: _id, ...rest } = s as Record<string, unknown> & { id?: number };
        void _id;
        return rest;
      }),
    };
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (editing && orderId != null) {
        await orderService.update(orderId, payload);
        antMessage.success('委托单已保存');
        nav(`/orders/${orderId}`);
      } else {
        const created = await orderService.create(payload);
        antMessage.success('委托单已创建');
        nav(`/orders/${created.id}`);
      }
    } catch {
      // request.ts 已弹通知。
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <Spin />;
  }

  return (
    <Card title={editing ? '编辑委托单' : '新建委托单'}>
      <Form<FormValues> form={form} layout="vertical">
        <Form.Item
          name="customerId"
          label="客户 ID"
          rules={[{ required: true, message: '请填写客户 ID' }]}
        >
          <InputNumber style={{ width: 220 }} min={1} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="businessType" label="业务类型" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
          <Select options={BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="tradeDirection" label="贸易方向" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
          <Select options={TRADE_DIRECTION_OPTIONS} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="incoterms" label="贸易条款" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
          <Select options={INCOTERM_OPTIONS} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="serviceType" label="服务类型" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
          <Select options={SERVICE_TYPE_OPTIONS} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="originAddress" label="起运地">
          <Input maxLength={255} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="destAddress" label="目的地">
          <Input maxLength={255} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="cargoReadyDate" label="货好日期">
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="etd" label="ETD">
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="eta" label="ETA">
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="containerQty" label="箱量">
          <InputNumber min={0} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="containerTypeCode" label="箱型">
          <Input maxLength={16} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="marksAndNos" label="唛头">
          <Input maxLength={500} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="cargoDescription" label="货物描述">
          <TextArea rows={2} maxLength={500} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="isOversized" label="超限">
          <Select
            allowClear
            options={[
              { value: true, label: '是' },
              { value: false, label: '否' },
            ]}
          />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="temperatureRange" label="温区">
          <Input maxLength={32} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="settlementCurrency" label="结算币种">
          {/* CHAR(3) —— maxLength 必须跟着列宽走, 放行 4~8 个字符只会让用户白填一半再吃一个 400 */}
          <Input maxLength={3} placeholder="CNY" />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="remark" label="备注">
          <TextArea rows={3} maxLength={1000} />
        </Form.Item>

        <Form.List name="cargo">
          {(fields, { add, remove }) => (
            <Card size="small" title="货物明细" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              {fields.map((field) => (
                <Space key={field.key} wrap align="start">
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'cargoNameCn']}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请填写品名' }]}
                  >
                    {/* VARCHAR(128) —— maxLength 跟着列宽走。
                        ⚠️ 这里的 `required: true` 是**前端侧加严** —— 后端
                        `CargoRequest.cargoNameCn` 只有 `@Size(max = 128)`、**没有** `@NotBlank`,
                        V42 的 `t_order_cargo.cargo_name_cn` 是 `VARCHAR(128) DEFAULT NULL`(可空)。
                        两侧口径不一致, 已登记在 4.6 设计文档的「本轮不修的既有缺陷」一节。 */}
                    <Input placeholder="品名 (中)" style={{ width: 160 }} maxLength={128} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'pkgQty']}
                    // 件数 ≥ 0 (不是 > 0) —— 0 或不传表示「件数未知」(散货拼箱 LCL 常见),
                    // 与后端 @PositiveOrZero + DDL 的 NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 同口径
                  >
                    <InputNumber placeholder="件数" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'weightKg']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="重量 kg" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'volumeCbm']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="体积 m³" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'hsCode']}>
                    {/* VARCHAR(16) */}
                    <Input placeholder="HS 编码" style={{ width: 140 }} maxLength={16} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Button danger onClick={() => remove(field.name)}>
                    删除
                  </Button>
                </Space>
              ))}
              <Button onClick={() => add({})}>添加货物明细</Button>
            </Card>
          )}
        </Form.List>

        <Form.List name="serviceItems">
          {(fields, { add, remove }) => (
            <Card size="small" title="服务项 / 费用" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              {fields.map((field) => (
                <Space key={field.key} wrap align="start">
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'feeName']}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请填写费用名称' }]}
                  >
                    {/* VARCHAR(128) —— maxLength 跟着列宽走 */}
                    <Input placeholder="费用名称" style={{ width: 160 }} maxLength={128} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'feeType']}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请选择费用类型' }]}
                  >
                    {/* 自由 Input → Select: feeType 是 @NotBlank 且是受控词表,
                        手打一个词表外的值 = 提交后吃 400_106 */}
                    <Select
                      placeholder="费用类型"
                      style={{ width: 140 }}
                      options={FEE_TYPE_OPTIONS}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'calcBasis']}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请选择计费基准' }]}
                  >
                    {/* calcBasis 是 @NotBlank (V42: t_order_service_item.calc_basis VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL)
                        —— 不给这个字段 = undefined = JSON 里直接省略 = 后端必 400 */}
                    <Select
                      placeholder="计费基准"
                      style={{ width: 140 }}
                      options={CALC_BASIS_OPTIONS}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'feeCategory']}>
                    <Select
                      placeholder="费用大类"
                      style={{ width: 120 }}
                      allowClear
                      options={FEE_CATEGORY_OPTIONS}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'qty']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="数量" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'unitPrice']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="单价" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'amount']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="金额" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'currencyCode']}>
                    {/* CHAR(3) */}
                    <Input placeholder="币种" style={{ width: 80 }} maxLength={3} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'costAmount']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="我方成本" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Button danger onClick={() => remove(field.name)}>
                    删除
                  </Button>
                </Space>
              ))}
              <Button onClick={() => add({})}>添加服务项</Button>
            </Card>
          )}
        </Form.List>

        <Space>
          <Button type="primary" loading={submitting} onClick={onSubmit}>
            保存
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={() => nav('/orders')}>取消</Button>
        </Space>
      </Form>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 加路由**

改 `lumen-admin-web/src/App.tsx`：

1. 在 `const DispatchDashboard = lazy(...)` 那行**后面**加：

```tsx
const OrderList = lazy(() => import('@/pages/Order/List'));
const OrderDetail = lazy(() => import('@/pages/Order/Detail'));
const OrderForm = lazy(() => import('@/pages/Order/Form'));
```

2. 在 `<Route path="/dispatch/dashboard" ...>` 那条**后面**加三条路由：

```tsx
                        <Route
                          path="/orders"
                          element={<Suspense fallback={pageFallback}><OrderList /></Suspense>}
                        />
                        <Route
                          path="/orders/new"
                          element={<Suspense fallback={pageFallback}><OrderForm /></Suspense>}
                        />
                        <Route
                          path="/orders/:id"
                          element={<Suspense fallback={pageFallback}><OrderDetail /></Suspense>}
                        />
                        <Route
                          path="/orders/:id/edit"
                          element={<Suspense fallback={pageFallback}><OrderForm /></Suspense>}
                        />
```

> **路由顺序**: React Router v6 按路由表顺序匹配, 但静态段优先于动态段 ——
> `/orders/new` **不会**被 `/orders/:id` 抢走 (`new` 是静态段)。顺序仍按上表写,
> 读起来更清楚。

- [ ] **Step 7: 加菜单**

改 `lumen-admin-web/src/layouts/BasicLayout.tsx` —— 在 `menu.dispatch` 那一组**之前**
（也就是 RBAC 段之后、WMS 段之前）插入：

```tsx
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------
        // 货运委托单 (Iteration 4.6)
        //   Backend APIs at /api/v1/orders (lumen-order 新模块)。
        //   FE route prefix is /orders/。
        //   perm code 与 @PreAuthorize 逐字一致 —— 源在 OrderController + V42 seed。
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------
        {
          path: '/orders',
          name: t('menu.orders'),
          icon: <FileTextOutlined />,
          children: [
            { path: '/orders', name: t('menu.orders.list'), perm: 'order:view' },
          ],
        },
```

> `FileTextOutlined` 已经在 import 里了（财务组在用），**不要重复加 import**。

- [ ] **Step 8: 加 locale 文案**

改 `lumen-admin-web/src/locale/zh-CN.ts` —— 在 `'menu.dispatch': '配送调度',` **前面**加两行：

```ts
  'menu.orders': '货运委托',
  'menu.orders.list': '委托单列表',
```

改 `lumen-admin-web/src/locale/en-US.ts` —— 在对应的 `'menu.dispatch'` 行前面加：

```ts
  'menu.orders': 'Freight Orders',
  'menu.orders.list': 'Order List',
```

> **两个 locale 文件的 key 必须逐字一致** —— `TranslationKey` 类型是从 zh-CN 推导的,
> en-US 少一个 key 会类型报错。

- [ ] **Step 9: 类型检查 + 构建**

```bash
cd lumen-admin-web && ./node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit
```

预期：无输出（0 错误）。

```bash
cd lumen-admin-web && ./node_modules/.bin/vite build
```

预期：构建成功，产物在 `dist/`。

>
> **本 Task 的前端用例数（2026-11-12 实测，Task 11 回填）** ——
> admin-web **有**单元测试基建（`vitest.config.ts` + `src/setupTests.ts`），
> 本 Task 新增 **4 个测试文件 / 26 条用例**，全绿：
> `src/pages/Order/__tests__/List.test.tsx` 2 + `Detail.test.tsx` 5 + `Form.test.tsx` 6 +
> `src/services/order/__tests__/orderService.test.ts` 13 = **26**。
> 验证命令（**不要跑全量 vitest**，全量在机器高负载下会大量超时假红）：
> ```bash
> cd lumen-admin-web && ./node_modules/.bin/vitest run src/pages/Order/__tests__ src/services/order/__tests__
> ```
> 另有类型检查 + 构建两条验收：上面两条命令（`tsc --noEmit` 无输出 / `vite build` 成功）。

- [ ] **Step 10: 提交**

```bash
git add lumen-admin-web
git commit -F <utf8 提交信息文件>
```

提交信息：

```
feat(admin-web): 货运委托单 3 页 + 13 端点 service + 路由菜单

- services/order/orderService.ts 13 个端点, 路径用相对形式 /orders
  (request.ts 的 baseURL 已经是 /api/v1; 仓里既有的绝对路径写法会拼成
   /api/v1/api/v1/... —— 那是既有缺陷, 本轮不复制, 记入 Task 11 订正清单)
- OrderStatusTag 单独建组件, 不复用通用 StatusTag (CLOSED/DELIVERED 语义冲突)
```

```ts
/**
 * 4.6 — 货运委托单 TypeScript 类型。
 *
 * 对应后端:
 *   lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/dto/OrderView.java
 *   lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/dto/OrderDetailView.java
 *   lumen-parent/lumen-parent/lumen-customer/.../dto/PortalFreightOrderRequest.java
 *
 * 字段名用 camelCase, 与 Jackson 默认命名策略一致。
 */

/** 货物明细行。 */
export interface FreightOrderCargo {
  id: number;
  cargoNameCn?: string | null;
  cargoNameEn?: string | null;
  hsCode?: string | null;
  pkgType?: string | null;
  pkgQty?: number | null;
  unit?: string | null;
  weightKg?: number | null;
  netWeightKg?: number | null;
  volumeCbm?: number | null;
  cargoValue?: number | null;
  currencyCode?: string | null;
  brandName?: string | null;
  isDangerous?: number | null;
  unNo?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
}

/** 服务项行 —— 门户侧**不会**有 supplierId / costAmount (后端写入侧已置 null)。 */
export interface FreightOrderServiceItem {
  id: number;
  feeType?: string | null;
  feeName?: string | null;
  feeCategory?: string | null;
  calcBasis?: string | null;
  qty?: number | null;
  unitPrice?: number | null;
  amount?: number | null;
  currencyCode?: string | null;
  isMandatory?: number | null;
  remark?: string | null;
}

/** 列表行。 */
export interface FreightOrderRow {
  id: number;
  orderNo: string;
  customerId: number;
  businessType?: string | null;
  tradeDirection?: string | null;
  incoterms?: string | null;
  serviceType?: string | null;
  originAddress?: string | null;
  destAddress?: string | null;
  totalQty?: number | null;
  totalWeightKg?: number | null;
  totalVolumeCbm?: number | null;
  orderStatus: string;
  settlementCurrency?: string | null;
  etd?: string | null;
  eta?: string | null;
  createdAt?: string | null;
}

/** 详情。 */
export interface FreightOrderDetail extends FreightOrderRow {
  cargoReadyDate?: string | null;
  containerQty?: number | null;
  containerTypeCode?: string | null;
  marksAndNos?: string | null;
  cargoDescription?: string | null;
  isOversized?: number | null;
  temperatureRange?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
  cancelReason?: string | null;
  submittedAt?: string | null;
  confirmedAt?: string | null;
  deliveredAt?: string | null;
  cargo: FreightOrderCargo[];
  serviceItems: FreightOrderServiceItem[];
  allowedNextStates: string[];
}

/** 列表响应 —— 后端 `PageResult<T>`。 */
export interface FreightOrderPageData {
  records: FreightOrderRow[];
  total: number;
  pageNum: number;
  pageSize: number;
}

/** 列表查询参数。 */
export interface FreightOrderListQuery {
  current?: number;
  size?: number;
  status?: string;
}

/**
 * 下单请求 —— 对应后端 `PortalFreightOrderRequest`。
 *
 * **没有 customerId** —— 客户归属由后端从 JWT 强制注入, 客户端不能指定。
 */
export interface FreightOrderCreateRequest {
  businessType: string;
  tradeDirection: string;
  incoterms: string;
  serviceType: string;
  originAddress?: string | null;
  destAddress?: string | null;
  cargoReadyDate?: string | null;
  etd?: string | null;
  eta?: string | null;
  containerQty?: number | null;
  containerTypeCode?: string | null;
  marksAndNos?: string | null;
  cargoDescription?: string | null;
  isOversized?: boolean | null;
  temperatureRange?: string | null;
  settlementCurrency?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
  cargo: Array<Partial<FreightOrderCargo>>;
  serviceItems: Array<Partial<FreightOrderServiceItem>>;
}
```

> 实际路径是

- [ ] **Step 2: 建 service**

Create `lumen-customer-web/src/services/portalFreightOrderService.ts`：

```ts
import { http } from './http';
import type { ApiResponse } from '../types/portal';
import type {
  FreightOrderCreateRequest,
  FreightOrderDetail,
  FreightOrderListQuery,
  FreightOrderPageData,
  FreightOrderRow,
} from '../types/freightOrder';

/**
 * 4.6 — 门户端货运委托单 service。
 *
 * 后端: `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/.../controller/PortalFreightOrderController.java`
 * 路径前缀 `/api/customer/freight-order`, 5 个端点。
 *
 * http baseURL 是 "/api", 所以下面的路径**不带** /api 前缀。
 *
 * 与既有 `/customer/order`（城配单）**完全分开** —— 两个业务互不影响。
 */

const BASE = '/customer/freight-order';

export const portalFreightOrderService = {
  /** 1. 我的委托单列表 (后端按 JWT 里的客户过滤)。 */
  async list(query: FreightOrderListQuery): Promise<FreightOrderPageData> {
    const r = await http.get<ApiResponse<FreightOrderPageData>>(BASE, {
      params: query,
    });
    return r.data.data;
  },

  /** 2. 详情 (跨客户 → 404)。 */
  async detail(id: number): Promise<FreightOrderDetail> {
    const r = await http.get<ApiResponse<FreightOrderDetail>>(`${BASE}/${id}`);
    return r.data.data;
  },

  /** 3. 下单 (落 DRAFT)。 */
  async create(req: FreightOrderCreateRequest): Promise<FreightOrderRow> {
    const r = await http.post<ApiResponse<FreightOrderRow>>(BASE, req);
    return r.data.data;
  },

  /** 4. 确认 (PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED)。 */
  async confirm(id: number): Promise<FreightOrderDetail> {
    const r = await http.post<ApiResponse<FreightOrderDetail>>(
      `${BASE}/${id}/confirm`
    );
    return r.data.data;
  },

  /** 5. 取消 (PENDING_CONFIRM → CANCELLED), 必须带原因。 */
  async cancel(id: number, reason: string): Promise<FreightOrderDetail> {
    const r = await http.post<ApiResponse<FreightOrderDetail>>(
      `${BASE}/${id}/cancel`,
      { reason }
    );
    return r.data.data;
  },
};
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 建状态标签组件**

Create `lumen-customer-web/src/components/FreightOrderStatusTag.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 4.6 — 货运委托单状态标签。
 *
 * <p>不复用 `components/StatusTag.tsx` —— 那个组件的 label 来自 `zhCN.status`
 * (城配单的 8 个状态), 其中 `DELIVERED` 是「已签收」, 而委托单的 `DELIVERED`
 * 是「已交付」; `CLOSED` 在委托单里是「已归档」。
 * 语义不同, 复用会显示错标签。
 */

import React from 'react';
import { Tag } from 'antd';
import { zhCN } from '../locale/zh-CN';

interface Props {
  status?: string | null;
}

const COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  DRAFT: 'default',
  PENDING_CONFIRM: 'gold',
  CONFIRMED: 'blue',
  IN_OPERATION: 'processing',
  DELIVERED: 'cyan',
  SETTLED: 'purple',
  CLOSED: 'default',
  CANCELLED: 'default',
  VOID: 'red',
};

const FreightOrderStatusTag: React.FC<Props> = ({ status }) => {
  const key = (status ?? '').toUpperCase();
  const color = COLORS[key] ?? 'default';
  const label =
    zhCN.freightOrder.status[key as keyof typeof zhCN.freightOrder.status] ?? key;
  return <Tag color={color}>{label}</Tag>;
};

export default FreightOrderStatusTag;
```

Create `lumen-customer-web/src/pages/FreightOrder/List.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 4.6 — 门户端货运委托单列表。
 *
 * 与城配单列表 (`pages/Order/OrderList.tsx`) 同一套 ProTable 模式,
 * 但走的是**另一个**后端资源 (`/api/customer/freight-order`)。
 */

import React from 'react';
import type { ProColumns } from '@ant-design/pro-components';
import { ProTable } from '@ant-design/pro-components';
import { Button, Card } from 'antd';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { portalFreightOrderService } from '../../services/portalFreightOrderService';
import type { FreightOrderRow } from '../../types/freightOrder';
import FreightOrderStatusTag from '../../components/FreightOrderStatusTag';
import { zhCN } from '../../locale/zh-CN';

const STATUS_OPTIONS = Object.entries(zhCN.freightOrder.status).map(
  ([value, label]) => ({ value, label })
);

const FreightOrderList: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const columns: ProColumns<FreightOrderRow>[] = [
    {
      title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.orderNo,
      dataIndex: 'orderNo',
      width: 180,
      // 后端门户列表**只支持 status 过滤** (见 PortalFreightOrderController.list),
      // 没有 orderNo 检索 —— 这里关掉搜索框, 免得用户填了没反应。
      // 补 orderNo 检索列入 Task 11 文档订正清单。
      hideInSearch: true,
    },
    {
      title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.status,
      dataIndex: 'orderStatus',
      width: 110,
      render: (_, record) => <FreightOrderStatusTag status={record.orderStatus} />,
      valueType: 'select',
      valueEnum: Object.fromEntries(
        STATUS_OPTIONS.map((o) => [o.value, { text: o.label }])
      ),
    },
    {
      title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.businessType,
      dataIndex: 'businessType',
      width: 110,
    },
    {
      title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.origin,
      dataIndex: 'originAddress',
      ellipsis: true,
    },
    {
      title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.destination,
      dataIndex: 'destAddress',
      ellipsis: true,
    },
    {
      title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.weight,
      dataIndex: 'totalWeightKg',
      width: 110,
      hideInSearch: true,
      render: (_, r) => r.totalWeightKg ?? '—',
    },
    {
      title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.etd,
      dataIndex: 'etd',
      width: 110,
      hideInSearch: true,
    },
    {
      title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.createdAt,
      dataIndex: 'createdAt',
      width: 170,
      hideInSearch: true,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <Card bordered={false}>
      <ProTable<FreightOrderRow>
        headerTitle={zhCN.freightOrder.title}
        rowKey="id"
        columns={columns}
        search={{ labelWidth: 80, defaultCollapsed: false }}
        toolBarRender={() => [
          <Button
            key="create"
            type="primary"
            onClick={() => navigate('/freight-orders/new')}
          >
            {zhCN.freightOrder.create}
          </Button>,
        ]}
        options={{ reload: () => {}, density: true, setting: false }}
        pagination={{ pageSize: 10, showSizeChanger: true }}
        request={async (params) => {
          const { current, pageSize, orderStatus } = params;
          try {
            const r = await portalFreightOrderService.list({
              status: typeof orderStatus === 'string' ? orderStatus : undefined,
              current: current ?? 1,
              size: pageSize ?? 10,
            });
            return { data: r.records, total: r.total, success: true };
          } catch {
            return { data: [], total: 0, success: false };
          }
        }}
        onRow={(record) => ({
          onClick: () => navigate(`/freight-orders/${record.id}`),
          style: { cursor: 'pointer' },
        })}
      />
    </Card>
  );
};

export default FreightOrderList;
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 详情页**

Create `lumen-customer-web/src/pages/FreightOrder/Detail.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 4.6 — 门户端货运委托单详情。
 *
 * 客户能做的动作只有两个: 确认 (PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED) 与
 * 取消 (PENDING_CONFIRM → CANCELLED)。按钮显隐由后端返回的
 * `allowedNextStates` 决定 —— 前端不自己判断状态机。
 */

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Button,
  Card,
  Descriptions,
  Input,
  Modal,
  Spin,
  Table,
  message,
} from 'antd';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { portalFreightOrderService } from '../../services/portalFreightOrderService';
import type {
  FreightOrderCargo,
  FreightOrderDetail,
  FreightOrderServiceItem,
} from '../../types/freightOrder';
import FreightOrderStatusTag from '../../components/FreightOrderStatusTag';
import { zhCN } from '../../locale/zh-CN';

const { TextArea } = Input;

/** 原因最少 5 个字 —— 与后端 `OrderConstants.REASON_MIN_LENGTH` 一致。 */
const REASON_MIN = 5;
/** 原因最多 255 个字 —— 与后端 `OrderConstants.REASON_MAX_LENGTH` 一致
 *  (V42 的 cancel_reason / close_reason / void_reason 三列都是 VARCHAR(255);
 *   超长后端抛 REASON_TOO_LONG 400_107, 前端先挡住, 免得用户填了半天气泡)。 */
const REASON_MAX = 255;

const FreightOrderDetailPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const orderId = Number(id);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<FreightOrderDetail | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [cancelOpen, setCancelOpen] = useState(false);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const [acting, setActing] = useState(false);

  const load = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setDetail(await portalFreightOrderService.detail(orderId));
    } catch {
      message.error('委托单加载失败');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
  }, [orderId]);

  const onConfirm = async () => {
    setActing(true);
    try {
      await portalFreightOrderService.confirm(orderId);
      message.success('委托单已确认');
      await load();
    } catch {
      // http.ts 不弹通知, 这里给一条兜底提示。
      message.error('确认失败, 请稍后重试');
    } finally {
      setActing(false);
    }
  };

  const onCancel = async () => {
    if (reason.trim().length < REASON_MIN) {
      message.error(`取消原因至少 ${REASON_MIN} 个字`);
      return;
    }
    if (reason.trim().length > REASON_MAX) {
      message.error(`取消原因最多 ${REASON_MAX} 个字`);
      return;
    }
    setActing(true);
    try {
      await portalFreightOrderService.cancel(orderId, reason.trim());
      message.success('委托单已取消');
      setCancelOpen(false);
      setReason('');
      await load();
    } catch {
      message.error('取消失败, 请稍后重试');
    } finally {
      setActing(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <Spin />;
  }
  if (!detail) {
    return <Card>委托单不存在</Card>;
  }

  const canConfirm = detail.allowedNextStates.includes('CONFIRMED');
  const canCancel = detail.allowedNextStates.includes('CANCELLED');

  return (
    <Card
      title={
        <span>
          {detail.orderNo} <FreightOrderStatusTag status={detail.orderStatus} />
        </span>
      }
      extra={
        <span>
          {canConfirm && (
            <Button type="primary" loading={acting} onClick={onConfirm}>
              {zhCN.freightOrder.confirm}
            </Button>
          )}
          {canCancel && (
            <Button
              danger
              style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}
              onClick={() => setCancelOpen(true)}
            >
              {zhCN.freightOrder.cancel}
            </Button>
          )}
          <Button style={{ marginLeft: 8 }} onClick={() => navigate('/freight-orders')}>
            {zhCN.freightOrder.backToList}
          </Button>
        </span>
      }
    >
      <Descriptions bordered column={2} size="small">
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.businessType}>
          {detail.businessType ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.tradeDirection}>
          {detail.tradeDirection ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.incoterms}>
          {detail.incoterms ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.serviceType}>
          {detail.serviceType ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.origin}>
          {detail.originAddress ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.destination}>
          {detail.destAddress ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.etd}>
          {detail.etd ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.eta}>
          {detail.eta ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.weight}>
          {detail.totalWeightKg ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.volume}>
          {detail.totalVolumeCbm ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.container}>
          {detail.containerQty ?? '—'} {detail.containerTypeCode ?? ''}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.currency}>
          {detail.settlementCurrency ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.marks} span={2}>
          {detail.marksAndNos ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.description} span={2}>
          {detail.cargoDescription ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
      </Descriptions>

      <Card size="small" title={zhCN.freightOrder.cargoTitle} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
        <Table<FreightOrderCargo>
          rowKey="id"
          size="small"
          pagination={false}
          dataSource={detail.cargo}
          columns={[
            { title: '品名', dataIndex: 'cargoNameCn' },
            { title: 'HS 编码', dataIndex: 'hsCode' },
            { title: '件数', dataIndex: 'pkgQty' },
            { title: '重量 (kg)', dataIndex: 'weightKg' },
            { title: '体积 (m³)', dataIndex: 'volumeCbm' },
          ]}
        />
      </Card>

      <Card size="small" title={zhCN.freightOrder.serviceTitle} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
        <Table<FreightOrderServiceItem>
          rowKey="id"
          size="small"
          pagination={false}
          dataSource={detail.serviceItems}
          columns={[
            { title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.feeType, dataIndex: 'feeType' },
            { title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.feeName, dataIndex: 'feeName' },
            { title: zhCN.freightOrder.column.calcBasis, dataIndex: 'calcBasis' },
            { title: '数量', dataIndex: 'qty' },
            { title: '单价', dataIndex: 'unitPrice' },
            { title: '金额', dataIndex: 'amount' },
            { title: '币种', dataIndex: 'currencyCode' },
          ]}
        />
      </Card>

      <Modal
        open={cancelOpen}
        title={zhCN.freightOrder.cancelTitle}
        onCancel={() => setCancelOpen(false)}
        onOk={onCancel}
        confirmLoading={acting}
        okText="确认"
        cancelText="再想想"
      >
        <TextArea
          rows={4}
          value={reason}
          maxLength={REASON_MAX}
          onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={zhCN.freightOrder.cancelReasonPlaceholder}
        />
      </Modal>
    </Card>
  );
};

export default FreightOrderDetailPage;
```

Create `lumen-customer-web/src/pages/FreightOrder/Create.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 4.6 — 门户端委托下单页。
 *
 * 提交后单子落在 DRAFT —— 客户还需要在详情页点「确认」才会进 PENDING_CONFIRM
 * → CONFIRMED。**客户不能自己提交** —— submit 是管理端的动作 (order:submit)。
 */

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Button,
  Card,
  DatePicker,
  Form,
  Input,
  InputNumber,
  Select,
  Space,
  message,
} from 'antd';
import dayjs, { Dayjs } from 'dayjs';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { portalFreightOrderService } from '../../services/portalFreightOrderService';
import type { FreightOrderCreateRequest } from '../../types/freightOrder';
import { zhCN } from '../../locale/zh-CN';

const { TextArea } = Input;

interface FormValues {
  businessType: string;
  tradeDirection: string;
  incoterms: string;
  serviceType: string;
  originAddress?: string;
  destAddress?: string;
  cargoReadyDate?: Dayjs;
  etd?: Dayjs;
  eta?: Dayjs;
  containerQty?: number;
  containerTypeCode?: string;
  marksAndNos?: string;
  cargoDescription?: string;
  settlementCurrency?: string;
  remark?: string;
  cargo?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  serviceItems?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
}

const FreightOrderCreate: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [form] = Form.useForm<FormValues>();
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  const onSubmit = async () => {
    let values: FormValues;
    try {
      values = await form.validateFields();
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    const payload: FreightOrderCreateRequest = {
      businessType: values.businessType,
      tradeDirection: values.tradeDirection,
      incoterms: values.incoterms,
      serviceType: values.serviceType,
      originAddress: values.originAddress ?? null,
      destAddress: values.destAddress ?? null,
      cargoReadyDate: values.cargoReadyDate
        ? values.cargoReadyDate.format('YYYY-MM-DD')
        : null,
      etd: values.etd ? values.etd.format('YYYY-MM-DD') : null,
      eta: values.eta ? values.eta.format('YYYY-MM-DD') : null,
      containerQty: values.containerQty ?? null,
      containerTypeCode: values.containerTypeCode ?? null,
      marksAndNos: values.marksAndNos ?? null,
      cargoDescription: values.cargoDescription ?? null,
      settlementCurrency: values.settlementCurrency ?? null,
      remark: values.remark ?? null,
      cargo: (values.cargo ?? []).map((c) => {
        const { id: _id, ...rest } = c as Record<string, unknown> & { id?: number };
        void _id;
        return rest;
      }),
      serviceItems: (values.serviceItems ?? []).map((s) => {
        const { id: _id, ...rest } = s as Record<string, unknown> & { id?: number };
        void _id;
        return rest;
      }),
    };
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const created = await portalFreightOrderService.create(payload);
      message.success('委托单已提交');
      navigate(`/freight-orders/${created.id}`);
    } catch {
      message.error('下单失败, 请稍后重试');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Card title={zhCN.freightOrder.createTitle}>
      <Form<FormValues> form={form} layout="vertical">
        <Form.Item
          name="businessType"
          label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.businessType}
          rules={[{ required: true }]}
        >
          <Select
            options={[
              // = OrderConstants.BUSINESS_TYPES (8 个, 全集) —— 实施期按全集实现
              { value: 'OCEAN_FCL', label: '海运整箱 (OCEAN_FCL)' },
              { value: 'OCEAN_LCL', label: '海运拼箱 (OCEAN_LCL)' },
              { value: 'AIR', label: '空运 (AIR)' },
              { value: 'RAIL', label: '铁路 (RAIL)' },
              { value: 'TRUCK', label: '陆运 (TRUCK)' },
              { value: 'EXPRESS', label: '快递 (EXPRESS)' },
              { value: 'MULTIMODAL', label: '多式联运 (MULTIMODAL)' },
              { value: 'WAREHOUSE', label: '仓储 (WAREHOUSE)' },
            ]}
          />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item
          name="tradeDirection"
          label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.tradeDirection}
          rules={[{ required: true }]}
        >
          <Select
            options={[
              // = OrderConstants.TRADE_DIRECTIONS (4 个, 全集) —— 实施期按全集实现
              { value: 'EXPORT', label: '出口 (EXPORT)' },
              { value: 'IMPORT', label: '进口 (IMPORT)' },
              { value: 'TRANSIT', label: '过境 (TRANSIT)' },
              { value: 'TRANSIT_OVERLAND', label: '过境陆运 (TRANSIT_OVERLAND)' },
            ]}
          />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item
          name="incoterms"
          label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.incoterms}
          rules={[{ required: true }]}
        >
          <Select
            options={[
              // = OrderConstants.INCOTERMS (10 个, 全集) —— 实施期按全集实现
              { value: 'EXW', label: '工厂交货 (EXW)' },
              { value: 'FCA', label: '货交承运人 (FCA)' },
              { value: 'FOB', label: '装运港船上交货 (FOB)' },
              { value: 'CFR', label: '成本加运费 (CFR)' },
              { value: 'CIF', label: '成本、保险费加运费 (CIF)' },
              { value: 'CPT', label: '运费付至 (CPT)' },
              { value: 'CIP', label: '运费和保险费付至 (CIP)' },
              { value: 'DAP', label: '目的地交货 (DAP)' },
              { value: 'DPU', label: '目的地卸货交货 (DPU)' },
              { value: 'DDP', label: '完税后交货 (DDP)' },
            ]}
          />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item
          name="serviceType"
          label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.serviceType}
          rules={[{ required: true }]}
        >
          <Select
            options={[
              // = OrderConstants.SERVICE_TYPES —— DDP 是贸易条款 (Incoterm), 不是服务类型,
              // 不在这里 (后端 service 校验受控词表, 选错必然 400_106)
              { value: 'D2D', label: '门到门 (D2D)' },
              { value: 'P2P', label: '港到港 (P2P)' },
              { value: 'D2P', label: '门到港 (D2P)' },
              { value: 'P2D', label: '港到门 (P2D)' },
            ]}
          />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="originAddress" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.origin}>
          <Input maxLength={255} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="destAddress" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.destination}>
          <Input maxLength={255} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="cargoReadyDate" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.cargoReadyDate}>
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="etd" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.etd}>
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="eta" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.eta}>
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="containerQty" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.containerQty}>
          <InputNumber min={0} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="containerTypeCode" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.containerType}>
          <Input maxLength={16} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="marksAndNos" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.marks}>
          <Input maxLength={500} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="cargoDescription" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.description}>
          <TextArea rows={2} maxLength={500} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="settlementCurrency" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.currency}>
          {/* CHAR(3) —— maxLength 跟着列宽走 */}
          <Input maxLength={3} placeholder="CNY" />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="remark" label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.remark}>
          <TextArea rows={3} maxLength={1000} />
        </Form.Item>

        <Form.List name="cargo">
          {(fields, { add, remove }) => (
            <Card size="small" title={zhCN.freightOrder.cargoTitle} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              {fields.map((field) => (
                <Space key={field.key} wrap align="start">
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'cargoNameCn']}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请填写品名' }]}
                  >
                    {/* VARCHAR(128) —— maxLength 跟着列宽走。
                        ⚠️ 这里的 `required: true` 是**门户侧加严** —— 后端
                        `CargoRequest.cargoNameCn` 只有 `@Size(max = 128)`、**没有** `@NotBlank`,
                        V42 的 `t_order_cargo.cargo_name_cn` 是 `VARCHAR(128) DEFAULT NULL`(可空)。
                        两侧口径不一致, 已登记在 4.6 设计文档的「本轮不修的既有缺陷」一节。 */}
                    <Input placeholder="品名" style={{ width: 160 }} maxLength={128} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'pkgQty']}
                    // 件数 ≥ 0 (不是 > 0) —— 0 或不传表示「件数未知」(散货拼箱 LCL 常见),
                    // 与后端 @PositiveOrZero + DDL 的 NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 同口径
                  >
                    <InputNumber placeholder="件数" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'weightKg']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="重量 kg" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'volumeCbm']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="体积 m³" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'hsCode']}>
                    {/* VARCHAR(16) */}
                    <Input placeholder="HS 编码" style={{ width: 140 }} maxLength={16} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Button danger onClick={() => remove(field.name)}>
                    删除
                  </Button>
                </Space>
              ))}
              <Button onClick={() => add({})}>{zhCN.freightOrder.addCargo}</Button>
            </Card>
          )}
        </Form.List>

        <Form.List name="serviceItems">
          {(fields, { add, remove }) => (
            <Card size="small" title={zhCN.freightOrder.serviceTitle} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              {fields.map((field) => (
                <Space key={field.key} wrap align="start">
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'feeName']}
                    label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.feeName}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请填写费用名称' }]}
                  >
                    {/* VARCHAR(128) —— maxLength 跟着列宽走 */}
                    <Input placeholder="费用名称" style={{ width: 160 }} maxLength={128} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'feeType']}
                    label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.feeType}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请选择费用类型' }]}
                  >
                    {/* feeType 是 @NotBlank + 受控词表 —— 自由输入必被后端 400_106 打回 */}
                    <Select
                      placeholder="费用类型"
                      style={{ width: 140 }}
                      options={[
                        { value: 'OCEAN_FREIGHT', label: '海运费' },
                        { value: 'AIR_FREIGHT', label: '空运费' },
                        { value: 'BAF', label: '燃油附加费' },
                        { value: 'THC', label: '港口操作费' },
                        { value: 'DOC_FEE', label: '单证费' },
                        { value: 'CUSTOMS', label: '清关费' },
                        { value: 'DELIVERY', label: '派送费' },
                        { value: 'OTHER', label: '其他' },
                      ]}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'calcBasis']}
                    label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.calcBasis}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请选择计费基准' }]}
                  >
                    {/* calcBasis 是 @NotBlank (V42: calc_basis VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL)
                        —— 不给这个字段 = undefined = JSON 里直接省略 = 后端必 400 */}
                    <Select
                      placeholder="计费基准"
                      style={{ width: 140 }}
                      options={[
                        { value: 'PER_SHIPMENT', label: '按票' },
                        { value: 'PER_CONTAINER', label: '按箱' },
                        { value: 'PER_BOX', label: '按件' },
                        { value: 'PER_KG', label: '按公斤' },
                        { value: 'PER_CBM', label: '按方' },
                        { value: 'PER_CARGO', label: '按货物' },
                      ]}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'feeCategory']}
                    label={zhCN.freightOrder.column.feeCategory}
                  >
                    <Select
                      placeholder="费用大类"
                      style={{ width: 120 }}
                      allowClear
                      options={[
                        { value: 'FREIGHT', label: '运费' },
                        { value: 'SURCHARGE', label: '附加费' },
                        { value: 'CUSTOMS', label: '清关' },
                        { value: 'DELIVERY', label: '派送' },
                        { value: 'WAREHOUSE', label: '仓储' },
                        { value: 'OTHER', label: '其他' },
                      ]}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'qty']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="数量" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'unitPrice']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="单价" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'amount']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="金额" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'currencyCode']}>
                    {/* CHAR(3) */}
                    <Input placeholder="币种" style={{ width: 80 }} maxLength={3} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Button danger onClick={() => remove(field.name)}>
                    删除
                  </Button>
                </Space>
              ))}
              <Button onClick={() => add({})}>{zhCN.freightOrder.addServiceItem}</Button>
            </Card>
          )}
        </Form.List>

        <Space>
          <Button type="primary" loading={submitting} onClick={onSubmit}>
            {zhCN.common.submit}
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={() => navigate('/freight-orders')}>
            {zhCN.common.cancel}
          </Button>
        </Space>
      </Form>
    </Card>
  );
};

export default FreightOrderCreate;
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 加路由**

改 `lumen-customer-web/src/App.tsx`：

1. 在 `import OrderDetail from './pages/Order/OrderDetail';` **后面**加三行 import：

```tsx
import FreightOrderList from './pages/FreightOrder/List';
import FreightOrderDetail from './pages/FreightOrder/Detail';
import FreightOrderCreate from './pages/FreightOrder/Create';
```

2. 在 `<Route path="/orders/:doId" element={...} />` **后面**加三条路由：

```tsx
        <Route path="/freight-orders" element={<RequireAuth><FreightOrderList /></RequireAuth>} />
        <Route path="/freight-orders/new" element={<RequireAuth><FreightOrderCreate /></RequireAuth>} />
        <Route path="/freight-orders/:id" element={<RequireAuth><FreightOrderDetail /></RequireAuth>} />
```

> **`/freight-orders/new` 与 `/freight-orders/:id` 的顺序**: React Router v6 按
> 静态段优先匹配, `new` 是静态段, 不会被 `:id` 抢走。

- [ ] **Step 8: 加菜单**

改 `lumen-customer-web/src/layouts/PortalLayout.tsx` 的 `MENU` 数组 ——
在 `{ key: 'orders-new', ... }` **后面**加两条：

```tsx
  { key: 'freight-orders', path: '/freight-orders', icon: <FileTextOutlined />, label: zhCN.menu.freightOrders },
  { key: 'freight-orders-new', path: '/freight-orders/new', icon: <PlusSquareOutlined />, label: zhCN.menu.createFreightOrder },
```

> `FileTextOutlined` / `PlusSquareOutlined` 已经在 import 里了（既有的 orders / orders-new 两条在用），
> **不要重复加 import**。

> **菜单选中态不会串**：`selectMenuKey` 取**最长**前缀匹配 ——
> `/freight-orders/new` 命中 `freight-orders-new`（15 字符）而不是
> `freight-orders`（14 字符）；而 `/freight-orders` **不会**命中 `orders`
> （`'/freight-orders'.startsWith('/orders/')` 是 false）。

> ```bash

- [ ] **Step 11: 提交**

```bash
git add lumen-customer-web
git commit -F <utf8 提交信息文件>
```

提交信息：

```
feat(customer-web): 门户端委托单 3 页 + service + 类型

- types/freightOrder.ts + services/portalFreightOrderService.ts (5 端点)
- 路由用 /freight-orders 前缀, 不与既有城配单 /orders 撞车
- PortalLayout 菜单加「我的委托单」与「委托下单」两条
- FreightOrderStatusTag 独立组件 (城配 DELIVERED=已签收, 委托单=已交付)
- locale 用嵌套对象结构 (与 admin-web 的扁平 key 不同)
- 下单页的服务项补 calcBasis (必填 Select) / feeType (必填 Select) / feeCategory (可选 Select),
  与后端 ServiceItemRequest 的 @NotBlank + OrderConstants 的受控词表同口径
- serviceType 下拉去掉 DDP (那是 Incoterm, 不在 SERVICE_TYPES 里) 补齐 P2P/P2D
- 输入框 maxLength 跟着 DDL 列宽走: 结算币种 8→3 (CHAR(3)), 取消原因 500→255 (VARCHAR(255)),
  品名 128 / HS 编码 16 / 费用名称 128 / 币种 3
- locale 的 column 段补「费用类型 / 费用名称 / 费用大类 / 计费基准」四条 (zh-CN 与 en-US 逐字对齐)

Co-Authored-By: Claude Code <noreply@anthropic.com>
```

```markdown
>     `fx_locked` → **`is_locked`**、`locked_at` → **`lock_at`**；
>     V42 另加 `exchange_rate_type` / `lock_by` —— **只落字段，本轮无锁汇率逻辑**，
>     故可空且不参与计算；

> - **已预留为空列、本轮无上游来源**：`inquiry_id` / `quotation_id`。

>   - `currency_code`：本节写 `varchar(8)`，V42 是 **`CHAR(3)`**（ISO 4217 三位币种码，够用）；
>   - `remark`：本节写 `text`；V42 里 **`t_order` 是 `VARCHAR(1000)`**，
>     **`t_order_cargo` / `t_order_service_item` 是 `VARCHAR(500)`**（两张明细表不是 1000）。
> - **本节 §4.1 / §4.3 表格里其它字段 V42 都已落**（别误列为未落）：
>   `customer_contract_id` / `sales_id` / `operator_id` / `origin_port_id` /
>   `dest_port_id` / `pickup_warehouse_id` / `delivery_warehouse_id` /

>   V42 还额外加了 `fee_category` / `source` / `is_mandatory`
>   （相对 docs/02 §4.3 是额外的；这三个在 `05v3` §5.2 里本来就有）。
> - **编号口径**：委托单号统一为 **`ORD-yyyyMMdd-6位流水`**（例 `ORD-20261110-000001`），
>   由 `sys_number_rule` 的 `code='ORDER'`（V4 已 seed：`prefix='ORD'` / `yyyyMMdd` / 6 位 / DAY）产出；
>   唯一键是 `uk_order_no (tenant_id, order_no)` —— **不带 `deleted`**：
>   委托单号是合同级单据，发出即永久占用，软删后不得复用（否则同一单号会指向两笔不同的业务）。
> - **客户表口径**：`t_customer` **≡ 仓库里的 `wms_customer`**（同一张表的两个历史叫法）——
>   本仓所有代码只认 `wms_customer`。
> - **编号规则表口径**：`t_code_rule` **作废** —— 实际实现是
>   `sys_number_rule` + `sys_number_sequence`。
> - **API 前缀**：管理端 **`/api/v1/orders`**（模块 `lumen-order`），
>   门户端 **`/api/customer/freight-order`**（模块 `lumen-customer`）。
>   两者与城配单的 `/api/v1/dispatch/orders`、门户城配的 `/api/customer/order`
>   **互不冲突** —— 四条路径各有各的业务。
```
